================
File: account-service/mvnw
================
  1: #!/bin/sh
  2: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  3: # Licensed to the Apache Software Foundation (ASF) under one
  4: # or more contributor license agreements.  See the NOTICE file
  5: # distributed with this work for additional information
  6: # regarding copyright ownership.  The ASF licenses this file
  7: # to you under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the
  8: # "License"); you may not use this file except in compliance
  9: # with the License.  You may obtain a copy of the License at
 10: #
 11: #    http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
 12: #
 13: # Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing,
 14: # software distributed under the License is distributed on an
 15: # "AS IS" BASIS, WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY
 16: # KIND, either express or implied.  See the License for the
 17: # specific language governing permissions and limitations
 18: # under the License.
 19: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 20: 
 21: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 22: # Maven2 Start Up Batch script
 23: #
 24: # Required ENV vars:
 25: # ------------------
 26: #   JAVA_HOME - location of a JDK home dir
 27: #
 28: # Optional ENV vars
 29: # -----------------
 30: #   M2_HOME - location of maven2's installed home dir
 31: #   MAVEN_OPTS - parameters passed to the Java VM when running Maven
 32: #     e.g. to debug Maven itself, use
 33: #       set MAVEN_OPTS=-Xdebug -Xrunjdwp:transport=dt_socket,server=y,suspend=y,address=8000
 34: #   MAVEN_SKIP_RC - flag to disable loading of mavenrc files
 35: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 36: 
 37: if [ -z "$MAVEN_SKIP_RC" ] ; then
 38: 
 39:   if [ -f /etc/mavenrc ] ; then
 40:     . /etc/mavenrc
 41:   fi
 42: 
 43:   if [ -f "$HOME/.mavenrc" ] ; then
 44:     . "$HOME/.mavenrc"
 45:   fi
 46: 
 47: fi
 48: 
 49: # OS specific support.  $var _must_ be set to either true or false.
 50: cygwin=false;
 51: darwin=false;
 52: mingw=false
 53: case "`uname`" in
 54:   CYGWIN*) cygwin=true ;;
 55:   MINGW*) mingw=true;;
 56:   Darwin*) darwin=true
 57:     # Use /usr/libexec/java_home if available, otherwise fall back to /Library/Java/Home
 58:     # See https://developer.apple.com/library/mac/qa/qa1170/_index.html
 59:     if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ]; then
 60:       if [ -x "/usr/libexec/java_home" ]; then
 61:         export JAVA_HOME="`/usr/libexec/java_home`"
 62:       else
 63:         export JAVA_HOME="/Library/Java/Home"
 64:       fi
 65:     fi
 66:     ;;
 67: esac
 68: 
 69: if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ] ; then
 70:   if [ -r /etc/gentoo-release ] ; then
 71:     JAVA_HOME=`java-config --jre-home`
 72:   fi
 73: fi
 74: 
 75: if [ -z "$M2_HOME" ] ; then
 76:   ## resolve links - $0 may be a link to maven's home
 77:   PRG="$0"
 78: 
 79:   # need this for relative symlinks
 80:   while [ -h "$PRG" ] ; do
 81:     ls=`ls -ld "$PRG"`
 82:     link=`expr "$ls" : '.*-> \(.*\)$'`
 83:     if expr "$link" : '/.*' > /dev/null; then
 84:       PRG="$link"
 85:     else
 86:       PRG="`dirname "$PRG"`/$link"
 87:     fi
 88:   done
 89: 
 90:   saveddir=`pwd`
 91: 
 92:   M2_HOME=`dirname "$PRG"`/..
 93: 
 94:   # make it fully qualified
 95:   M2_HOME=`cd "$M2_HOME" && pwd`
 96: 
 97:   cd "$saveddir"
 98:   # echo Using m2 at $M2_HOME
 99: fi
100: 
101: # For Cygwin, ensure paths are in UNIX format before anything is touched
102: if $cygwin ; then
103:   [ -n "$M2_HOME" ] &&
104:     M2_HOME=`cygpath --unix "$M2_HOME"`
105:   [ -n "$JAVA_HOME" ] &&
106:     JAVA_HOME=`cygpath --unix "$JAVA_HOME"`
107:   [ -n "$CLASSPATH" ] &&
108:     CLASSPATH=`cygpath --path --unix "$CLASSPATH"`
109: fi
110: 
111: # For Migwn, ensure paths are in UNIX format before anything is touched
112: if $mingw ; then
113:   [ -n "$M2_HOME" ] &&
114:     M2_HOME="`(cd "$M2_HOME"; pwd)`"
115:   [ -n "$JAVA_HOME" ] &&
116:     JAVA_HOME="`(cd "$JAVA_HOME"; pwd)`"
117:   # TODO classpath?
118: fi
119: 
120: if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ]; then
121:   javaExecutable="`which javac`"
122:   if [ -n "$javaExecutable" ] && ! [ "`expr \"$javaExecutable\" : '\([^ ]*\)'`" = "no" ]; then
123:     # readlink(1) is not available as standard on Solaris 10.
124:     readLink=`which readlink`
125:     if [ ! `expr "$readLink" : '\([^ ]*\)'` = "no" ]; then
126:       if $darwin ; then
127:         javaHome="`dirname \"$javaExecutable\"`"
128:         javaExecutable="`cd \"$javaHome\" && pwd -P`/javac"
129:       else
130:         javaExecutable="`readlink -f \"$javaExecutable\"`"
131:       fi
132:       javaHome="`dirname \"$javaExecutable\"`"
133:       javaHome=`expr "$javaHome" : '\(.*\)/bin'`
134:       JAVA_HOME="$javaHome"
135:       export JAVA_HOME
136:     fi
137:   fi
138: fi
139: 
140: if [ -z "$JAVACMD" ] ; then
141:   if [ -n "$JAVA_HOME"  ] ; then
142:     if [ -x "$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java" ] ; then
143:       # IBM's JDK on AIX uses strange locations for the executables
144:       JAVACMD="$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java"
145:     else
146:       JAVACMD="$JAVA_HOME/bin/java"
147:     fi
148:   else
149:     JAVACMD="`which java`"
150:   fi
151: fi
152: 
153: if [ ! -x "$JAVACMD" ] ; then
154:   echo "Error: JAVA_HOME is not defined correctly." >&2
155:   echo "  We cannot execute $JAVACMD" >&2
156:   exit 1
157: fi
158: 
159: if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ] ; then
160:   echo "Warning: JAVA_HOME environment variable is not set."
161: fi
162: 
163: CLASSWORLDS_LAUNCHER=org.codehaus.plexus.classworlds.launcher.Launcher
164: 
165: # traverses directory structure from process work directory to filesystem root
166: # first directory with .mvn subdirectory is considered project base directory
167: find_maven_basedir() {
168: 
169:   if [ -z "$1" ]
170:   then
171:     echo "Path not specified to find_maven_basedir"
172:     return 1
173:   fi
174: 
175:   basedir="$1"
176:   wdir="$1"
177:   while [ "$wdir" != '/' ] ; do
178:     if [ -d "$wdir"/.mvn ] ; then
179:       basedir=$wdir
180:       break
181:     fi
182:     # workaround for JBEAP-8937 (on Solaris 10/Sparc)
183:     if [ -d "${wdir}" ]; then
184:       wdir=`cd "$wdir/.."; pwd`
185:     fi
186:     # end of workaround
187:   done
188:   echo "${basedir}"
189: }
190: 
191: # concatenates all lines of a file
192: concat_lines() {
193:   if [ -f "$1" ]; then
194:     echo "$(tr -s '\n' ' ' < "$1")"
195:   fi
196: }
197: 
198: BASE_DIR=`find_maven_basedir "$(pwd)"`
199: if [ -z "$BASE_DIR" ]; then
200:   exit 1;
201: fi
202: 
203: export MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=${MAVEN_BASEDIR:-"$BASE_DIR"}
204: echo $MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR
205: MAVEN_OPTS="$(concat_lines "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR/.mvn/jvm.config") $MAVEN_OPTS"
206: 
207: # For Cygwin, switch paths to Windows format before running java
208: if $cygwin; then
209:   [ -n "$M2_HOME" ] &&
210:     M2_HOME=`cygpath --path --windows "$M2_HOME"`
211:   [ -n "$JAVA_HOME" ] &&
212:     JAVA_HOME=`cygpath --path --windows "$JAVA_HOME"`
213:   [ -n "$CLASSPATH" ] &&
214:     CLASSPATH=`cygpath --path --windows "$CLASSPATH"`
215:   [ -n "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR" ] &&
216:     MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=`cygpath --path --windows "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR"`
217: fi
218: 
219: WRAPPER_LAUNCHER=org.apache.maven.wrapper.MavenWrapperMain
220: 
221: exec "$JAVACMD" \
222:   $MAVEN_OPTS \
223:   -classpath "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR/.mvn/wrapper/maven-wrapper.jar" \
224:   "-Dmaven.home=${M2_HOME}" "-Dmaven.multiModuleProjectDirectory=${MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR}" \
225:   ${WRAPPER_LAUNCHER} $MAVEN_CONFIG "$@"

  1: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  2: @REM Licensed to the Apache Software Foundation (ASF) under one
  3: @REM or more contributor license agreements.  See the NOTICE file
  4: @REM distributed with this work for additional information
  5: @REM regarding copyright ownership.  The ASF licenses this file
  6: @REM to you under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the
  7: @REM "License"); you may not use this file except in compliance
  8: @REM with the License.  You may obtain a copy of the License at
  9: @REM
 10: @REM    http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
 11: @REM
 12: @REM Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing,
 13: @REM software distributed under the License is distributed on an
 14: @REM "AS IS" BASIS, WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY
 15: @REM KIND, either express or implied.  See the License for the
 16: @REM specific language governing permissions and limitations
 17: @REM under the License.
 18: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 19: 
 20: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 21: @REM Maven2 Start Up Batch script
 22: @REM
 23: @REM Required ENV vars:
 24: @REM JAVA_HOME - location of a JDK home dir
 25: @REM
 26: @REM Optional ENV vars
 27: @REM M2_HOME - location of maven2's installed home dir
 28: @REM MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO - set to 'on' to enable the echoing of the batch commands
 29: @REM MAVEN_BATCH_PAUSE - set to 'on' to wait for a key stroke before ending
 30: @REM MAVEN_OPTS - parameters passed to the Java VM when running Maven
 31: @REM     e.g. to debug Maven itself, use
 32: @REM set MAVEN_OPTS=-Xdebug -Xrunjdwp:transport=dt_socket,server=y,suspend=y,address=8000
 33: @REM MAVEN_SKIP_RC - flag to disable loading of mavenrc files
 34: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 35: 
 36: @REM Begin all REM lines with '@' in case MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO is 'on'
 37: @echo off
 38: @REM enable echoing my setting MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO to 'on'
 39: @if "%MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO%" == "on"  echo %MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO%
 40: 
 41: @REM set %HOME% to equivalent of $HOME
 42: if "%HOME%" == "" (set "HOME=%HOMEDRIVE%%HOMEPATH%")
 43: 
 44: @REM Execute a user defined script before this one
 45: if not "%MAVEN_SKIP_RC%" == "" goto skipRcPre
 46: @REM check for pre script, once with legacy .bat ending and once with .cmd ending
 47: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.bat" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.bat"
 48: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.cmd" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.cmd"
 49: :skipRcPre
 50: 
 51: @setlocal
 52: 
 53: set ERROR_CODE=0
 54: 
 55: @REM To isolate internal variables from possible post scripts, we use another setlocal
 56: @setlocal
 57: 
 58: @REM ==== START VALIDATION ====
 59: if not "%JAVA_HOME%" == "" goto OkJHome
 60: 
 61: echo.
 62: echo Error: JAVA_HOME not found in your environment. >&2
 63: echo Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the >&2
 64: echo location of your Java installation. >&2
 65: echo.
 66: goto error
 67: 
 68: :OkJHome
 69: if exist "%JAVA_HOME%\bin\java.exe" goto init
 70: 
 71: echo.
 72: echo Error: JAVA_HOME is set to an invalid directory. >&2
 73: echo JAVA_HOME = "%JAVA_HOME%" >&2
 74: echo Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the >&2
 75: echo location of your Java installation. >&2
 76: echo.
 77: goto error
 78: 
 79: @REM ==== END VALIDATION ====
 80: 
 81: :init
 82: 
 83: @REM Find the project base dir, i.e. the directory that contains the folder ".mvn".
 84: @REM Fallback to current working directory if not found.
 85: 
 86: set MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=%MAVEN_BASEDIR%
 87: IF NOT "%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%"=="" goto endDetectBaseDir
 88: 
 89: set EXEC_DIR=%CD%
 90: set WDIR=%EXEC_DIR%
 91: :findBaseDir
 92: IF EXIST "%WDIR%"\.mvn goto baseDirFound
 93: cd ..
 94: IF "%WDIR%"=="%CD%" goto baseDirNotFound
 95: set WDIR=%CD%
 96: goto findBaseDir
 97: 
 98: :baseDirFound
 99: set MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=%WDIR%
100: cd "%EXEC_DIR%"
101: goto endDetectBaseDir
102: 
103: :baseDirNotFound
104: set MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=%EXEC_DIR%
105: cd "%EXEC_DIR%"
106: 
107: :endDetectBaseDir
108: 
109: IF NOT EXIST "%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%\.mvn\jvm.config" goto endReadAdditionalConfig
110: 
111: @setlocal EnableExtensions EnableDelayedExpansion
112: for /F "usebackq delims=" %%a in ("%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%\.mvn\jvm.config") do set JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS=!JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS! %%a
113: @endlocal & set JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS=%JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS%
114: 
115: :endReadAdditionalConfig
116: 
117: SET MAVEN_JAVA_EXE="%JAVA_HOME%\bin\java.exe"
118: 
119: set WRAPPER_JAR="%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%\.mvn\wrapper\maven-wrapper.jar"
120: set WRAPPER_LAUNCHER=org.apache.maven.wrapper.MavenWrapperMain
121: 
122: %MAVEN_JAVA_EXE% %JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS% %MAVEN_OPTS% %MAVEN_DEBUG_OPTS% -classpath %WRAPPER_JAR% "-Dmaven.multiModuleProjectDirectory=%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%" %WRAPPER_LAUNCHER% %MAVEN_CONFIG% %*
123: if ERRORLEVEL 1 goto error
124: goto end
125: 
126: :error
127: set ERROR_CODE=1
128: 
129: :end
130: @endlocal & set ERROR_CODE=%ERROR_CODE%
131: 
132: if not "%MAVEN_SKIP_RC%" == "" goto skipRcPost
133: @REM check for post script, once with legacy .bat ending and once with .cmd ending
134: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.bat" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.bat"
135: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.cmd" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.cmd"
136: :skipRcPost
137: 
138: @REM pause the script if MAVEN_BATCH_PAUSE is set to 'on'
139: if "%MAVEN_BATCH_PAUSE%" == "on" pause
140: 
141: if "%MAVEN_TERMINATE_CMD%" == "on" exit %ERROR_CODE%
142: 
143: exit /B %ERROR_CODE%

File: account-service/src/main/java/com/project/warehouse/event/factory/DomainEventFactory.java

 1: package com.project.warehouse.event.factory;
 2: import com.datastax.driver.core.utils.UUIDs;
 3: import com.project.warehouse.event.model.*;
 4: import com.project.warehouse.security.SecurityUtils;
 5: import lombok.Data;
 6: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 7: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
 8: import java.util.Date;
 9: import java.util.Map;
10: @Data
11: @Component
12: public class DomainEventFactory {
13:     @Autowired
14:     private SecurityUtils securityUtils;
15:     public AccountCreationEvent newAccountCreationEvent(String correlationId, String userName){
16:         AccountCreationEvent event = new AccountCreationEvent();
17:         event.setId(UUIDs.timeBased());
18:         event.setUserName(userName);
19:         event.setAuditData(newEventAuditData(correlationId));
20:         return event;
21:     }
22:     public AccountNotFoundEvent newAccountNotFoundEvent(String correlationId, String userName){
23:         AccountNotFoundEvent event = new AccountNotFoundEvent();
24:         event.setId(UUIDs.timeBased());
25:         event.setUserName(userName);
26:         event.setAuditData(newEventAuditData(correlationId));
27:         return event;
28:     }
29:     public AccountValidationErrorEvent newAccountValidationErrorEvent(String correlationId, Map<String,String> errors){
30:         AccountValidationErrorEvent event = new AccountValidationErrorEvent();
31:         event.setId(UUIDs.timeBased());
32:         event.setValidationError(errors);
33:         event.setAuditData(newEventAuditData(correlationId));
34:         return event;
35:     }
36:     public ChangeAccountPasswordEvent newChangeAccountPasswordEvent(String correlationId, String userName){
37:         ChangeAccountPasswordEvent event = new ChangeAccountPasswordEvent();
38:         event.setId(UUIDs.timeBased());
39:         event.setUserName(userName);
40:         event.setAuditData(newEventAuditData(correlationId));
41:         return event;
42:     }
43:     public IdentityValidationErrorEvent newIdentityValidationErrorEvent(String correlationId, String userName, String message){
44:         IdentityValidationErrorEvent event = new IdentityValidationErrorEvent();
45:         event.setId(UUIDs.timeBased());
46:         event.setUserName(userName);
47:         event.setMessage(message);
48:         event.setAuditData(newEventAuditData(correlationId));
49:         return event;
50:     }
51:     public RemoveAccountErrorEvent newRemoveAccountErrorEvent(String correlationId, String userName,
52:                                                               String message, Class exceptionClassName){
53:         RemoveAccountErrorEvent event = new RemoveAccountErrorEvent();
54:         event.setId(UUIDs.timeBased());
55:         event.setUserName(userName);
56:         event.setMessage(message);
57:         event.setExceptionClassName(exceptionClassName.getName());
58:         event.setAuditData(newEventAuditData(correlationId));
59:         return event;
60:     }
61:     public RemoveAccountEvent newRemoveAccountEvent(String correlationId, String userName){
62:         RemoveAccountEvent event = new RemoveAccountEvent();
63:         event.setId(UUIDs.timeBased());
64:         event.setUserName(userName);
65:         event.setAuditData(newEventAuditData(correlationId));
66:         return event;
67:     }
68:     public SaveAccountErrorEvent newSaveAccountErrorEvent(String correlationId,String userName,
69:                                                           String message, Class exceptionClassName){
70:         SaveAccountErrorEvent event = new SaveAccountErrorEvent();
71:         event.setId(UUIDs.timeBased());
72:         event.setUserName(userName);
73:         event.setMessage(message);
74:         event.setExceptionClassName(exceptionClassName.getName());
75:         event.setAuditData(newEventAuditData(correlationId));
76:         return event;
77:     }
78:     private EventAuditData newEventAuditData(String correlationId){
79:         EventAuditData eventAuditData = new EventAuditData();
80:         eventAuditData.setCorrelationId(correlationId);
81:         eventAuditData.setUserName(securityUtils.getPrincipalUserName());
82:         eventAuditData.setTimeStamp(new Date());
83:         return eventAuditData;
84:     }
85: }

 1: package com.project.warehouse.event.model;

File: account-service/src/main/java/com/project/warehouse/event/service/EventDomainPubblishService.java
================
 1: package com.project.warehouse.event.service;
 2: import com.project.warehouse.event.factory.DomainEventFactory;
 3: import com.project.warehouse.event.model.*;
 4: import com.project.warehouse.event.repository.*;
 5: import lombok.Data;
 6: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 7: import org.springframework.cloud.stream.annotation.EnableBinding;
 8: import org.springframework.messaging.SubscribableChannel;
 9: import org.springframework.messaging.support.MessageBuilder;
10: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
11: import java.util.Map;
12: @Data
13: @Service
14: public class EventDomainPubblishService {
15:     @Autowired
16:     private DomainEventFactory domainEventFactory;
17:     @Autowired
18:     private AccountCreationEventRepository accountCreationEventRepository;
19:     @Autowired
20:     private AccountNotFoundEventRepository accountNotFoundEventRepository;
21:     @Autowired
22:     private AccountValidationErrorEventRepository accountValidationErrorEventRepository;
23:     @Autowired
24:     private ChangeAccountPasswordEventRepository changeAccountPasswordEventRepository;
25:     @Autowired
26:     private IdentityValidationErrorEventRepository identityValidationErrorEventRepository;
27:     @Autowired
28:     private RemoveAccountErrorEventRepository removeAccountErrorEventRepository;
29:     @Autowired
30:     private RemoveAccountEventRepository removeAccountEventRepository;
31:     @Autowired
32:     private SaveAccountErrorEventRepository saveAccountErrorEventRepository;
33:     @Autowired
34:     private SubscribableChannel accountEventOutboundChannel;
35:     public AccountCreationEvent publishAccountCreationEvent(String correlationId, String userName){
36:         AccountCreationEvent event = domainEventFactory.newAccountCreationEvent(correlationId, userName);
37:         accountCreationEventRepository.save(event);
38:         accountEventOutboundChannel.send(MessageBuilder.withPayload(event).build());
39:         return event;
40:     }
41:     public AccountNotFoundEvent publishAccountNotFoundEvent(String correlationId, String userName){
42:         AccountNotFoundEvent event = domainEventFactory.newAccountNotFoundEvent(correlationId, userName);
43:         accountNotFoundEventRepository.save(event);
44:         accountEventOutboundChannel.send(MessageBuilder.withPayload(event).build());
45:         return event;
46:     }
47:     public AccountValidationErrorEvent publishAccountValidationErrorEvent(String correlationId, Map<String,String> errors){
48:         AccountValidationErrorEvent event = domainEventFactory.newAccountValidationErrorEvent(correlationId, errors);
49:         accountValidationErrorEventRepository.save(event);
50:         accountEventOutboundChannel.send(MessageBuilder.withPayload(event).build());
51:         return event;
52:     }
53:     public ChangeAccountPasswordEvent publishChangeAccountPasswordEvent(String correlationId, String userName){
54:         ChangeAccountPasswordEvent event = domainEventFactory.newChangeAccountPasswordEvent(correlationId, userName);
55:         changeAccountPasswordEventRepository.save(event);
56:         accountEventOutboundChannel.send(MessageBuilder.withPayload(event).build());
57:         return event;
58:     }
59:     public IdentityValidationErrorEvent publishIdentityValidationErrorEvent(String correlationId, String userName, String message){
60:         IdentityValidationErrorEvent event = domainEventFactory.newIdentityValidationErrorEvent(correlationId, userName, message);
61:         identityValidationErrorEventRepository.save(event);
62:         accountEventOutboundChannel.send(MessageBuilder.withPayload(event).build());
63:         return event;
64:     }
65:     public RemoveAccountErrorEvent publishRemoveAccountErrorEvent(String correlationId, String userName,
66:                                                                   String message, Class exceptionClassName){
67:         RemoveAccountErrorEvent event = domainEventFactory.newRemoveAccountErrorEvent(correlationId, userName, message,exceptionClassName);
68:         removeAccountErrorEventRepository.save(event);
69:         accountEventOutboundChannel.send(MessageBuilder.withPayload(event).build());
70:         return event;
71:     }
72:     public RemoveAccountEvent publishRemoveAccountEvent(String correlationId, String userName){
73:         RemoveAccountEvent event = domainEventFactory.newRemoveAccountEvent(correlationId, userName);
74:         removeAccountEventRepository.save(event);
75:         accountEventOutboundChannel.send(MessageBuilder.withPayload(event).build());
76:         return event;
77:     }
78:     public SaveAccountErrorEvent publishSaveAccountErrorEvent(String correlationId,String userName,
79:                                                               String message, Class exceptionClassName){
80:         SaveAccountErrorEvent event = domainEventFactory.newSaveAccountErrorEvent(correlationId, userName, message, exceptionClassName);
81:         saveAccountErrorEventRepository.save(event);
82:         accountEventOutboundChannel.send(MessageBuilder.withPayload(event).build());
83:         return event;
84:     }
85: }

================
 1: package com.project.warehouse.exception;
 2: import com.project.warehouse.event.model.AbstractDomainEvent;
 3: import lombok.Getter;
 4: @Getter
 5: public abstract class AbstractAccountException extends RuntimeException {
 6:     protected final AbstractDomainEvent event;
 7:     public AbstractAccountException(AbstractDomainEvent event, String msg) {
 8:         super(msg);
 9:         this.event = event;
10:     }
11:     public AbstractAccountException(AbstractDomainEvent event, String msg, Throwable cause) {
12:         super(msg, cause);
13:         this.event = event;
14:     }
15: }

 1: package com.project.warehouse.factory;
 2: import com.project.warehouse.model.Account;
 3: import com.project.warehouse.model.Address;
 4: import com.project.warehouse.model.Phone;
 5: import java.util.Date;
 6: public class AccountBuilder {
 7:     private Account account;
 8:     private  AccountBuilder(){}
 9:     private void setAccount(Account account) {
10:         this.account = account;
11:     }
12:     public static AccountBuilder newAccountBuilder(){
13:         AccountBuilder accountBuilder = new AccountBuilder();
14:         accountBuilder.setAccount(new Account());
15:         return accountBuilder;
16:     }
17:     public AccountBuilder userName(String userName){
18:         this.account.setUserName(userName);
19:         return this;
20:     }
21:     public AccountBuilder password(String password){
22:         this.account.setPassword(password);
23:         return this;
24:     }
25:     public AccountBuilder mail(String mail){
26:         this.account.setMail(mail);
27:         return this;
28:     }
29:     public AccountBuilder role(String role){
30:         this.account.setRole(role);
31:         return this;
32:     }
33:     public AccountBuilder taxCode(String taxCode){
34:         this.account.setTaxCode(taxCode);
35:         return this;
36:     }
37:     public AccountBuilder firstName(String firstName){
38:         this.account.setFirstName(firstName);
39:         return this;
40:     }
41:     public AccountBuilder lastName(String lastName){
42:         this.account.setLastName(lastName);
43:         return this;
44:     }
45:     public AccountBuilder birthDate(Date birthDate){
46:         this.account.setBirthDate(birthDate);
47:         return this;
48:     }
49:     public AccountBuilder withAddrees(String street, String streenNumber, String zip, String country, String region, String city){
50:         Address address = new Address();
51:         address.setStreet(street);
52:         address.setStreenNumber(streenNumber);
53:         address.setZip(zip);
54:         address.setCountry(country);
55:         address.setRegion(region);
56:         address.setCity(city);
57:         this.account.setAddress(address);
58:         return this;
59:     }
60:     public AccountBuilder withTelephoneNumber(String countryPrefix, String prefix, String telephone){
61:         Phone telephoneNumber = new Phone();
62:         telephoneNumber.setCountryPrefix(countryPrefix);
63:         telephoneNumber.setPrefix(prefix);
64:         telephoneNumber.setTelephone(telephone);
65:         this.account.setTelephoneNumber(telephoneNumber);
66:         return this;
67:     }
68:     public Account build(){
69:         return this.account;
70:     }
71: }

File: account-service/src/main/java/com/project/warehouse/hateoas/AccountHateoasFactory.java
================
 1: package com.project.warehouse.hateoas;
 2: import com.project.warehouse.model.Account;
 3: import com.project.warehouse.rest.AccountRestController;
 4: import org.springframework.hateoas.Link;
 5: import org.springframework.hateoas.Resource;
 6: import org.springframework.hateoas.Resources;
 7: import org.springframework.hateoas.UriTemplate;
 8: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
 9: import java.util.List;
10: import static org.springframework.hateoas.mvc.ControllerLinkBuilder.linkTo;
11: @Component
12: public class AccountHateoasFactory {
13:     public Resource<Account> toResource(Account account) {
14:         Resource<Account> accountResource = new Resource<>(account);
15:         Link link = linkTo(AccountRestController.class).slash(account.getUserName())
16:                 .withSelfRel();
17:         accountResource.add(link);
18:         return accountResource;
19:     }
20:     public Resources<Account> toResources(List<Account> accountList) {
21:         Resources<Account> accountResource = new Resources<>(accountList);
22:         UriTemplate uriTemplate = new UriTemplate(String.format("%s/{userName}",linkTo(AccountRestController.class).toString()));
23:         Link accountLink = new Link(uriTemplate, "account");
24:         accountResource.add(accountLink);
25:         return accountResource;
26:     }
27: }

================
File: account-service/src/main/java/com/project/warehouse/rest/AccountRestController.java
================
 1: package com.project.warehouse.rest;
 2: import com.netflix.hystrix.contrib.javanica.annotation.HystrixCommand;
 3: import com.netflix.hystrix.contrib.javanica.annotation.HystrixProperty;
 4: import com.project.warehouse.hateoas.AccountHateoasFactory;
 5: import com.project.warehouse.model.Account;
 6: import com.project.warehouse.service.AccountService;
 7: import lombok.Data;
 8: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 9: import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
10: import org.springframework.security.access.prepost.PreAuthorize;
11: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;
12: import org.springframework.web.servlet.mvc.method.annotation.MvcUriComponentsBuilder;
13: @Data
14: @RestController
15: @RequestMapping("/account")
16: public class AccountRestController {
17:     @Autowired
18:     private AccountService accountService;
19:     @Autowired
20:     private AccountHateoasFactory accountHateoasFactory;
21:     @PostMapping
22:     @HystrixCommand(commandProperties = {@HystrixProperty(name="execution.isolation.strategy", value="SEMAPHORE")})
23:     public ResponseEntity createAccount(@RequestBody Account account){
24:         Account savedAccount = accountService.createAccount(account);
25:         return ResponseEntity.created(MvcUriComponentsBuilder
26:                 .fromMethodName(AccountRestController.class,"findAccount",savedAccount.getUserName())
27:                 .build().toUri()).build();
28:     }
29:     @GetMapping
30:     @PreAuthorize("isAuthenticated()")
31:     @HystrixCommand(commandProperties = {@HystrixProperty(name="execution.isolation.strategy", value="SEMAPHORE")})
32:     public ResponseEntity findAccountList(){
33:         return ResponseEntity.ok(accountHateoasFactory.toResources(accountService.findAccountList()));
34:     }
35:     @GetMapping("/{userName}")
36:     @PreAuthorize("isAuthenticated()")
37:     @HystrixCommand(commandProperties = {@HystrixProperty(name="execution.isolation.strategy", value="SEMAPHORE")})
38:     public ResponseEntity findAccount(@PathVariable String userName){
39:         return ResponseEntity.ok(accountHateoasFactory.toResource(accountService.findAccount(userName)));
40:     }
41:     @PutMapping("/{userName}")
42:     @PreAuthorize("isAuthenticated()")
43:     @HystrixCommand(commandProperties = {@HystrixProperty(name="execution.isolation.strategy", value="SEMAPHORE")})
44:     public ResponseEntity updateAccount(@PathVariable String userName, @RequestBody Account account){
45:         account.setUserName(userName);
46:         accountService.updateAccount(account);
47:         return ResponseEntity.noContent().build();
48:     }
49:     @DeleteMapping("/{userName}")
50:     @PreAuthorize("isAuthenticated()")
51:     @HystrixCommand(commandProperties = {@HystrixProperty(name="execution.isolation.strategy", value="SEMAPHORE")})
52:     public ResponseEntity deleteAccount(@PathVariable String userName){
53:         accountService.deleteAccount(userName);
54:         return ResponseEntity.noContent().build();
55:     }
56: }

File: account-service/src/main/java/com/project/warehouse/security/AccountUserDetailsService.java
================
 1: package com.project.warehouse.security;
 2: import com.project.warehouse.repository.AccountRepository;
 3: import org.springframework.security.core.authority.SimpleGrantedAuthority;
 4: import org.springframework.security.core.userdetails.User;
 5: import org.springframework.security.core.userdetails.UserDetails;
 6: import org.springframework.security.core.userdetails.UserDetailsService;
 7: import org.springframework.security.core.userdetails.UsernameNotFoundException;
 8: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
 9: import java.util.Collections;
10: import java.util.Optional;
11: @Service
12: public class AccountUserDetailsService implements UserDetailsService {
13:     private final AccountRepository accountRepository;
14:     AccountUserDetailsService(AccountRepository accountRepository) {
15:         this.accountRepository = accountRepository;
16:     }
17:     @Override
18:     public UserDetails loadUserByUsername(String userName) throws UsernameNotFoundException {
19:         return Optional.ofNullable(accountRepository.findOne(userName))
20:                 .map((account) -> new User(account.getUserName(), account.getPassword(),
21:                         Collections.singleton(new SimpleGrantedAuthority(account.getRole()))))
22:                 .orElseThrow(() -> new UsernameNotFoundException(""));
23:     }
24: }

================
File: account-service/src/main/java/com/project/warehouse/service/AccountServiceImpl.java
================
  1: package com.project.warehouse.service;
  2: import com.project.warehouse.event.model.AccountNotFoundEvent;
  3: import com.project.warehouse.event.model.RemoveAccountErrorEvent;
  4: import com.project.warehouse.event.model.SaveAccountErrorEvent;
  5: import com.project.warehouse.event.service.EventDomainPubblishService;
  6: import com.project.warehouse.exception.ConflictSaveAccountException;
  7: import com.project.warehouse.exception.RemoveAccountException;
  8: import com.project.warehouse.exception.SaveAccountException;
  9: import com.project.warehouse.model.Account;
 10: import com.project.warehouse.repository.AccountRepository;
 11: import com.project.warehouse.validator.AccountDataValidationService;
 12: import lombok.Data;
 13: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 14: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
 15: import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
 16: import javax.security.auth.login.AccountNotFoundException;
 17: import java.util.List;
 18: import java.util.UUID;
 19: import java.util.function.Function;
 20: @Data
 21: @Service
 22: @Transactional
 23: public class AccountServiceImpl implements AccountService {
 24:     @Autowired
 25:     private AccountDataValidationService accountDataValidationService;
 26:     @Autowired
 27:     private EventDomainPubblishService eventDomainPubblishService;
 28:     @Autowired
 29:     private AccountRepository accountRepository;
 30:     @Override
 31:     public Account createAccount(Account account) {
 32:         String correlationId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
 33:         accountDataValidationService.validate(correlationId, account);
 34:         Account save = doSaveAccountData(correlationId, account, true);
 35:         eventDomainPubblishService.publishAccountCreationEvent(correlationId, save.getUserName());
 36:         return save;
 37:     }
 38:     @Override
 39:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
 40:     public List<Account> findAccountList() {
 41:         return accountRepository.findAll();
 42:     }
 43:     @Override
 44:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
 45:     public Account findAccount(String userName) {
 46:         doCheckAccountExist(UUID.randomUUID().toString(),userName);
 47:         return accountRepository.findOne(userName);
 48:     }
 49:     @Override
 50:     public Account updateAccount(Account account) {
 51:         String correlationId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
 52:         accountDataValidationService.validate(correlationId, account);
 53:         doCheckAccountExist(correlationId, account.getUserName());
 54:         Account save = doSaveAccountData(correlationId, account, false);
 55:         if(!account.getPassword().equals(save.getPassword())){
 56:             eventDomainPubblishService.publishChangeAccountPasswordEvent(correlationId, save.getUserName());
 57:         }
 58:         return save;
 59:     }
 60:     @Override
 61:     public void deleteAccount(String userName) {
 62:         String correlationId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
 63:         accountDataValidationService.validateUserName(correlationId,userName);
 64:         doDeleteAccount(correlationId, userName);
 65:         eventDomainPubblishService.publishRemoveAccountEvent(correlationId,userName);
 66:     }
 67:     private Account doSaveAccountData(String correlationId, Account account, boolean checkDuplicate) {
 68:         Account accountAux = account;
 69:         ConflictSaveAccountException conflictSaveAccountException = null;
 70:         SaveAccountErrorEvent saveAccountErrorEvent;
 71:         try{
 72:             if(checkDuplicate){
 73:                 Account one = accountRepository.findOne(account.getUserName());
 74:                 if(one == null){
 75:                     accountAux = accountRepository.saveAndFlush(account);
 76:                 } else {
 77:                     saveAccountErrorEvent = eventDomainPubblishService.publishSaveAccountErrorEvent(correlationId, account.getUserName(),
 78:                             ConflictSaveAccountException.DEFAULT_MESSAGE, ConflictSaveAccountException.class);
 79:                     conflictSaveAccountException = new ConflictSaveAccountException(saveAccountErrorEvent, ConflictSaveAccountException.DEFAULT_MESSAGE);
 80:                 }
 81:             }else {
 82:                 accountAux = accountRepository.saveAndFlush(account);
 83:             }
 84:         } catch (Exception e){
 85:             saveAccountErrorEvent = eventDomainPubblishService.publishSaveAccountErrorEvent(correlationId, account.getUserName(),
 86:                     SaveAccountException.DEFAULT_MESSAGE, SaveAccountException.class);
 87:             throw new SaveAccountException(saveAccountErrorEvent, SaveAccountException.DEFAULT_MESSAGE);
 88:         }
 89:         if(conflictSaveAccountException!= null){
 90:             throw conflictSaveAccountException;
 91:         }
 92:         return accountAux;
 93:     }
 94:     private void doCheckAccountExist(String correlationId, String userName) {
 95:         Account accountAux;
 96:         Function<String, AccountNotFoundException> f = userNameAux -> {
 97:             AccountNotFoundEvent accountNotFoundEvent = eventDomainPubblishService.publishAccountNotFoundEvent(correlationId, userNameAux);
 98:             return null;
 99:         };
100:         try{
101:             accountAux =  accountRepository.findOne(userName);
102:             if(accountAux == null){
103:                 throw f.apply(userName);
104:             }
105:         } catch (Exception e){
106:         }
107:     }
108:     private void doDeleteAccount(String correlationId, String userName) {
109:         doCheckAccountExist(correlationId, userName);
110:         try{
111:             accountRepository.delete(userName);
112:         } catch (Exception e){
113:             RemoveAccountErrorEvent removeAccountErrorEvent = eventDomainPubblishService.publishRemoveAccountErrorEvent(correlationId, userName,RemoveAccountException.DEFAULT_MESSAGE,RemoveAccountException.class);
114:             throw new RemoveAccountException(removeAccountErrorEvent, RemoveAccountException.DEFAULT_MESSAGE);
115:         }
116:     }
117: }

6:     void validateUserName(String correlationId, String userName) throws AccountValidationException;

================
File: account-service/src/main/java/com/project/warehouse/validator/AccountDataValidationServiceImpl.java
================
 1: package com.project.warehouse.validator;
 2: import com.project.warehouse.event.model.AccountValidationErrorEvent;
 3: import com.project.warehouse.event.service.EventDomainPubblishService;
 4: import com.project.warehouse.exception.AccountValidationException;
 5: import com.project.warehouse.model.Account;
 6: import lombok.Data;
 7: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 8: import org.springframework.context.MessageSource;
 9: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
10: import java.util.HashMap;
11: import java.util.Locale;
12: import java.util.Map;
13: import java.util.regex.Matcher;
14: import java.util.regex.Pattern;
15: @Data
16: @Component
17: public class AccountDataValidationServiceImpl implements AccountDataValidationService {
18:     public static final int TAX_CODE_LENGTH= 16;
19:     public static final String ACCOUNT_VALIDATION_EXCEPTION_MESSAGE = "The Account %s hasn't satisfies the constraints";
20:     private static final String EMAIL_PATTERN =
21:             "^[_A-Za-z0-9-\\+]+(\\.[_A-Za-z0-9-]+)*@"
22:                     + "[A-Za-z0-9-]+(\\.[A-Za-z0-9]+)*(\\.[A-Za-z]{2,})$";
23:     @Autowired
24:     private EventDomainPubblishService eventDomainPubblishService;
25:     @Autowired
26:     private MessageSource messageSource;
27:     @Override
28:     public void validate(String correlationId, Account account) {
29:         Map<String, String> errors = new HashMap<>();
30:         String userName = account.getUserName();
31:         String password = account.getPassword();
32:         String taxCode = account.getTaxCode();
33:         String firstName = account.getFirstName();
34:         String lastName = account.getLastName();
35:         String mail = account.getMail();
36:         validateNotNullAndNotEmpty("userName", userName, "AccountDataValidationService.Account.userName",errors);
37:         validateNotNullAndNotEmpty("password", password, "AccountDataValidationService.Account.password",errors);
38:         validateNotNullAndNotEmpty("firstName", firstName, "AccountDataValidationService.Account.firstName",errors);
39:         validateNotNullAndNotEmpty("lastName", lastName, "AccountDataValidationService.Account.lastName",errors);
40:         validateTaxCode("taxCode", taxCode, "AccountDataValidationService.Account.taxCode",errors);
41:         validateMail("mail", mail, "AccountDataValidationService.Account.mail",errors);
42:         manageError(correlationId,userName,errors);
43:     }
44:     @Override
45:     public void validateUserName(String correlationId, String userName) {
46:         Map<String, String> errors = new HashMap<>();
47:         validateNotNullAndNotEmpty("userName", userName, "AccountDataValidationService.Account.userName",errors);
48:         manageError(correlationId,userName,errors);
49:     }
50:     private void manageError(String correlationId, String userName, Map<String,String> errors) {
51:         if (errors.size() > 0) {
52:             AccountValidationErrorEvent accountValidationErrorEvent =
53:                     eventDomainPubblishService.publishAccountValidationErrorEvent(correlationId, errors);
54:             throw new AccountValidationException(accountValidationErrorEvent,
55:                     String.format(ACCOUNT_VALIDATION_EXCEPTION_MESSAGE, userName));
56:         }
57:     }
58:     private void validateMail(String key,String value,String validationMessageKey, Map<String,String> errors){
59:         boolean inError = value == null;
60:         if(!inError){
61:             Pattern pattern = Pattern.compile(EMAIL_PATTERN);
62:             Matcher matcher = pattern.matcher(value);
63:             inError = !matcher.matches();
64:         }
65:         if(inError){
66:             errors.put(key, messageSource.getMessage(validationMessageKey,new Object[]{}, Locale.ENGLISH));
67:         }
68:     }
69:     private void validateTaxCode(String key,String value,String validationMessageKey, Map<String,String> errors){
70:         boolean inError = value == null || value.length() != TAX_CODE_LENGTH;
71:         if(inError){
72:             errors.put(key, messageSource.getMessage(validationMessageKey,new Object[]{}, Locale.ENGLISH));
73:         }
74:     }
75:     private void validateNotNullAndNotEmpty(String key,String value,String validationMessageKey, Map<String,String> errors){
76:         if (value == null || "".equals(value)){
77:             errors.put(key, messageSource.getMessage(validationMessageKey,new Object[]{}, Locale.ENGLISH));
78:         }
79:     }
80: }

 1: package com.project.warehouse.web;

 4: import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
 5: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.ExceptionHandler;
 6: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.ResponseStatus;
 7: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestControllerAdvice;
 8: @RestControllerAdvice
 9: public class RestExceptionRestControllerAdvice {
10:     @ResponseStatus(HttpStatus.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR)
11:     @ExceptionHandler(value = {RemoveAccountException.class, SaveAccountException.class})
12:     public AbstractDomainEvent handleInternalServerErrorRequest(Exception ex) {
13:         return ((AbstractAccountException) ex).getEvent();
14:     }
15:     @ResponseStatus(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND)
16:     @ExceptionHandler(value = {AccountNotFoundException.class})
17:     public AbstractDomainEvent handleNotFoundRequest(Exception ex) {
18:         return ((AbstractAccountException) ex).getEvent();
19:     }
20:     @ResponseStatus(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST)
21:     @ExceptionHandler(value = {AccountValidationException.class})
22:     public AbstractDomainEvent handleBadRequest(Exception ex) {
23:         return ((AbstractAccountException) ex).getEvent();
24:     }
25:     @ResponseStatus(HttpStatus.FORBIDDEN)
26:     @ExceptionHandler(value = {IdentityValidationException.class})
27:     public AbstractDomainEvent handleForbiddenRequest(Exception ex) {
28:         return ((AbstractAccountException) ex).getEvent();
29:     }
30:     @ResponseStatus(HttpStatus.CONFLICT)
31:     @ExceptionHandler(value = {ConflictSaveAccountException.class})
32:     public AbstractDomainEvent handleConflictRequest(Exception ex) {
33:         return ((AbstractAccountException) ex).getEvent();
34:     }
35: }

================
File: api-gateway/mvnw
================
  1: #!/bin/sh
  2: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  3: # Licensed to the Apache Software Foundation (ASF) under one
  4: # or more contributor license agreements.  See the NOTICE file
  5: # distributed with this work for additional information
  6: # regarding copyright ownership.  The ASF licenses this file
  7: # to you under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the
  8: # "License"); you may not use this file except in compliance
  9: # with the License.  You may obtain a copy of the License at
 10: #
 11: #    http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
 12: #
 13: # Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing,
 14: # software distributed under the License is distributed on an
 15: # "AS IS" BASIS, WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY
 16: # KIND, either express or implied.  See the License for the
 17: # specific language governing permissions and limitations
 18: # under the License.
 19: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 20: 
 21: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 22: # Maven2 Start Up Batch script
 23: #
 24: # Required ENV vars:
 25: # ------------------
 26: #   JAVA_HOME - location of a JDK home dir
 27: #
 28: # Optional ENV vars
 29: # -----------------
 30: #   M2_HOME - location of maven2's installed home dir
 31: #   MAVEN_OPTS - parameters passed to the Java VM when running Maven
 32: #     e.g. to debug Maven itself, use
 33: #       set MAVEN_OPTS=-Xdebug -Xrunjdwp:transport=dt_socket,server=y,suspend=y,address=8000
 34: #   MAVEN_SKIP_RC - flag to disable loading of mavenrc files
 35: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 36: 
 37: if [ -z "$MAVEN_SKIP_RC" ] ; then
 38: 
 39:   if [ -f /etc/mavenrc ] ; then
 40:     . /etc/mavenrc
 41:   fi
 42: 
 43:   if [ -f "$HOME/.mavenrc" ] ; then
 44:     . "$HOME/.mavenrc"
 45:   fi
 46: 
 47: fi
 48: 
 49: # OS specific support.  $var _must_ be set to either true or false.
 50: cygwin=false;
 51: darwin=false;
 52: mingw=false
 53: case "`uname`" in
 54:   CYGWIN*) cygwin=true ;;
 55:   MINGW*) mingw=true;;
 56:   Darwin*) darwin=true
 57:     # Use /usr/libexec/java_home if available, otherwise fall back to /Library/Java/Home
 58:     # See https://developer.apple.com/library/mac/qa/qa1170/_index.html
 59:     if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ]; then
 60:       if [ -x "/usr/libexec/java_home" ]; then
 61:         export JAVA_HOME="`/usr/libexec/java_home`"
 62:       else
 63:         export JAVA_HOME="/Library/Java/Home"
 64:       fi
 65:     fi
 66:     ;;
 67: esac
 68: 
 69: if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ] ; then
 70:   if [ -r /etc/gentoo-release ] ; then
 71:     JAVA_HOME=`java-config --jre-home`
 72:   fi
 73: fi
 74: 
 75: if [ -z "$M2_HOME" ] ; then
 76:   ## resolve links - $0 may be a link to maven's home
 77:   PRG="$0"
 78: 
 79:   # need this for relative symlinks
 80:   while [ -h "$PRG" ] ; do
 81:     ls=`ls -ld "$PRG"`
 82:     link=`expr "$ls" : '.*-> \(.*\)$'`
 83:     if expr "$link" : '/.*' > /dev/null; then
 84:       PRG="$link"
 85:     else
 86:       PRG="`dirname "$PRG"`/$link"
 87:     fi
 88:   done
 89: 
 90:   saveddir=`pwd`
 91: 
 92:   M2_HOME=`dirname "$PRG"`/..
 93: 
 94:   # make it fully qualified
 95:   M2_HOME=`cd "$M2_HOME" && pwd`
 96: 
 97:   cd "$saveddir"
 98:   # echo Using m2 at $M2_HOME
 99: fi
100: 
101: # For Cygwin, ensure paths are in UNIX format before anything is touched
102: if $cygwin ; then
103:   [ -n "$M2_HOME" ] &&
104:     M2_HOME=`cygpath --unix "$M2_HOME"`
105:   [ -n "$JAVA_HOME" ] &&
106:     JAVA_HOME=`cygpath --unix "$JAVA_HOME"`
107:   [ -n "$CLASSPATH" ] &&
108:     CLASSPATH=`cygpath --path --unix "$CLASSPATH"`
109: fi
110: 
111: # For Migwn, ensure paths are in UNIX format before anything is touched
112: if $mingw ; then
113:   [ -n "$M2_HOME" ] &&
114:     M2_HOME="`(cd "$M2_HOME"; pwd)`"
115:   [ -n "$JAVA_HOME" ] &&
116:     JAVA_HOME="`(cd "$JAVA_HOME"; pwd)`"
117:   # TODO classpath?
118: fi
119: 
120: if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ]; then
121:   javaExecutable="`which javac`"
122:   if [ -n "$javaExecutable" ] && ! [ "`expr \"$javaExecutable\" : '\([^ ]*\)'`" = "no" ]; then
123:     # readlink(1) is not available as standard on Solaris 10.
124:     readLink=`which readlink`
125:     if [ ! `expr "$readLink" : '\([^ ]*\)'` = "no" ]; then
126:       if $darwin ; then
127:         javaHome="`dirname \"$javaExecutable\"`"
128:         javaExecutable="`cd \"$javaHome\" && pwd -P`/javac"
129:       else
130:         javaExecutable="`readlink -f \"$javaExecutable\"`"
131:       fi
132:       javaHome="`dirname \"$javaExecutable\"`"
133:       javaHome=`expr "$javaHome" : '\(.*\)/bin'`
134:       JAVA_HOME="$javaHome"
135:       export JAVA_HOME
136:     fi
137:   fi
138: fi
139: 
140: if [ -z "$JAVACMD" ] ; then
141:   if [ -n "$JAVA_HOME"  ] ; then
142:     if [ -x "$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java" ] ; then
143:       # IBM's JDK on AIX uses strange locations for the executables
144:       JAVACMD="$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java"
145:     else
146:       JAVACMD="$JAVA_HOME/bin/java"
147:     fi
148:   else
149:     JAVACMD="`which java`"
150:   fi
151: fi
152: 
153: if [ ! -x "$JAVACMD" ] ; then
154:   echo "Error: JAVA_HOME is not defined correctly." >&2
155:   echo "  We cannot execute $JAVACMD" >&2
156:   exit 1
157: fi
158: 
159: if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ] ; then
160:   echo "Warning: JAVA_HOME environment variable is not set."
161: fi
162: 
163: CLASSWORLDS_LAUNCHER=org.codehaus.plexus.classworlds.launcher.Launcher
164: 
165: # traverses directory structure from process work directory to filesystem root
166: # first directory with .mvn subdirectory is considered project base directory
167: find_maven_basedir() {
168: 
169:   if [ -z "$1" ]
170:   then
171:     echo "Path not specified to find_maven_basedir"
172:     return 1
173:   fi
174: 
175:   basedir="$1"
176:   wdir="$1"
177:   while [ "$wdir" != '/' ] ; do
178:     if [ -d "$wdir"/.mvn ] ; then
179:       basedir=$wdir
180:       break
181:     fi
182:     # workaround for JBEAP-8937 (on Solaris 10/Sparc)
183:     if [ -d "${wdir}" ]; then
184:       wdir=`cd "$wdir/.."; pwd`
185:     fi
186:     # end of workaround
187:   done
188:   echo "${basedir}"
189: }
190: 
191: # concatenates all lines of a file
192: concat_lines() {
193:   if [ -f "$1" ]; then
194:     echo "$(tr -s '\n' ' ' < "$1")"
195:   fi
196: }
197: 
198: BASE_DIR=`find_maven_basedir "$(pwd)"`
199: if [ -z "$BASE_DIR" ]; then
200:   exit 1;
201: fi
202: 
203: export MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=${MAVEN_BASEDIR:-"$BASE_DIR"}
204: echo $MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR
205: MAVEN_OPTS="$(concat_lines "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR/.mvn/jvm.config") $MAVEN_OPTS"
206: 
207: # For Cygwin, switch paths to Windows format before running java
208: if $cygwin; then
209:   [ -n "$M2_HOME" ] &&
210:     M2_HOME=`cygpath --path --windows "$M2_HOME"`
211:   [ -n "$JAVA_HOME" ] &&
212:     JAVA_HOME=`cygpath --path --windows "$JAVA_HOME"`
213:   [ -n "$CLASSPATH" ] &&
214:     CLASSPATH=`cygpath --path --windows "$CLASSPATH"`
215:   [ -n "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR" ] &&
216:     MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=`cygpath --path --windows "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR"`
217: fi
218: 
219: WRAPPER_LAUNCHER=org.apache.maven.wrapper.MavenWrapperMain
220: 
221: exec "$JAVACMD" \
222:   $MAVEN_OPTS \
223:   -classpath "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR/.mvn/wrapper/maven-wrapper.jar" \
224:   "-Dmaven.home=${M2_HOME}" "-Dmaven.multiModuleProjectDirectory=${MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR}" \
225:   ${WRAPPER_LAUNCHER} $MAVEN_CONFIG "$@"

File: api-gateway/mvnw.cmd

  1: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  2: @REM Licensed to the Apache Software Foundation (ASF) under one
  3: @REM or more contributor license agreements.  See the NOTICE file
  4: @REM distributed with this work for additional information
  5: @REM regarding copyright ownership.  The ASF licenses this file
  6: @REM to you under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the
  7: @REM "License"); you may not use this file except in compliance
  8: @REM with the License.  You may obtain a copy of the License at
  9: @REM
 10: @REM    http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
 11: @REM
 12: @REM Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing,
 13: @REM software distributed under the License is distributed on an
 14: @REM "AS IS" BASIS, WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY
 15: @REM KIND, either express or implied.  See the License for the
 16: @REM specific language governing permissions and limitations
 17: @REM under the License.
 18: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 19: 
 20: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 21: @REM Maven2 Start Up Batch script
 22: @REM
 23: @REM Required ENV vars:
 24: @REM JAVA_HOME - location of a JDK home dir
 25: @REM
 26: @REM Optional ENV vars
 27: @REM M2_HOME - location of maven2's installed home dir
 28: @REM MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO - set to 'on' to enable the echoing of the batch commands
 29: @REM MAVEN_BATCH_PAUSE - set to 'on' to wait for a key stroke before ending
 30: @REM MAVEN_OPTS - parameters passed to the Java VM when running Maven
 31: @REM     e.g. to debug Maven itself, use
 32: @REM set MAVEN_OPTS=-Xdebug -Xrunjdwp:transport=dt_socket,server=y,suspend=y,address=8000
 33: @REM MAVEN_SKIP_RC - flag to disable loading of mavenrc files
 34: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 35: 
 36: @REM Begin all REM lines with '@' in case MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO is 'on'
 37: @echo off
 38: @REM enable echoing my setting MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO to 'on'
 39: @if "%MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO%" == "on"  echo %MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO%
 40: 
 41: @REM set %HOME% to equivalent of $HOME
 42: if "%HOME%" == "" (set "HOME=%HOMEDRIVE%%HOMEPATH%")
 43: 
 44: @REM Execute a user defined script before this one
 45: if not "%MAVEN_SKIP_RC%" == "" goto skipRcPre
 46: @REM check for pre script, once with legacy .bat ending and once with .cmd ending
 47: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.bat" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.bat"
 48: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.cmd" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.cmd"
 49: :skipRcPre
 50: 
 51: @setlocal
 52: 
 53: set ERROR_CODE=0
 54: 
 55: @REM To isolate internal variables from possible post scripts, we use another setlocal
 56: @setlocal
 57: 
 58: @REM ==== START VALIDATION ====
 59: if not "%JAVA_HOME%" == "" goto OkJHome
 60: 
 61: echo.
 62: echo Error: JAVA_HOME not found in your environment. >&2
 63: echo Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the >&2
 64: echo location of your Java installation. >&2
 65: echo.
 66: goto error
 67: 
 68: :OkJHome
 69: if exist "%JAVA_HOME%\bin\java.exe" goto init
 70: 
 71: echo.
 72: echo Error: JAVA_HOME is set to an invalid directory. >&2
 73: echo JAVA_HOME = "%JAVA_HOME%" >&2
 74: echo Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the >&2
 75: echo location of your Java installation. >&2
 76: echo.
 77: goto error
 78: 
 79: @REM ==== END VALIDATION ====
 80: 
 81: :init
 82: 
 83: @REM Find the project base dir, i.e. the directory that contains the folder ".mvn".
 84: @REM Fallback to current working directory if not found.
 85: 
 86: set MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=%MAVEN_BASEDIR%
 87: IF NOT "%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%"=="" goto endDetectBaseDir
 88: 
 89: set EXEC_DIR=%CD%
 90: set WDIR=%EXEC_DIR%
 91: :findBaseDir
 92: IF EXIST "%WDIR%"\.mvn goto baseDirFound
 93: cd ..
 94: IF "%WDIR%"=="%CD%" goto baseDirNotFound
 95: set WDIR=%CD%
 96: goto findBaseDir
 97: 
 98: :baseDirFound
 99: set MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=%WDIR%
100: cd "%EXEC_DIR%"
101: goto endDetectBaseDir
102: 
103: :baseDirNotFound
104: set MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=%EXEC_DIR%
105: cd "%EXEC_DIR%"
106: 
107: :endDetectBaseDir
108: 
109: IF NOT EXIST "%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%\.mvn\jvm.config" goto endReadAdditionalConfig
110: 
111: @setlocal EnableExtensions EnableDelayedExpansion
112: for /F "usebackq delims=" %%a in ("%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%\.mvn\jvm.config") do set JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS=!JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS! %%a
113: @endlocal & set JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS=%JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS%
114: 
115: :endReadAdditionalConfig
116: 
117: SET MAVEN_JAVA_EXE="%JAVA_HOME%\bin\java.exe"
118: 
119: set WRAPPER_JAR="%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%\.mvn\wrapper\maven-wrapper.jar"
120: set WRAPPER_LAUNCHER=org.apache.maven.wrapper.MavenWrapperMain
121: 
122: %MAVEN_JAVA_EXE% %JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS% %MAVEN_OPTS% %MAVEN_DEBUG_OPTS% -classpath %WRAPPER_JAR% "-Dmaven.multiModuleProjectDirectory=%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%" %WRAPPER_LAUNCHER% %MAVEN_CONFIG% %*
123: if ERRORLEVEL 1 goto error
124: goto end
125: 
126: :error
127: set ERROR_CODE=1
128: 
129: :end
130: @endlocal & set ERROR_CODE=%ERROR_CODE%
131: 
132: if not "%MAVEN_SKIP_RC%" == "" goto skipRcPost
133: @REM check for post script, once with legacy .bat ending and once with .cmd ending
134: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.bat" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.bat"
135: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.cmd" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.cmd"
136: :skipRcPost
137: 
138: @REM pause the script if MAVEN_BATCH_PAUSE is set to 'on'
139: if "%MAVEN_BATCH_PAUSE%" == "on" pause
140: 
141: if "%MAVEN_TERMINATE_CMD%" == "on" exit %ERROR_CODE%
142: 
143: exit /B %ERROR_CODE%

================
File: authentication-server/mvnw
================
  1: #!/bin/sh
  2: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  3: # Licensed to the Apache Software Foundation (ASF) under one
  4: # or more contributor license agreements.  See the NOTICE file
  5: # distributed with this work for additional information
  6: # regarding copyright ownership.  The ASF licenses this file
  7: # to you under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the
  8: # "License"); you may not use this file except in compliance
  9: # with the License.  You may obtain a copy of the License at
 10: #
 11: #    http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
 12: #
 13: # Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing,
 14: # software distributed under the License is distributed on an
 15: # "AS IS" BASIS, WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY
 16: # KIND, either express or implied.  See the License for the
 17: # specific language governing permissions and limitations
 18: # under the License.
 19: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 20: 
 21: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 22: # Maven2 Start Up Batch script
 23: #
 24: # Required ENV vars:
 25: # ------------------
 26: #   JAVA_HOME - location of a JDK home dir
 27: #
 28: # Optional ENV vars
 29: # -----------------
 30: #   M2_HOME - location of maven2's installed home dir
 31: #   MAVEN_OPTS - parameters passed to the Java VM when running Maven
 32: #     e.g. to debug Maven itself, use
 33: #       set MAVEN_OPTS=-Xdebug -Xrunjdwp:transport=dt_socket,server=y,suspend=y,address=8000
 34: #   MAVEN_SKIP_RC - flag to disable loading of mavenrc files
 35: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 36: 
 37: if [ -z "$MAVEN_SKIP_RC" ] ; then
 38: 
 39:   if [ -f /etc/mavenrc ] ; then
 40:     . /etc/mavenrc
 41:   fi
 42: 
 43:   if [ -f "$HOME/.mavenrc" ] ; then
 44:     . "$HOME/.mavenrc"
 45:   fi
 46: 
 47: fi
 48: 
 49: # OS specific support.  $var _must_ be set to either true or false.
 50: cygwin=false;
 51: darwin=false;
 52: mingw=false
 53: case "`uname`" in
 54:   CYGWIN*) cygwin=true ;;
 55:   MINGW*) mingw=true;;
 56:   Darwin*) darwin=true
 57:     # Use /usr/libexec/java_home if available, otherwise fall back to /Library/Java/Home
 58:     # See https://developer.apple.com/library/mac/qa/qa1170/_index.html
 59:     if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ]; then
 60:       if [ -x "/usr/libexec/java_home" ]; then
 61:         export JAVA_HOME="`/usr/libexec/java_home`"
 62:       else
 63:         export JAVA_HOME="/Library/Java/Home"
 64:       fi
 65:     fi
 66:     ;;
 67: esac
 68: 
 69: if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ] ; then
 70:   if [ -r /etc/gentoo-release ] ; then
 71:     JAVA_HOME=`java-config --jre-home`
 72:   fi
 73: fi
 74: 
 75: if [ -z "$M2_HOME" ] ; then
 76:   ## resolve links - $0 may be a link to maven's home
 77:   PRG="$0"
 78: 
 79:   # need this for relative symlinks
 80:   while [ -h "$PRG" ] ; do
 81:     ls=`ls -ld "$PRG"`
 82:     link=`expr "$ls" : '.*-> \(.*\)$'`
 83:     if expr "$link" : '/.*' > /dev/null; then
 84:       PRG="$link"
 85:     else
 86:       PRG="`dirname "$PRG"`/$link"
 87:     fi
 88:   done
 89: 
 90:   saveddir=`pwd`
 91: 
 92:   M2_HOME=`dirname "$PRG"`/..
 93: 
 94:   # make it fully qualified
 95:   M2_HOME=`cd "$M2_HOME" && pwd`
 96: 
 97:   cd "$saveddir"
 98:   # echo Using m2 at $M2_HOME
 99: fi
100: 
101: # For Cygwin, ensure paths are in UNIX format before anything is touched
102: if $cygwin ; then
103:   [ -n "$M2_HOME" ] &&
104:     M2_HOME=`cygpath --unix "$M2_HOME"`
105:   [ -n "$JAVA_HOME" ] &&
106:     JAVA_HOME=`cygpath --unix "$JAVA_HOME"`
107:   [ -n "$CLASSPATH" ] &&
108:     CLASSPATH=`cygpath --path --unix "$CLASSPATH"`
109: fi
110: 
111: # For Migwn, ensure paths are in UNIX format before anything is touched
112: if $mingw ; then
113:   [ -n "$M2_HOME" ] &&
114:     M2_HOME="`(cd "$M2_HOME"; pwd)`"
115:   [ -n "$JAVA_HOME" ] &&
116:     JAVA_HOME="`(cd "$JAVA_HOME"; pwd)`"
117:   # TODO classpath?
118: fi
119: 
120: if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ]; then
121:   javaExecutable="`which javac`"
122:   if [ -n "$javaExecutable" ] && ! [ "`expr \"$javaExecutable\" : '\([^ ]*\)'`" = "no" ]; then
123:     # readlink(1) is not available as standard on Solaris 10.
124:     readLink=`which readlink`
125:     if [ ! `expr "$readLink" : '\([^ ]*\)'` = "no" ]; then
126:       if $darwin ; then
127:         javaHome="`dirname \"$javaExecutable\"`"
128:         javaExecutable="`cd \"$javaHome\" && pwd -P`/javac"
129:       else
130:         javaExecutable="`readlink -f \"$javaExecutable\"`"
131:       fi
132:       javaHome="`dirname \"$javaExecutable\"`"
133:       javaHome=`expr "$javaHome" : '\(.*\)/bin'`
134:       JAVA_HOME="$javaHome"
135:       export JAVA_HOME
136:     fi
137:   fi
138: fi
139: 
140: if [ -z "$JAVACMD" ] ; then
141:   if [ -n "$JAVA_HOME"  ] ; then
142:     if [ -x "$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java" ] ; then
143:       # IBM's JDK on AIX uses strange locations for the executables
144:       JAVACMD="$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java"
145:     else
146:       JAVACMD="$JAVA_HOME/bin/java"
147:     fi
148:   else
149:     JAVACMD="`which java`"
150:   fi
151: fi
152: 
153: if [ ! -x "$JAVACMD" ] ; then
154:   echo "Error: JAVA_HOME is not defined correctly." >&2
155:   echo "  We cannot execute $JAVACMD" >&2
156:   exit 1
157: fi
158: 
159: if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ] ; then
160:   echo "Warning: JAVA_HOME environment variable is not set."
161: fi
162: 
163: CLASSWORLDS_LAUNCHER=org.codehaus.plexus.classworlds.launcher.Launcher
164: 
165: # traverses directory structure from process work directory to filesystem root
166: # first directory with .mvn subdirectory is considered project base directory
167: find_maven_basedir() {
168: 
169:   if [ -z "$1" ]
170:   then
171:     echo "Path not specified to find_maven_basedir"
172:     return 1
173:   fi
174: 
175:   basedir="$1"
176:   wdir="$1"
177:   while [ "$wdir" != '/' ] ; do
178:     if [ -d "$wdir"/.mvn ] ; then
179:       basedir=$wdir
180:       break
181:     fi
182:     # workaround for JBEAP-8937 (on Solaris 10/Sparc)
183:     if [ -d "${wdir}" ]; then
184:       wdir=`cd "$wdir/.."; pwd`
185:     fi
186:     # end of workaround
187:   done
188:   echo "${basedir}"
189: }
190: 
191: # concatenates all lines of a file
192: concat_lines() {
193:   if [ -f "$1" ]; then
194:     echo "$(tr -s '\n' ' ' < "$1")"
195:   fi
196: }
197: 
198: BASE_DIR=`find_maven_basedir "$(pwd)"`
199: if [ -z "$BASE_DIR" ]; then
200:   exit 1;
201: fi
202: 
203: export MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=${MAVEN_BASEDIR:-"$BASE_DIR"}
204: echo $MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR
205: MAVEN_OPTS="$(concat_lines "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR/.mvn/jvm.config") $MAVEN_OPTS"
206: 
207: # For Cygwin, switch paths to Windows format before running java
208: if $cygwin; then
209:   [ -n "$M2_HOME" ] &&
210:     M2_HOME=`cygpath --path --windows "$M2_HOME"`
211:   [ -n "$JAVA_HOME" ] &&
212:     JAVA_HOME=`cygpath --path --windows "$JAVA_HOME"`
213:   [ -n "$CLASSPATH" ] &&
214:     CLASSPATH=`cygpath --path --windows "$CLASSPATH"`
215:   [ -n "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR" ] &&
216:     MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=`cygpath --path --windows "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR"`
217: fi
218: 
219: WRAPPER_LAUNCHER=org.apache.maven.wrapper.MavenWrapperMain
220: 
221: exec "$JAVACMD" \
222:   $MAVEN_OPTS \
223:   -classpath "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR/.mvn/wrapper/maven-wrapper.jar" \
224:   "-Dmaven.home=${M2_HOME}" "-Dmaven.multiModuleProjectDirectory=${MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR}" \
225:   ${WRAPPER_LAUNCHER} $MAVEN_CONFIG "$@"

  1: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  2: @REM Licensed to the Apache Software Foundation (ASF) under one
  3: @REM or more contributor license agreements.  See the NOTICE file
  4: @REM distributed with this work for additional information
  5: @REM regarding copyright ownership.  The ASF licenses this file

  8: @REM with the License.  You may obtain a copy of the License at
  9: @REM
 10: @REM    http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
 11: @REM
 12: @REM Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing,
 13: @REM software distributed under the License is distributed on an
 14: @REM "AS IS" BASIS, WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY
 15: @REM KIND, either express or implied.  See the License for the
 16: @REM specific language governing permissions and limitations
 17: @REM under the License.
 18: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 19: 
 20: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 21: @REM Maven2 Start Up Batch script
 22: @REM
 23: @REM Required ENV vars:
 24: @REM JAVA_HOME - location of a JDK home dir
 25: @REM
 26: @REM Optional ENV vars
 27: @REM M2_HOME - location of maven2's installed home dir
 28: @REM MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO - set to 'on' to enable the echoing of the batch commands
 29: @REM MAVEN_BATCH_PAUSE - set to 'on' to wait for a key stroke before ending
 30: @REM MAVEN_OPTS - parameters passed to the Java VM when running Maven
 31: @REM     e.g. to debug Maven itself, use
 32: @REM set MAVEN_OPTS=-Xdebug -Xrunjdwp:transport=dt_socket,server=y,suspend=y,address=8000
 33: @REM MAVEN_SKIP_RC - flag to disable loading of mavenrc files
 34: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 35: 
 36: @REM Begin all REM lines with '@' in case MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO is 'on'
 37: @echo off
 38: @REM enable echoing my setting MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO to 'on'
 39: @if "%MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO%" == "on"  echo %MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO%
 40: 
 41: @REM set %HOME% to equivalent of $HOME
 42: if "%HOME%" == "" (set "HOME=%HOMEDRIVE%%HOMEPATH%")
 43: 
 44: @REM Execute a user defined script before this one
 45: if not "%MAVEN_SKIP_RC%" == "" goto skipRcPre
 46: @REM check for pre script, once with legacy .bat ending and once with .cmd ending
 47: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.bat" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.bat"
 48: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.cmd" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.cmd"
 49: :skipRcPre
 50: 
 51: @setlocal
 52: 
 53: set ERROR_CODE=0
 54: 
 55: @REM To isolate internal variables from possible post scripts, we use another setlocal
 56: @setlocal
 57: 
 58: @REM ==== START VALIDATION ====
 59: if not "%JAVA_HOME%" == "" goto OkJHome
 60: 
 61: echo.
 62: echo Error: JAVA_HOME not found in your environment. >&2
 63: echo Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the >&2
 64: echo location of your Java installation. >&2
 65: echo.
 66: goto error
 67: 
 68: :OkJHome
 69: if exist "%JAVA_HOME%\bin\java.exe" goto init
 70: 
 71: echo.
 72: echo Error: JAVA_HOME is set to an invalid directory. >&2
 73: echo JAVA_HOME = "%JAVA_HOME%" >&2
 74: echo Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the >&2
 75: echo location of your Java installation. >&2
 76: echo.
 77: goto error
 78: 
 79: @REM ==== END VALIDATION ====
 80: 
 81: :init
 82: 
 83: @REM Find the project base dir, i.e. the directory that contains the folder ".mvn".
 84: @REM Fallback to current working directory if not found.
 85: 
 86: set MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=%MAVEN_BASEDIR%
 87: IF NOT "%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%"=="" goto endDetectBaseDir
 88: 
 89: set EXEC_DIR=%CD%
 90: set WDIR=%EXEC_DIR%
 91: :findBaseDir
 92: IF EXIST "%WDIR%"\.mvn goto baseDirFound
 93: cd ..
 94: IF "%WDIR%"=="%CD%" goto baseDirNotFound
 95: set WDIR=%CD%
 96: goto findBaseDir
 97: 
 98: :baseDirFound
 99: set MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=%WDIR%
100: cd "%EXEC_DIR%"
101: goto endDetectBaseDir
102: 
103: :baseDirNotFound
104: set MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=%EXEC_DIR%
105: cd "%EXEC_DIR%"
106: 
107: :endDetectBaseDir
108: 
109: IF NOT EXIST "%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%\.mvn\jvm.config" goto endReadAdditionalConfig
110: 
111: @setlocal EnableExtensions EnableDelayedExpansion
112: for /F "usebackq delims=" %%a in ("%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%\.mvn\jvm.config") do set JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS=!JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS! %%a
113: @endlocal & set JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS=%JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS%
114: 
115: :endReadAdditionalConfig
116: 
117: SET MAVEN_JAVA_EXE="%JAVA_HOME%\bin\java.exe"
118: 
119: set WRAPPER_JAR="%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%\.mvn\wrapper\maven-wrapper.jar"
120: set WRAPPER_LAUNCHER=org.apache.maven.wrapper.MavenWrapperMain
121: 
122: %MAVEN_JAVA_EXE% %JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS% %MAVEN_OPTS% %MAVEN_DEBUG_OPTS% -classpath %WRAPPER_JAR% "-Dmaven.multiModuleProjectDirectory=%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%" %WRAPPER_LAUNCHER% %MAVEN_CONFIG% %*
123: if ERRORLEVEL 1 goto error
124: goto end
125: 
126: :error
127: set ERROR_CODE=1
128: 
129: :end
130: @endlocal & set ERROR_CODE=%ERROR_CODE%
131: 
132: if not "%MAVEN_SKIP_RC%" == "" goto skipRcPost
133: @REM check for post script, once with legacy .bat ending and once with .cmd ending
134: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.bat" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.bat"
135: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.cmd" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.cmd"
136: :skipRcPost
137: 
138: @REM pause the script if MAVEN_BATCH_PAUSE is set to 'on'
139: if "%MAVEN_BATCH_PAUSE%" == "on" pause
140: 
141: if "%MAVEN_TERMINATE_CMD%" == "on" exit %ERROR_CODE%
142: 
143: exit /B %ERROR_CODE%

File: authentication-server/src/main/java/com/project/warehouse/config/AuthServerAccountServiceBridgePipelineConfig.java
================
 1: package com.project.warehouse.config;
 2: import org.springframework.amqp.rabbit.core.RabbitTemplate;
 3: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 4: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
 5: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
 6: import org.springframework.integration.amqp.dsl.Amqp;
 7: import org.springframework.integration.channel.DirectChannel;
 8: import org.springframework.integration.dsl.IntegrationFlow;
 9: import org.springframework.integration.dsl.IntegrationFlows;
10: @Configuration
11: public class AuthServerAccountServiceBridgePipelineConfig {
12:     @Autowired
13:     private RabbitTemplate rabbitTemplate;
14:     @Bean
15:     public IntegrationFlow getUserDetailsIntegrationPipelineConfig(DirectChannel authServerAccountServiceBridgeInboundChannel,
16:                                                                    DirectChannel authServerAccountServiceBridgeOutboundChannel) {
17:         return IntegrationFlows.from(authServerAccountServiceBridgeInboundChannel)
18:                 .handle(Amqp.outboundGateway(rabbitTemplate)
19:                         .routingKey("authServerAccountServiceBridgeInboundQueue")
20:                         .returnChannel(authServerAccountServiceBridgeOutboundChannel))
21:                 .get();
22:     }
23: }

File: authentication-server/src/main/java/com/project/warehouse/config/MessagingConfig.java
================
 1: package com.project.warehouse.config;
 2: import org.springframework.amqp.core.Queue;
 3: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
 4: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
 5: import org.springframework.integration.channel.DirectChannel;
 6: import org.springframework.integration.dsl.channel.MessageChannels;
 7: @Configuration
 8: public class MessagingConfig {
 9:     @Bean("authServerAccountServiceBridgeInboundChannel")
10:     public DirectChannel authServerAccountServiceBridgeInboundChannel(){
11:         return MessageChannels.direct().get();
12:     }
13:     @Bean("authServerAccountServiceBridgeOutboundChannel")
14:     public DirectChannel authServerAccountServiceBridgeOutboundChannel(){
15:         return MessageChannels.direct().get();
16:     }
17:     @Bean("authServerAccountServiceBridgeInboundQueue")
18:     public Queue authServerAccountServiceBridgeInboundQueue(){
19:         return new Queue("authServerAccountServiceBridgeInboundQueue", false);
20:     }
21:     @Bean("authServerAccountServiceBridgeOutboundQueue")
22:     public Queue authServerAccountServiceBridgeOutboundQueue(){
23:         return new Queue("authServerAccountServiceBridgeOutboundQueue", false);
24:     }
25: }

File: authentication-server/src/main/java/com/project/warehouse/security/AccountUserDetailsService.java

 1: package com.project.warehouse.security;
 2: import com.project.warehouse.integration.LogInRequestGateway;
 3: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 4: import org.springframework.security.core.userdetails.UserDetails;
 5: import org.springframework.security.core.userdetails.UserDetailsService;
 6: import org.springframework.security.core.userdetails.UsernameNotFoundException;
 7: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
 8: @Service
 9: public class AccountUserDetailsService implements UserDetailsService {
