File: authentication-server/src/main/java/com/project/warehouse/security/LoginConfig.java
================
 1: package com.project.warehouse.security;
 2: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 3: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
 4: import org.springframework.core.annotation.Order;
 5: import org.springframework.security.authentication.AuthenticationManager;
 6: import org.springframework.security.config.annotation.authentication.builders.AuthenticationManagerBuilder;
 7: import org.springframework.security.config.annotation.web.builders.HttpSecurity;
 8: import org.springframework.security.config.annotation.web.configuration.WebSecurityConfigurerAdapter;
 9: @Configuration
10: @Order(-20)
11: public class LoginConfig extends WebSecurityConfigurerAdapter {
12:     @Autowired
13:     private AuthenticationManager authenticationManager;
14:     @Override
15:     protected void configure(HttpSecurity http) throws Exception {
16:         http.csrf().disable()
17:                 .formLogin().loginPage("/login").permitAll()
18:                 .and()
19:                 .requestMatchers().antMatchers("/account/userInfo","/login", "/oauth/authorize", "/oauth/confirm_access")
20:                 .and()
21:                 .authorizeRequests().anyRequest().authenticated();
22:     }
23:     @Override
24:     protected void configure(AuthenticationManagerBuilder auth) throws Exception {
25:         auth.parentAuthenticationManager(authenticationManager);
26:     }
27: }

================
File: authentication-server/src/main/java/com/project/warehouse/security/SecurityOAuth2AutorizationServerConfig.java
================
 1: package com.project.warehouse.security;
 2: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 3: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
 4: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
 5: import org.springframework.data.redis.connection.RedisConnectionFactory;
 6: import org.springframework.security.authentication.AuthenticationManager;
 7: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.config.annotation.configurers.ClientDetailsServiceConfigurer;
 8: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.config.annotation.web.configuration.AuthorizationServerConfigurerAdapter;
 9: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.config.annotation.web.configuration.EnableAuthorizationServer;
10: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.config.annotation.web.configurers.AuthorizationServerEndpointsConfigurer;
11: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.config.annotation.web.configurers.AuthorizationServerSecurityConfigurer;
12: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.provider.token.store.redis.JdkSerializationStrategy;
13: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.provider.token.store.redis.RedisTokenStore;
14: @Configuration
15: @EnableAuthorizationServer
16: public class SecurityOAuth2AutorizationServerConfig extends AuthorizationServerConfigurerAdapter {
17:     @Autowired
18:     private AuthenticationManager authenticationManager;
19:     @Autowired
20:     private AccountUserDetailsService accountUserDetailsService;
21:     @Autowired
22:     private RedisConnectionFactory redisConnectionFactory;
23:     @Override
24:     public void configure(AuthorizationServerEndpointsConfigurer endpoints) throws Exception {
25:         endpoints.authenticationManager(authenticationManager)
26:                 .approvalStoreDisabled()
27:                 .tokenStore(tokenStore())
28:                 .userDetailsService(accountUserDetailsService);
29:     }
30:     @Bean
31:     public RedisTokenStore tokenStore(){
32:         JdkSerializationStrategy jdkSerializationStrategy = new JdkSerializationStrategy();
33:         RedisTokenStore redisTokenStore = new RedisTokenStore(redisConnectionFactory);
34:         redisTokenStore.setSerializationStrategy(jdkSerializationStrategy);
35:         return redisTokenStore;
36:     }
37:     @Override
38:     public void configure(AuthorizationServerSecurityConfigurer oauthServer) throws Exception {
39:         oauthServer.tokenKeyAccess("permitAll()")
40:                 .checkTokenAccess("isAuthenticated()")
41:                 .allowFormAuthenticationForClients();
42:     }
43:     @Override
44:     public void configure(ClientDetailsServiceConfigurer clients) throws Exception {
45:         clients.inMemory()
46:                 .withClient("my-trusted-client")
47:                 .authorizedGrantTypes("client_credentials", "password", "authorization_code", "refresh_token", "implicit")
48:                 .authorities("ROLE_USER","ROLE_EMPLOYEE")
49:                 .scopes("read", "write", "trust","openid")
50:                 .resourceIds("oauth2-resource")
51:                 .autoApprove(true)
52:                 .accessTokenValiditySeconds(200);
53:     }
54: }

================
File: authentication-server/src/main/java/com/project/warehouse/security/SecurityOAuth2ResourceServerConfig.java
================
 1: package com.project.warehouse.security;
 2: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
 3: import org.springframework.security.config.annotation.web.builders.HttpSecurity;
 4: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.config.annotation.web.configuration.EnableResourceServer;
 5: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.config.annotation.web.configuration.ResourceServerConfigurerAdapter;
 6: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.provider.error.OAuth2AccessDeniedHandler;
 7: @Configuration
 8: @EnableResourceServer
 9: public class SecurityOAuth2ResourceServerConfig extends ResourceServerConfigurerAdapter {
10:     @Override
11:     public void configure(HttpSecurity http) throws Exception {
12:         http.authorizeRequests()
13:                 .antMatchers("/account/**").permitAll()
14:                 .anyRequest().authenticated()
15:                 .and().exceptionHandling().accessDeniedHandler(new OAuth2AccessDeniedHandler());
16:     }
17: }

================
File: config-server/mvnw
================
  1: #!/bin/sh
  2: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  3: # Licensed to the Apache Software Foundation (ASF) under one
  4: # or more contributor license agreements.  See the NOTICE file
  5: # distributed with this work for additional information
  6: # regarding copyright ownership.  The ASF licenses this file
  7: # to you under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the
  8: # "License"); you may not use this file except in compliance
  9: # with the License.  You may obtain a copy of the License at
 10: #
 11: #    http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
 12: #
 13: # Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing,
 14: # software distributed under the License is distributed on an
 15: # "AS IS" BASIS, WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY
 16: # KIND, either express or implied.  See the License for the
 17: # specific language governing permissions and limitations
 18: # under the License.
 19: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 20: 
 21: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 22: # Maven2 Start Up Batch script
 23: #
 24: # Required ENV vars:
 25: # ------------------
 26: #   JAVA_HOME - location of a JDK home dir
 27: #
 28: # Optional ENV vars
 29: # -----------------
 30: #   M2_HOME - location of maven2's installed home dir
 31: #   MAVEN_OPTS - parameters passed to the Java VM when running Maven
 32: #     e.g. to debug Maven itself, use
 33: #       set MAVEN_OPTS=-Xdebug -Xrunjdwp:transport=dt_socket,server=y,suspend=y,address=8000
 34: #   MAVEN_SKIP_RC - flag to disable loading of mavenrc files
 35: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 36: 
 37: if [ -z "$MAVEN_SKIP_RC" ] ; then
 38: 
 39:   if [ -f /etc/mavenrc ] ; then
 40:     . /etc/mavenrc
 41:   fi
 42: 
 43:   if [ -f "$HOME/.mavenrc" ] ; then
 44:     . "$HOME/.mavenrc"
 45:   fi
 46: 
 47: fi
 48: 
 49: # OS specific support.  $var _must_ be set to either true or false.
 50: cygwin=false;
 51: darwin=false;
 52: mingw=false
 53: case "`uname`" in
 54:   CYGWIN*) cygwin=true ;;
 55:   MINGW*) mingw=true;;
 56:   Darwin*) darwin=true
 57:     # Use /usr/libexec/java_home if available, otherwise fall back to /Library/Java/Home
 58:     # See https://developer.apple.com/library/mac/qa/qa1170/_index.html
 59:     if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ]; then
 60:       if [ -x "/usr/libexec/java_home" ]; then
 61:         export JAVA_HOME="`/usr/libexec/java_home`"
 62:       else
 63:         export JAVA_HOME="/Library/Java/Home"
 64:       fi
 65:     fi
 66:     ;;
 67: esac
 68: 
 69: if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ] ; then
 70:   if [ -r /etc/gentoo-release ] ; then
 71:     JAVA_HOME=`java-config --jre-home`
 72:   fi
 73: fi
 74: 
 75: if [ -z "$M2_HOME" ] ; then
 76:   ## resolve links - $0 may be a link to maven's home
 77:   PRG="$0"
 78: 
 79:   # need this for relative symlinks
 80:   while [ -h "$PRG" ] ; do
 81:     ls=`ls -ld "$PRG"`
 82:     link=`expr "$ls" : '.*-> \(.*\)$'`
 83:     if expr "$link" : '/.*' > /dev/null; then
 84:       PRG="$link"
 85:     else
 86:       PRG="`dirname "$PRG"`/$link"
 87:     fi
 88:   done
 89: 
 90:   saveddir=`pwd`
 91: 
 92:   M2_HOME=`dirname "$PRG"`/..
 93: 
 94:   # make it fully qualified
 95:   M2_HOME=`cd "$M2_HOME" && pwd`
 96: 
 97:   cd "$saveddir"
 98:   # echo Using m2 at $M2_HOME
 99: fi
100: 
101: # For Cygwin, ensure paths are in UNIX format before anything is touched
102: if $cygwin ; then
103:   [ -n "$M2_HOME" ] &&
104:     M2_HOME=`cygpath --unix "$M2_HOME"`
105:   [ -n "$JAVA_HOME" ] &&
106:     JAVA_HOME=`cygpath --unix "$JAVA_HOME"`
107:   [ -n "$CLASSPATH" ] &&
108:     CLASSPATH=`cygpath --path --unix "$CLASSPATH"`
109: fi
110: 
111: # For Migwn, ensure paths are in UNIX format before anything is touched
112: if $mingw ; then
113:   [ -n "$M2_HOME" ] &&
114:     M2_HOME="`(cd "$M2_HOME"; pwd)`"
115:   [ -n "$JAVA_HOME" ] &&
116:     JAVA_HOME="`(cd "$JAVA_HOME"; pwd)`"
117:   # TODO classpath?
118: fi
119: 
120: if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ]; then
121:   javaExecutable="`which javac`"
122:   if [ -n "$javaExecutable" ] && ! [ "`expr \"$javaExecutable\" : '\([^ ]*\)'`" = "no" ]; then
123:     # readlink(1) is not available as standard on Solaris 10.
124:     readLink=`which readlink`
125:     if [ ! `expr "$readLink" : '\([^ ]*\)'` = "no" ]; then
126:       if $darwin ; then
127:         javaHome="`dirname \"$javaExecutable\"`"
128:         javaExecutable="`cd \"$javaHome\" && pwd -P`/javac"
129:       else
130:         javaExecutable="`readlink -f \"$javaExecutable\"`"
131:       fi
132:       javaHome="`dirname \"$javaExecutable\"`"
133:       javaHome=`expr "$javaHome" : '\(.*\)/bin'`
134:       JAVA_HOME="$javaHome"
135:       export JAVA_HOME
136:     fi
137:   fi
138: fi
139: 
140: if [ -z "$JAVACMD" ] ; then
141:   if [ -n "$JAVA_HOME"  ] ; then
142:     if [ -x "$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java" ] ; then
143:       # IBM's JDK on AIX uses strange locations for the executables
144:       JAVACMD="$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java"
145:     else
146:       JAVACMD="$JAVA_HOME/bin/java"
147:     fi
148:   else
149:     JAVACMD="`which java`"
150:   fi
151: fi
152: 
153: if [ ! -x "$JAVACMD" ] ; then
154:   echo "Error: JAVA_HOME is not defined correctly." >&2
155:   echo "  We cannot execute $JAVACMD" >&2
156:   exit 1
157: fi
158: 
159: if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ] ; then
160:   echo "Warning: JAVA_HOME environment variable is not set."
161: fi
162: 
163: CLASSWORLDS_LAUNCHER=org.codehaus.plexus.classworlds.launcher.Launcher
164: 
165: # traverses directory structure from process work directory to filesystem root
166: # first directory with .mvn subdirectory is considered project base directory
167: find_maven_basedir() {
168: 
169:   if [ -z "$1" ]
170:   then
171:     echo "Path not specified to find_maven_basedir"
172:     return 1
173:   fi
174: 
175:   basedir="$1"
176:   wdir="$1"
177:   while [ "$wdir" != '/' ] ; do
178:     if [ -d "$wdir"/.mvn ] ; then
179:       basedir=$wdir
180:       break
181:     fi
182:     # workaround for JBEAP-8937 (on Solaris 10/Sparc)
183:     if [ -d "${wdir}" ]; then
184:       wdir=`cd "$wdir/.."; pwd`
185:     fi
186:     # end of workaround
187:   done
188:   echo "${basedir}"
189: }
190: 
191: # concatenates all lines of a file
192: concat_lines() {
193:   if [ -f "$1" ]; then
194:     echo "$(tr -s '\n' ' ' < "$1")"
195:   fi
196: }
197: 
198: BASE_DIR=`find_maven_basedir "$(pwd)"`
199: if [ -z "$BASE_DIR" ]; then
200:   exit 1;
201: fi
202: 
203: export MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=${MAVEN_BASEDIR:-"$BASE_DIR"}
204: echo $MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR
205: MAVEN_OPTS="$(concat_lines "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR/.mvn/jvm.config") $MAVEN_OPTS"
206: 
207: # For Cygwin, switch paths to Windows format before running java
208: if $cygwin; then
209:   [ -n "$M2_HOME" ] &&
210:     M2_HOME=`cygpath --path --windows "$M2_HOME"`
211:   [ -n "$JAVA_HOME" ] &&
212:     JAVA_HOME=`cygpath --path --windows "$JAVA_HOME"`
213:   [ -n "$CLASSPATH" ] &&
214:     CLASSPATH=`cygpath --path --windows "$CLASSPATH"`
215:   [ -n "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR" ] &&
216:     MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=`cygpath --path --windows "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR"`
217: fi
218: 
219: WRAPPER_LAUNCHER=org.apache.maven.wrapper.MavenWrapperMain
220: 
221: exec "$JAVACMD" \
222:   $MAVEN_OPTS \
223:   -classpath "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR/.mvn/wrapper/maven-wrapper.jar" \
224:   "-Dmaven.home=${M2_HOME}" "-Dmaven.multiModuleProjectDirectory=${MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR}" \
225:   ${WRAPPER_LAUNCHER} $MAVEN_CONFIG "$@"

================
File: config-server/mvnw.cmd
================
  1: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  2: @REM Licensed to the Apache Software Foundation (ASF) under one
  3: @REM or more contributor license agreements.  See the NOTICE file
  4: @REM distributed with this work for additional information
  5: @REM regarding copyright ownership.  The ASF licenses this file
  6: @REM to you under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the
  7: @REM "License"); you may not use this file except in compliance
  8: @REM with the License.  You may obtain a copy of the License at
  9: @REM
 10: @REM    http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
 11: @REM
 12: @REM Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing,
 13: @REM software distributed under the License is distributed on an
 14: @REM "AS IS" BASIS, WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY
 15: @REM KIND, either express or implied.  See the License for the
 16: @REM specific language governing permissions and limitations
 17: @REM under the License.
 18: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 19: 
 20: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 21: @REM Maven2 Start Up Batch script
 22: @REM
 23: @REM Required ENV vars:
 24: @REM JAVA_HOME - location of a JDK home dir
 25: @REM
 26: @REM Optional ENV vars
 27: @REM M2_HOME - location of maven2's installed home dir
 28: @REM MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO - set to 'on' to enable the echoing of the batch commands
 29: @REM MAVEN_BATCH_PAUSE - set to 'on' to wait for a key stroke before ending
 30: @REM MAVEN_OPTS - parameters passed to the Java VM when running Maven
 31: @REM     e.g. to debug Maven itself, use
 32: @REM set MAVEN_OPTS=-Xdebug -Xrunjdwp:transport=dt_socket,server=y,suspend=y,address=8000
 33: @REM MAVEN_SKIP_RC - flag to disable loading of mavenrc files
 34: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 35: 
 36: @REM Begin all REM lines with '@' in case MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO is 'on'
 37: @echo off
 38: @REM enable echoing my setting MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO to 'on'
 39: @if "%MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO%" == "on"  echo %MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO%
 40: 
 41: @REM set %HOME% to equivalent of $HOME
 42: if "%HOME%" == "" (set "HOME=%HOMEDRIVE%%HOMEPATH%")
 43: 
 44: @REM Execute a user defined script before this one
 45: if not "%MAVEN_SKIP_RC%" == "" goto skipRcPre
 46: @REM check for pre script, once with legacy .bat ending and once with .cmd ending
 47: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.bat" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.bat"
 48: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.cmd" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.cmd"
 49: :skipRcPre
 50: 
 51: @setlocal
 52: 
 53: set ERROR_CODE=0
 54: 
 55: @REM To isolate internal variables from possible post scripts, we use another setlocal
 56: @setlocal
 57: 
 58: @REM ==== START VALIDATION ====
 59: if not "%JAVA_HOME%" == "" goto OkJHome
 60: 
 61: echo.
 62: echo Error: JAVA_HOME not found in your environment. >&2
 63: echo Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the >&2
 64: echo location of your Java installation. >&2
 65: echo.
 66: goto error
 67: 
 68: :OkJHome
 69: if exist "%JAVA_HOME%\bin\java.exe" goto init
 70: 
 71: echo.
 72: echo Error: JAVA_HOME is set to an invalid directory. >&2
 73: echo JAVA_HOME = "%JAVA_HOME%" >&2
 74: echo Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the >&2
 75: echo location of your Java installation. >&2
 76: echo.
 77: goto error
 78: 
 79: @REM ==== END VALIDATION ====
 80: 
 81: :init
 82: 
 83: @REM Find the project base dir, i.e. the directory that contains the folder ".mvn".
 84: @REM Fallback to current working directory if not found.
 85: 
 86: set MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=%MAVEN_BASEDIR%
 87: IF NOT "%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%"=="" goto endDetectBaseDir
 88: 
 89: set EXEC_DIR=%CD%
 90: set WDIR=%EXEC_DIR%
 91: :findBaseDir
 92: IF EXIST "%WDIR%"\.mvn goto baseDirFound
 93: cd ..
 94: IF "%WDIR%"=="%CD%" goto baseDirNotFound
 95: set WDIR=%CD%
 96: goto findBaseDir
 97: 
 98: :baseDirFound
 99: set MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=%WDIR%
100: cd "%EXEC_DIR%"
101: goto endDetectBaseDir
102: 
103: :baseDirNotFound
104: set MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=%EXEC_DIR%
105: cd "%EXEC_DIR%"
106: 
107: :endDetectBaseDir
108: 
109: IF NOT EXIST "%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%\.mvn\jvm.config" goto endReadAdditionalConfig
110: 
111: @setlocal EnableExtensions EnableDelayedExpansion
112: for /F "usebackq delims=" %%a in ("%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%\.mvn\jvm.config") do set JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS=!JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS! %%a
113: @endlocal & set JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS=%JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS%
114: 
115: :endReadAdditionalConfig
116: 
117: SET MAVEN_JAVA_EXE="%JAVA_HOME%\bin\java.exe"
118: 
119: set WRAPPER_JAR="%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%\.mvn\wrapper\maven-wrapper.jar"
120: set WRAPPER_LAUNCHER=org.apache.maven.wrapper.MavenWrapperMain
121: 
122: %MAVEN_JAVA_EXE% %JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS% %MAVEN_OPTS% %MAVEN_DEBUG_OPTS% -classpath %WRAPPER_JAR% "-Dmaven.multiModuleProjectDirectory=%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%" %WRAPPER_LAUNCHER% %MAVEN_CONFIG% %*
123: if ERRORLEVEL 1 goto error
124: goto end
125: 
126: :error
127: set ERROR_CODE=1
128: 
129: :end
130: @endlocal & set ERROR_CODE=%ERROR_CODE%
131: 
132: if not "%MAVEN_SKIP_RC%" == "" goto skipRcPost
133: @REM check for post script, once with legacy .bat ending and once with .cmd ending
134: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.bat" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.bat"
135: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.cmd" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.cmd"
136: :skipRcPost
137: 
138: @REM pause the script if MAVEN_BATCH_PAUSE is set to 'on'
139: if "%MAVEN_BATCH_PAUSE%" == "on" pause
140: 
141: if "%MAVEN_TERMINATE_CMD%" == "on" exit %ERROR_CODE%
142: 
143: exit /B %ERROR_CODE%

65: 				<artifactId>spring-boot-maven-plugin</artifactId>
66: 			</plugin>
67: 		</plugins>
68: 	</build>
69: </project>

================
File: eureka-server/mvnw
================
  1: #!/bin/sh
  2: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  3: # Licensed to the Apache Software Foundation (ASF) under one
  4: # or more contributor license agreements.  See the NOTICE file
  5: # distributed with this work for additional information
  6: # regarding copyright ownership.  The ASF licenses this file
  7: # to you under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the
  8: # "License"); you may not use this file except in compliance
  9: # with the License.  You may obtain a copy of the License at
 10: #
 11: #    http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
 12: #
 13: # Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing,
 14: # software distributed under the License is distributed on an
 15: # "AS IS" BASIS, WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY
 16: # KIND, either express or implied.  See the License for the
 17: # specific language governing permissions and limitations
 18: # under the License.
 19: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 20: 
 21: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 22: # Maven2 Start Up Batch script
 23: #
 24: # Required ENV vars:
 25: # ------------------
 26: #   JAVA_HOME - location of a JDK home dir
 27: #
 28: # Optional ENV vars
 29: # -----------------
 30: #   M2_HOME - location of maven2's installed home dir
 31: #   MAVEN_OPTS - parameters passed to the Java VM when running Maven
 32: #     e.g. to debug Maven itself, use
 33: #       set MAVEN_OPTS=-Xdebug -Xrunjdwp:transport=dt_socket,server=y,suspend=y,address=8000
 34: #   MAVEN_SKIP_RC - flag to disable loading of mavenrc files
 35: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 36: 
 37: if [ -z "$MAVEN_SKIP_RC" ] ; then
 38: 
 39:   if [ -f /etc/mavenrc ] ; then
 40:     . /etc/mavenrc
 41:   fi
 42: 
 43:   if [ -f "$HOME/.mavenrc" ] ; then
 44:     . "$HOME/.mavenrc"
 45:   fi
 46: 
 47: fi
 48: 
 49: # OS specific support.  $var _must_ be set to either true or false.
 50: cygwin=false;
 51: darwin=false;
 52: mingw=false
 53: case "`uname`" in
 54:   CYGWIN*) cygwin=true ;;
 55:   MINGW*) mingw=true;;
 56:   Darwin*) darwin=true
 57:     # Use /usr/libexec/java_home if available, otherwise fall back to /Library/Java/Home
 58:     # See https://developer.apple.com/library/mac/qa/qa1170/_index.html
 59:     if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ]; then
 60:       if [ -x "/usr/libexec/java_home" ]; then
 61:         export JAVA_HOME="`/usr/libexec/java_home`"
 62:       else
 63:         export JAVA_HOME="/Library/Java/Home"
 64:       fi
 65:     fi
 66:     ;;
 67: esac
 68: 
 69: if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ] ; then
 70:   if [ -r /etc/gentoo-release ] ; then
 71:     JAVA_HOME=`java-config --jre-home`
 72:   fi
 73: fi
 74: 
 75: if [ -z "$M2_HOME" ] ; then
 76:   ## resolve links - $0 may be a link to maven's home
 77:   PRG="$0"
 78: 
 79:   # need this for relative symlinks
 80:   while [ -h "$PRG" ] ; do
 81:     ls=`ls -ld "$PRG"`
 82:     link=`expr "$ls" : '.*-> \(.*\)$'`
 83:     if expr "$link" : '/.*' > /dev/null; then
 84:       PRG="$link"
 85:     else
 86:       PRG="`dirname "$PRG"`/$link"
 87:     fi
 88:   done
 89: 
 90:   saveddir=`pwd`
 91: 
 92:   M2_HOME=`dirname "$PRG"`/..
 93: 
 94:   # make it fully qualified
 95:   M2_HOME=`cd "$M2_HOME" && pwd`
 96: 
 97:   cd "$saveddir"
 98:   # echo Using m2 at $M2_HOME
 99: fi
100: 
101: # For Cygwin, ensure paths are in UNIX format before anything is touched
102: if $cygwin ; then
103:   [ -n "$M2_HOME" ] &&
104:     M2_HOME=`cygpath --unix "$M2_HOME"`
105:   [ -n "$JAVA_HOME" ] &&
106:     JAVA_HOME=`cygpath --unix "$JAVA_HOME"`
107:   [ -n "$CLASSPATH" ] &&
108:     CLASSPATH=`cygpath --path --unix "$CLASSPATH"`
109: fi
110: 
111: # For Migwn, ensure paths are in UNIX format before anything is touched
112: if $mingw ; then
113:   [ -n "$M2_HOME" ] &&
114:     M2_HOME="`(cd "$M2_HOME"; pwd)`"
115:   [ -n "$JAVA_HOME" ] &&
116:     JAVA_HOME="`(cd "$JAVA_HOME"; pwd)`"
117:   # TODO classpath?
118: fi
119: 
120: if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ]; then
121:   javaExecutable="`which javac`"
122:   if [ -n "$javaExecutable" ] && ! [ "`expr \"$javaExecutable\" : '\([^ ]*\)'`" = "no" ]; then
123:     # readlink(1) is not available as standard on Solaris 10.
124:     readLink=`which readlink`
125:     if [ ! `expr "$readLink" : '\([^ ]*\)'` = "no" ]; then
126:       if $darwin ; then
127:         javaHome="`dirname \"$javaExecutable\"`"
128:         javaExecutable="`cd \"$javaHome\" && pwd -P`/javac"
129:       else
130:         javaExecutable="`readlink -f \"$javaExecutable\"`"
131:       fi
132:       javaHome="`dirname \"$javaExecutable\"`"
133:       javaHome=`expr "$javaHome" : '\(.*\)/bin'`
134:       JAVA_HOME="$javaHome"
135:       export JAVA_HOME
136:     fi
137:   fi
138: fi
139: 
140: if [ -z "$JAVACMD" ] ; then
141:   if [ -n "$JAVA_HOME"  ] ; then
142:     if [ -x "$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java" ] ; then
143:       # IBM's JDK on AIX uses strange locations for the executables
144:       JAVACMD="$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java"
145:     else
146:       JAVACMD="$JAVA_HOME/bin/java"
147:     fi
148:   else
149:     JAVACMD="`which java`"
150:   fi
151: fi
152: 
153: if [ ! -x "$JAVACMD" ] ; then
154:   echo "Error: JAVA_HOME is not defined correctly." >&2
155:   echo "  We cannot execute $JAVACMD" >&2
156:   exit 1
157: fi
158: 
159: if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ] ; then
160:   echo "Warning: JAVA_HOME environment variable is not set."
161: fi
162: 
163: CLASSWORLDS_LAUNCHER=org.codehaus.plexus.classworlds.launcher.Launcher
164: 
165: # traverses directory structure from process work directory to filesystem root
166: # first directory with .mvn subdirectory is considered project base directory
167: find_maven_basedir() {
168: 
169:   if [ -z "$1" ]
170:   then
171:     echo "Path not specified to find_maven_basedir"
172:     return 1
173:   fi
174: 
175:   basedir="$1"
176:   wdir="$1"
177:   while [ "$wdir" != '/' ] ; do
178:     if [ -d "$wdir"/.mvn ] ; then
179:       basedir=$wdir
180:       break
181:     fi
182:     # workaround for JBEAP-8937 (on Solaris 10/Sparc)
183:     if [ -d "${wdir}" ]; then
184:       wdir=`cd "$wdir/.."; pwd`
185:     fi
186:     # end of workaround
187:   done
188:   echo "${basedir}"
189: }
190: 
191: # concatenates all lines of a file
192: concat_lines() {
193:   if [ -f "$1" ]; then
194:     echo "$(tr -s '\n' ' ' < "$1")"
195:   fi
196: }
197: 
198: BASE_DIR=`find_maven_basedir "$(pwd)"`
199: if [ -z "$BASE_DIR" ]; then
200:   exit 1;
201: fi
202: 
203: export MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=${MAVEN_BASEDIR:-"$BASE_DIR"}
204: echo $MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR
205: MAVEN_OPTS="$(concat_lines "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR/.mvn/jvm.config") $MAVEN_OPTS"
206: 
207: # For Cygwin, switch paths to Windows format before running java
208: if $cygwin; then
209:   [ -n "$M2_HOME" ] &&
210:     M2_HOME=`cygpath --path --windows "$M2_HOME"`
211:   [ -n "$JAVA_HOME" ] &&
212:     JAVA_HOME=`cygpath --path --windows "$JAVA_HOME"`
213:   [ -n "$CLASSPATH" ] &&
214:     CLASSPATH=`cygpath --path --windows "$CLASSPATH"`
215:   [ -n "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR" ] &&
216:     MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=`cygpath --path --windows "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR"`
217: fi
218: 
219: WRAPPER_LAUNCHER=org.apache.maven.wrapper.MavenWrapperMain
220: 
221: exec "$JAVACMD" \
222:   $MAVEN_OPTS \
223:   -classpath "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR/.mvn/wrapper/maven-wrapper.jar" \
224:   "-Dmaven.home=${M2_HOME}" "-Dmaven.multiModuleProjectDirectory=${MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR}" \
225:   ${WRAPPER_LAUNCHER} $MAVEN_CONFIG "$@"

================
File: eureka-server/mvnw.cmd
================
  1: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  2: @REM Licensed to the Apache Software Foundation (ASF) under one
  3: @REM or more contributor license agreements.  See the NOTICE file
  4: @REM distributed with this work for additional information
  5: @REM regarding copyright ownership.  The ASF licenses this file
  6: @REM to you under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the
  7: @REM "License"); you may not use this file except in compliance
  8: @REM with the License.  You may obtain a copy of the License at
  9: @REM
 10: @REM    http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
 11: @REM
 12: @REM Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing,
 13: @REM software distributed under the License is distributed on an
 14: @REM "AS IS" BASIS, WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY
 15: @REM KIND, either express or implied.  See the License for the
 16: @REM specific language governing permissions and limitations
 17: @REM under the License.
 18: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 19: 
 20: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 21: @REM Maven2 Start Up Batch script
 22: @REM
 23: @REM Required ENV vars:
 24: @REM JAVA_HOME - location of a JDK home dir
 25: @REM
 26: @REM Optional ENV vars
 27: @REM M2_HOME - location of maven2's installed home dir
 28: @REM MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO - set to 'on' to enable the echoing of the batch commands
 29: @REM MAVEN_BATCH_PAUSE - set to 'on' to wait for a key stroke before ending
 30: @REM MAVEN_OPTS - parameters passed to the Java VM when running Maven
 31: @REM     e.g. to debug Maven itself, use
 32: @REM set MAVEN_OPTS=-Xdebug -Xrunjdwp:transport=dt_socket,server=y,suspend=y,address=8000
 33: @REM MAVEN_SKIP_RC - flag to disable loading of mavenrc files
 34: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 35: 
 36: @REM Begin all REM lines with '@' in case MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO is 'on'
 37: @echo off
 38: @REM enable echoing my setting MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO to 'on'
 39: @if "%MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO%" == "on"  echo %MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO%
 40: 
 41: @REM set %HOME% to equivalent of $HOME
 42: if "%HOME%" == "" (set "HOME=%HOMEDRIVE%%HOMEPATH%")
 43: 
 44: @REM Execute a user defined script before this one
 45: if not "%MAVEN_SKIP_RC%" == "" goto skipRcPre
 46: @REM check for pre script, once with legacy .bat ending and once with .cmd ending
 47: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.bat" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.bat"
 48: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.cmd" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.cmd"
 49: :skipRcPre
 50: 
 51: @setlocal
 52: 
 53: set ERROR_CODE=0
 54: 
 55: @REM To isolate internal variables from possible post scripts, we use another setlocal
 56: @setlocal
 57: 
 58: @REM ==== START VALIDATION ====
 59: if not "%JAVA_HOME%" == "" goto OkJHome
 60: 
 61: echo.
 62: echo Error: JAVA_HOME not found in your environment. >&2
 63: echo Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the >&2
 64: echo location of your Java installation. >&2
 65: echo.
 66: goto error
 67: 
 68: :OkJHome
 69: if exist "%JAVA_HOME%\bin\java.exe" goto init
 70: 
 71: echo.
 72: echo Error: JAVA_HOME is set to an invalid directory. >&2
 73: echo JAVA_HOME = "%JAVA_HOME%" >&2
 74: echo Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the >&2
 75: echo location of your Java installation. >&2
 76: echo.
 77: goto error
 78: 
 79: @REM ==== END VALIDATION ====
 80: 
 81: :init
 82: 
 83: @REM Find the project base dir, i.e. the directory that contains the folder ".mvn".
 84: @REM Fallback to current working directory if not found.
 85: 
 86: set MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=%MAVEN_BASEDIR%
 87: IF NOT "%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%"=="" goto endDetectBaseDir
 88: 
 89: set EXEC_DIR=%CD%
 90: set WDIR=%EXEC_DIR%
 91: :findBaseDir
 92: IF EXIST "%WDIR%"\.mvn goto baseDirFound
 93: cd ..
 94: IF "%WDIR%"=="%CD%" goto baseDirNotFound
 95: set WDIR=%CD%
 96: goto findBaseDir
 97: 
 98: :baseDirFound
 99: set MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=%WDIR%
100: cd "%EXEC_DIR%"
101: goto endDetectBaseDir
102: 
103: :baseDirNotFound
104: set MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=%EXEC_DIR%
105: cd "%EXEC_DIR%"
106: 
107: :endDetectBaseDir
108: 
109: IF NOT EXIST "%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%\.mvn\jvm.config" goto endReadAdditionalConfig
110: 
111: @setlocal EnableExtensions EnableDelayedExpansion
112: for /F "usebackq delims=" %%a in ("%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%\.mvn\jvm.config") do set JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS=!JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS! %%a
113: @endlocal & set JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS=%JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS%
114: 
115: :endReadAdditionalConfig
116: 
117: SET MAVEN_JAVA_EXE="%JAVA_HOME%\bin\java.exe"
118: 
119: set WRAPPER_JAR="%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%\.mvn\wrapper\maven-wrapper.jar"
120: set WRAPPER_LAUNCHER=org.apache.maven.wrapper.MavenWrapperMain
121: 
122: %MAVEN_JAVA_EXE% %JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS% %MAVEN_OPTS% %MAVEN_DEBUG_OPTS% -classpath %WRAPPER_JAR% "-Dmaven.multiModuleProjectDirectory=%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%" %WRAPPER_LAUNCHER% %MAVEN_CONFIG% %*
123: if ERRORLEVEL 1 goto error
124: goto end
125: 
126: :error
127: set ERROR_CODE=1
128: 
129: :end
130: @endlocal & set ERROR_CODE=%ERROR_CODE%
131: 
132: if not "%MAVEN_SKIP_RC%" == "" goto skipRcPost
133: @REM check for post script, once with legacy .bat ending and once with .cmd ending
134: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.bat" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.bat"
135: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.cmd" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.cmd"
136: :skipRcPost
137: 
138: @REM pause the script if MAVEN_BATCH_PAUSE is set to 'on'
139: if "%MAVEN_BATCH_PAUSE%" == "on" pause
140: 
141: if "%MAVEN_TERMINATE_CMD%" == "on" exit %ERROR_CODE%
142: 
143: exit /B %ERROR_CODE%

  1: #!/bin/sh
  2: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  3: # Licensed to the Apache Software Foundation (ASF) under one
  4: # or more contributor license agreements.  See the NOTICE file
  5: # distributed with this work for additional information
  6: # regarding copyright ownership.  The ASF licenses this file
  7: # to you under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the
  8: # "License"); you may not use this file except in compliance
  9: # with the License.  You may obtain a copy of the License at
 10: #
 11: #    http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
 12: #
 13: # Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing,
 14: # software distributed under the License is distributed on an
 15: # "AS IS" BASIS, WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY
 16: # KIND, either express or implied.  See the License for the
 17: # specific language governing permissions and limitations
 18: # under the License.
 19: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 20: 
 21: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 22: # Maven2 Start Up Batch script
 23: #
 24: # Required ENV vars:
 25: # ------------------
 26: #   JAVA_HOME - location of a JDK home dir
 27: #
 28: # Optional ENV vars
 29: # -----------------
 30: #   M2_HOME - location of maven2's installed home dir
 31: #   MAVEN_OPTS - parameters passed to the Java VM when running Maven
 32: #     e.g. to debug Maven itself, use
 33: #       set MAVEN_OPTS=-Xdebug -Xrunjdwp:transport=dt_socket,server=y,suspend=y,address=8000
 34: #   MAVEN_SKIP_RC - flag to disable loading of mavenrc files
 35: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 36: 
 37: if [ -z "$MAVEN_SKIP_RC" ] ; then
 38: 
 39:   if [ -f /etc/mavenrc ] ; then
 40:     . /etc/mavenrc
 41:   fi
 42: 
 43:   if [ -f "$HOME/.mavenrc" ] ; then
 44:     . "$HOME/.mavenrc"
 45:   fi
 46: 
 47: fi
 48: 
 49: # OS specific support.  $var _must_ be set to either true or false.
 50: cygwin=false;
 51: darwin=false;
 52: mingw=false
 53: case "`uname`" in
 54:   CYGWIN*) cygwin=true ;;
 55:   MINGW*) mingw=true;;
 56:   Darwin*) darwin=true
 57:     # Use /usr/libexec/java_home if available, otherwise fall back to /Library/Java/Home
 58:     # See https://developer.apple.com/library/mac/qa/qa1170/_index.html
 59:     if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ]; then
 60:       if [ -x "/usr/libexec/java_home" ]; then
 61:         export JAVA_HOME="`/usr/libexec/java_home`"
 62:       else
 63:         export JAVA_HOME="/Library/Java/Home"
 64:       fi
 65:     fi
 66:     ;;
 67: esac
 68: 
 69: if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ] ; then
 70:   if [ -r /etc/gentoo-release ] ; then
 71:     JAVA_HOME=`java-config --jre-home`
 72:   fi
 73: fi
 74: 
 75: if [ -z "$M2_HOME" ] ; then
 76:   ## resolve links - $0 may be a link to maven's home
 77:   PRG="$0"
 78: 
 79:   # need this for relative symlinks
 80:   while [ -h "$PRG" ] ; do
 81:     ls=`ls -ld "$PRG"`
 82:     link=`expr "$ls" : '.*-> \(.*\)$'`
 83:     if expr "$link" : '/.*' > /dev/null; then
 84:       PRG="$link"
 85:     else
 86:       PRG="`dirname "$PRG"`/$link"
 87:     fi
 88:   done
 89: 
 90:   saveddir=`pwd`
 91: 
 92:   M2_HOME=`dirname "$PRG"`/..
 93: 
 94:   # make it fully qualified
 95:   M2_HOME=`cd "$M2_HOME" && pwd`
 96: 
 97:   cd "$saveddir"
 98:   # echo Using m2 at $M2_HOME
 99: fi
100: 
101: # For Cygwin, ensure paths are in UNIX format before anything is touched
102: if $cygwin ; then
103:   [ -n "$M2_HOME" ] &&
104:     M2_HOME=`cygpath --unix "$M2_HOME"`
105:   [ -n "$JAVA_HOME" ] &&
106:     JAVA_HOME=`cygpath --unix "$JAVA_HOME"`
107:   [ -n "$CLASSPATH" ] &&
108:     CLASSPATH=`cygpath --path --unix "$CLASSPATH"`
109: fi
110: 
111: # For Migwn, ensure paths are in UNIX format before anything is touched
112: if $mingw ; then
113:   [ -n "$M2_HOME" ] &&
114:     M2_HOME="`(cd "$M2_HOME"; pwd)`"
115:   [ -n "$JAVA_HOME" ] &&
116:     JAVA_HOME="`(cd "$JAVA_HOME"; pwd)`"
117:   # TODO classpath?
118: fi
119: 
120: if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ]; then
121:   javaExecutable="`which javac`"
122:   if [ -n "$javaExecutable" ] && ! [ "`expr \"$javaExecutable\" : '\([^ ]*\)'`" = "no" ]; then
123:     # readlink(1) is not available as standard on Solaris 10.
124:     readLink=`which readlink`
125:     if [ ! `expr "$readLink" : '\([^ ]*\)'` = "no" ]; then
126:       if $darwin ; then
127:         javaHome="`dirname \"$javaExecutable\"`"
128:         javaExecutable="`cd \"$javaHome\" && pwd -P`/javac"
129:       else
130:         javaExecutable="`readlink -f \"$javaExecutable\"`"
131:       fi
132:       javaHome="`dirname \"$javaExecutable\"`"
133:       javaHome=`expr "$javaHome" : '\(.*\)/bin'`
134:       JAVA_HOME="$javaHome"
135:       export JAVA_HOME
136:     fi
137:   fi
138: fi
139: 
140: if [ -z "$JAVACMD" ] ; then
141:   if [ -n "$JAVA_HOME"  ] ; then
142:     if [ -x "$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java" ] ; then
143:       # IBM's JDK on AIX uses strange locations for the executables
144:       JAVACMD="$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java"
145:     else
146:       JAVACMD="$JAVA_HOME/bin/java"
147:     fi
148:   else
149:     JAVACMD="`which java`"
150:   fi
151: fi
152: 
153: if [ ! -x "$JAVACMD" ] ; then
154:   echo "Error: JAVA_HOME is not defined correctly." >&2
155:   echo "  We cannot execute $JAVACMD" >&2
156:   exit 1
157: fi
158: 
159: if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ] ; then
160:   echo "Warning: JAVA_HOME environment variable is not set."
161: fi
162: 
163: CLASSWORLDS_LAUNCHER=org.codehaus.plexus.classworlds.launcher.Launcher
164: 
165: # traverses directory structure from process work directory to filesystem root
166: # first directory with .mvn subdirectory is considered project base directory
167: find_maven_basedir() {
168: 
169:   if [ -z "$1" ]
170:   then
171:     echo "Path not specified to find_maven_basedir"
172:     return 1
173:   fi
174: 
175:   basedir="$1"
176:   wdir="$1"
177:   while [ "$wdir" != '/' ] ; do
178:     if [ -d "$wdir"/.mvn ] ; then
179:       basedir=$wdir
180:       break
181:     fi
182:     # workaround for JBEAP-8937 (on Solaris 10/Sparc)
183:     if [ -d "${wdir}" ]; then
184:       wdir=`cd "$wdir/.."; pwd`
185:     fi
186:     # end of workaround
187:   done
188:   echo "${basedir}"
189: }
190: 
191: # concatenates all lines of a file
192: concat_lines() {
193:   if [ -f "$1" ]; then
194:     echo "$(tr -s '\n' ' ' < "$1")"
195:   fi
196: }
197: 
198: BASE_DIR=`find_maven_basedir "$(pwd)"`
199: if [ -z "$BASE_DIR" ]; then
200:   exit 1;
201: fi
202: 
203: export MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=${MAVEN_BASEDIR:-"$BASE_DIR"}
204: echo $MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR
205: MAVEN_OPTS="$(concat_lines "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR/.mvn/jvm.config") $MAVEN_OPTS"
206: 
207: # For Cygwin, switch paths to Windows format before running java
208: if $cygwin; then
209:   [ -n "$M2_HOME" ] &&
210:     M2_HOME=`cygpath --path --windows "$M2_HOME"`
211:   [ -n "$JAVA_HOME" ] &&
212:     JAVA_HOME=`cygpath --path --windows "$JAVA_HOME"`
213:   [ -n "$CLASSPATH" ] &&
214:     CLASSPATH=`cygpath --path --windows "$CLASSPATH"`
215:   [ -n "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR" ] &&
216:     MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=`cygpath --path --windows "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR"`
217: fi
218: 
219: WRAPPER_LAUNCHER=org.apache.maven.wrapper.MavenWrapperMain
220: 
221: exec "$JAVACMD" \
222:   $MAVEN_OPTS \
223:   -classpath "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR/.mvn/wrapper/maven-wrapper.jar" \
224:   "-Dmaven.home=${M2_HOME}" "-Dmaven.multiModuleProjectDirectory=${MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR}" \
225:   ${WRAPPER_LAUNCHER} $MAVEN_CONFIG "$@"

  1: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  2: @REM Licensed to the Apache Software Foundation (ASF) under one
  3: @REM or more contributor license agreements.  See the NOTICE file
  4: @REM distributed with this work for additional information
  5: @REM regarding copyright ownership.  The ASF licenses this file
  6: @REM to you under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the

  8: @REM with the License.  You may obtain a copy of the License at
  9: @REM
 10: @REM    http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
 11: @REM
 12: @REM Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing,
 13: @REM software distributed under the License is distributed on an
 14: @REM "AS IS" BASIS, WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY
 15: @REM KIND, either express or implied.  See the License for the
 16: @REM specific language governing permissions and limitations
 17: @REM under the License.
 18: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 19: 
 20: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 21: @REM Maven2 Start Up Batch script
 22: @REM
 23: @REM Required ENV vars:
 24: @REM JAVA_HOME - location of a JDK home dir
 25: @REM
 26: @REM Optional ENV vars
 27: @REM M2_HOME - location of maven2's installed home dir
 28: @REM MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO - set to 'on' to enable the echoing of the batch commands
 29: @REM MAVEN_BATCH_PAUSE - set to 'on' to wait for a key stroke before ending
 30: @REM MAVEN_OPTS - parameters passed to the Java VM when running Maven
 31: @REM     e.g. to debug Maven itself, use
 32: @REM set MAVEN_OPTS=-Xdebug -Xrunjdwp:transport=dt_socket,server=y,suspend=y,address=8000
 33: @REM MAVEN_SKIP_RC - flag to disable loading of mavenrc files
 34: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 35: 
 36: @REM Begin all REM lines with '@' in case MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO is 'on'
 37: @echo off
 38: @REM enable echoing my setting MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO to 'on'
 39: @if "%MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO%" == "on"  echo %MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO%
 40: 
 41: @REM set %HOME% to equivalent of $HOME
 42: if "%HOME%" == "" (set "HOME=%HOMEDRIVE%%HOMEPATH%")
 43: 
 44: @REM Execute a user defined script before this one
 45: if not "%MAVEN_SKIP_RC%" == "" goto skipRcPre
 46: @REM check for pre script, once with legacy .bat ending and once with .cmd ending
 47: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.bat" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.bat"
 48: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.cmd" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.cmd"
 49: :skipRcPre
 50: 
 51: @setlocal
 52: 
 53: set ERROR_CODE=0
 54: 
 55: @REM To isolate internal variables from possible post scripts, we use another setlocal
 56: @setlocal
 57: 
 58: @REM ==== START VALIDATION ====
 59: if not "%JAVA_HOME%" == "" goto OkJHome
 60: 
 61: echo.
 62: echo Error: JAVA_HOME not found in your environment. >&2
 63: echo Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the >&2
 64: echo location of your Java installation. >&2
 65: echo.
 66: goto error
 67: 
 68: :OkJHome
 69: if exist "%JAVA_HOME%\bin\java.exe" goto init
 70: 
 71: echo.
 72: echo Error: JAVA_HOME is set to an invalid directory. >&2
 73: echo JAVA_HOME = "%JAVA_HOME%" >&2
 74: echo Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the >&2
 75: echo location of your Java installation. >&2
 76: echo.
 77: goto error
 78: 
 79: @REM ==== END VALIDATION ====
 80: 
 81: :init
 82: 
 83: @REM Find the project base dir, i.e. the directory that contains the folder ".mvn".
 84: @REM Fallback to current working directory if not found.
 85: 
 86: set MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=%MAVEN_BASEDIR%
 87: IF NOT "%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%"=="" goto endDetectBaseDir
 88: 
 89: set EXEC_DIR=%CD%
 90: set WDIR=%EXEC_DIR%
 91: :findBaseDir
 92: IF EXIST "%WDIR%"\.mvn goto baseDirFound
 93: cd ..
 94: IF "%WDIR%"=="%CD%" goto baseDirNotFound
 95: set WDIR=%CD%
 96: goto findBaseDir
 97: 
 98: :baseDirFound
 99: set MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=%WDIR%
100: cd "%EXEC_DIR%"
101: goto endDetectBaseDir
102: 
103: :baseDirNotFound
104: set MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=%EXEC_DIR%
105: cd "%EXEC_DIR%"
106: 
107: :endDetectBaseDir
108: 
109: IF NOT EXIST "%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%\.mvn\jvm.config" goto endReadAdditionalConfig
110: 
111: @setlocal EnableExtensions EnableDelayedExpansion
112: for /F "usebackq delims=" %%a in ("%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%\.mvn\jvm.config") do set JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS=!JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS! %%a
113: @endlocal & set JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS=%JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS%
114: 
115: :endReadAdditionalConfig
116: 
117: SET MAVEN_JAVA_EXE="%JAVA_HOME%\bin\java.exe"
118: 
119: set WRAPPER_JAR="%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%\.mvn\wrapper\maven-wrapper.jar"
120: set WRAPPER_LAUNCHER=org.apache.maven.wrapper.MavenWrapperMain
121: 
122: %MAVEN_JAVA_EXE% %JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS% %MAVEN_OPTS% %MAVEN_DEBUG_OPTS% -classpath %WRAPPER_JAR% "-Dmaven.multiModuleProjectDirectory=%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%" %WRAPPER_LAUNCHER% %MAVEN_CONFIG% %*
123: if ERRORLEVEL 1 goto error
124: goto end
125: 
126: :error
127: set ERROR_CODE=1
128: 
129: :end
130: @endlocal & set ERROR_CODE=%ERROR_CODE%
131: 
132: if not "%MAVEN_SKIP_RC%" == "" goto skipRcPost
133: @REM check for post script, once with legacy .bat ending and once with .cmd ending
134: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.bat" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.bat"
135: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.cmd" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.cmd"
136: :skipRcPost
137: 
138: @REM pause the script if MAVEN_BATCH_PAUSE is set to 'on'
139: if "%MAVEN_BATCH_PAUSE%" == "on" pause
140: 
141: if "%MAVEN_TERMINATE_CMD%" == "on" exit %ERROR_CODE%
142: 
143: exit /B %ERROR_CODE%

File: order-service/src/main/java/com/project/warehouse/anticorruptation/account/AccountAntiCorruptionLayerService.java
================
 1: package com.project.warehouse.anticorruptation.account;
 2: import com.project.warehouse.anticorruptation.AbstractAntiCorruptionLayerService;
 3: import com.project.warehouse.anticorruptation.AntiCorruptionLayerStrategy;
 4: import com.project.warehouse.model.Customer;
 5: import com.project.warehouse.model.CustomerContact;
 6: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 7: import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
 8: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
 9: import javax.annotation.PostConstruct;
10: import java.util.HashMap;
11: import java.util.Map;
12: import java.util.Optional;
13: @Service
14: public class AccountAntiCorruptionLayerService extends AbstractAntiCorruptionLayerService {
15:     private Map<String, AntiCorruptionLayerStrategy> customerAntiCorruptationRegistry;
16:     private Map<String, AntiCorruptionLayerStrategy> customerContactAnticorruptationRegistry;
17:     @Autowired
18:     private AccountToCustomerAntiCorruptionLayerServiceHalJsonStrategy accountToCustomerAntiCorruptionLayerServiceHalJsonStrategy;
19:     @Autowired
20:     private AccountToCustomerContactAntiCorruptionLayerServiceHalJsonStrategy accountToCustomerContactAntiCorruptionLayerServiceHalJsonStrategy;
21:     @PostConstruct
22:     public void init(){
23:         customerAntiCorruptationRegistry = new HashMap<>();
24:         customerAntiCorruptationRegistry.put(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_VALUE, accountToCustomerAntiCorruptionLayerServiceHalJsonStrategy);
25:         customerAntiCorruptationRegistry.put(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_UTF8_VALUE, accountToCustomerAntiCorruptionLayerServiceHalJsonStrategy);
26:         customerContactAnticorruptationRegistry = new HashMap<>();
27:         customerContactAnticorruptationRegistry.put(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_VALUE, accountToCustomerContactAntiCorruptionLayerServiceHalJsonStrategy);
28:         customerContactAnticorruptationRegistry.put(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_UTF8_VALUE, accountToCustomerContactAntiCorruptionLayerServiceHalJsonStrategy);
29:     }
30:     public Customer newCustomer(String customer, String mediaType){
31:         return (Customer) Optional.ofNullable(customerAntiCorruptationRegistry.get(mediaType))
32:                 .map(antiCorruptationLayerStrategy -> antiCorruptationLayerStrategy.traslate(customer))
33:                 .orElse(null);
34:     }
35:     public CustomerContact newCustomerContact(String gustomerContact, String mediaType){
36:         return (CustomerContact) Optional.ofNullable(customerContactAnticorruptationRegistry.get(mediaType))
37:                 .map(antiCorruptationLayerStrategy -> antiCorruptationLayerStrategy.traslate(gustomerContact))
38:                 .orElse(null);
39:     }
40: }

================
File: order-service/src/main/java/com/project/warehouse/anticorruptation/account/AccountToCustomerAntiCorruptionLayerServiceHalJsonStrategy.java
================
 1: package com.project.warehouse.anticorruptation.account;
 2: import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.node.ObjectNode;
 3: import com.project.warehouse.anticorruptation.AbstractAntiCorruptionLayerStrategy;
 4: import com.project.warehouse.model.Customer;
 5: import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
 6: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
 7: import java.io.IOException;
 8: @Slf4j
 9: @Component
10: public class AccountToCustomerAntiCorruptionLayerServiceHalJsonStrategy extends AbstractAntiCorruptionLayerStrategy<Customer> {
11:     @Override
12:     public Customer traslate(String body) {
13:         Customer customer = null;
14:         try {
15:             customer  = new Customer();
16:             ObjectNode node = (ObjectNode) objectMapper.readTree(body);
17:             customer.setFirstName(node.get("firstName").asText());
18:             customer.setLastName(node.get("lastName").asText());
19:             customer.setTaxCode(node.get("taxCode").asText());
20:         } catch (IOException e) {
21:             log.error(e.getMessage());
22:         }
23:         return customer;
24:     }
25: }

================
File: order-service/src/main/java/com/project/warehouse/anticorruptation/account/AccountToCustomerContactAntiCorruptionLayerServiceHalJsonStrategy.java
================
 1: package com.project.warehouse.anticorruptation.account;
 2: import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode;
 3: import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.node.ObjectNode;
 4: import com.project.warehouse.anticorruptation.AbstractAntiCorruptionLayerStrategy;
 5: import com.project.warehouse.model.CustomerContact;
 6: import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
 7: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
 8: import java.io.IOException;
 9: import java.util.Optional;
10: import java.util.function.BiFunction;
11: @Slf4j
12: @Component
13: public class AccountToCustomerContactAntiCorruptionLayerServiceHalJsonStrategy extends AbstractAntiCorruptionLayerStrategy<CustomerContact> {
14:     @Override
15:     public CustomerContact traslate(String body) {
16:         log.info("body" + body);
17:         CustomerContact customerContact = null;
18:         try {
19:             customerContact  = new CustomerContact();
20:             ObjectNode node = (ObjectNode) objectMapper.readTree(body);
21:             JsonNode telephoneNumber = node.get("telephoneNumber");
22:             customerContact.setTelephone(String.format("%s %s%s",
23:                     getNodeValue.apply("countryPrefix",telephoneNumber),
24:                     getNodeValue.apply("prefix",telephoneNumber),
25:                     getNodeValue.apply("telephone",telephoneNumber)));
26:             customerContact.setMail(getNodeValue.apply("mail", node));
27:         } catch (IOException e) {
28:             log.error(e.getMessage());
29:         }
30:         return customerContact;
31:     }
32:     private BiFunction<String,JsonNode,String> getNodeValue = (key, node) ->
33:             Optional.ofNullable(node.get(key)).map(JsonNode::asText).orElse("");
34: }

2: public interface AntiCorruptionLayerStrategy<T> {
3:     T traslate(String body);
4: }

File: order-service/src/main/java/com/project/warehouse/anticorruptation/productcatalog/ProductCatalogAntiCorruptionLayerService.java
================
 1: package com.project.warehouse.anticorruptation.productcatalog;
 2: import com.project.warehouse.anticorruptation.AbstractAntiCorruptionLayerService;
 3: import com.project.warehouse.anticorruptation.AntiCorruptionLayerStrategy;
 4: import com.project.warehouse.model.Product;
 5: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 6: import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
 7: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
 8: import javax.annotation.PostConstruct;
 9: import java.util.HashMap;
10: import java.util.Map;
11: import java.util.Optional;
12: @Service
13: public class ProductCatalogAntiCorruptionLayerService extends AbstractAntiCorruptionLayerService {
14:     private Map<String, AntiCorruptionLayerStrategy> productCatalogAntiCorruptationRegistry;
15:     @Autowired
16:     private ProductCatalogAntiCorruptionLayerServiceHalJsonStrategy productCatalogAntiCorruptionLayerServiceHalJsonStrategy;
17:     @PostConstruct
18:     public void init(){
19:         productCatalogAntiCorruptationRegistry = new HashMap<>();
20:         productCatalogAntiCorruptationRegistry.put(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_VALUE,productCatalogAntiCorruptionLayerServiceHalJsonStrategy);
21:         productCatalogAntiCorruptationRegistry.put(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_UTF8_VALUE,productCatalogAntiCorruptionLayerServiceHalJsonStrategy);
22:     }
23:     public Product newGoods(String goods, String mediaType){
24:         return (Product) Optional.ofNullable(productCatalogAntiCorruptationRegistry.get(mediaType))
25:                 .map(anticCorruptationLayerStrategy -> anticCorruptationLayerStrategy.traslate(goods))
26:                 .orElse(null);
27:     }
28: }

================
File: order-service/src/main/java/com/project/warehouse/anticorruptation/productcatalog/ProductCatalogAntiCorruptionLayerServiceHalJsonStrategy.java
================
 1: package com.project.warehouse.anticorruptation.productcatalog;
 2: import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.node.ObjectNode;
 3: import com.project.warehouse.anticorruptation.AbstractAntiCorruptionLayerStrategy;
 4: import com.project.warehouse.model.Product;
 5: import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
 6: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
 7: import java.io.IOException;
 8: import java.math.BigDecimal;
 9: import java.util.Map;
10: @Slf4j
11: @Component
12: public class ProductCatalogAntiCorruptionLayerServiceHalJsonStrategy extends AbstractAntiCorruptionLayerStrategy<Product> {
13:     @Override
14:     public Product traslate(String body) {
15:         Product goods = new Product();
16:         try {
17:             ObjectNode node = (ObjectNode) objectMapper.readTree(body);
18:             ObjectNode goodsNode = (ObjectNode) node.get("goods");
19:             Map links = objectMapper.convertValue(node.get("_links"), Map.class);
20:             String[] split = String.valueOf(links.get("price-list")).split("/");
21:             String pirceListId = split[split.length-1].substring(0, split[split.length-1].length()-1);
22:             goods.setPriceListId(pirceListId);
23:             goods.setId(goodsNode.get("id").asText());
24:             goods.setBarCode(goodsNode.get("barCode").asText());
25:             goods.setName(goodsNode.get("name").asText());
26:             goods.setProductsAttribute(objectMapper.convertValue(goodsNode.get("goodsAttribute"), Map.class));
27:             goods.setPrice(new BigDecimal(node.get("price").asDouble()).setScale(2, BigDecimal.ROUND_HALF_DOWN));
28:         } catch (IOException e) {
29:             log.error(e.getMessage());
30:         }
31:         return goods;
32:     }
33: }

 1: package com.project.warehouse.config;
 2: import com.datastax.driver.core.Cluster;
 3: import org.springframework.beans.factory.BeanFactory;
 4: import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.cassandra.CassandraProperties;
 5: import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.data.cassandra.CassandraDataAutoConfiguration;
 6: import org.springframework.boot.context.properties.EnableConfigurationProperties;
 7: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
 8: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
 9: import org.springframework.core.env.Environment;
10: import org.springframework.data.cassandra.config.CassandraEntityClassScanner;
11: import org.springframework.data.cassandra.mapping.BasicCassandraMappingContext;
12: import org.springframework.data.cassandra.mapping.CassandraMappingContext;
13: import org.springframework.data.cassandra.mapping.SimpleUserTypeResolver;
14: @Configuration
15: @EnableConfigurationProperties(CassandraProperties.class)
16: class CassandraConfiguration extends CassandraDataAutoConfiguration {
17:     public CassandraConfiguration(BeanFactory beanFactory,
18:                                   CassandraProperties properties,
19:                                   Cluster cluster, Environment environment) {
20:         super(beanFactory, properties, cluster, environment);
21:     }
22:     @Bean
23:     public CassandraMappingContext cassandraMapping(BeanFactory beanFactory, Cluster cluster, CassandraProperties properties) throws ClassNotFoundException {
24:         BasicCassandraMappingContext context = new BasicCassandraMappingContext();
25:         context.setInitialEntitySet(CassandraEntityClassScanner.scan("com.project.warehouse.event"));
26:         context.setUserTypeResolver(new SimpleUserTypeResolver(cluster,
27:                 properties.getKeyspaceName()));
28:         return context;
29:     }
30: }

File: order-service/src/main/java/com/project/warehouse/event/factory/DomainEventFactory.java
================
 1: package com.project.warehouse.event.factory;
 2: import com.datastax.driver.core.utils.UUIDs;
 3: import com.project.warehouse.event.model.AbstractDomainEvent;
 4: import com.project.warehouse.event.model.EventTypeEnum;
 5: import com.project.warehouse.event.model.PurchaseOrderErrorEvent;
 6: import com.project.warehouse.event.model.PurchaseOrderEvent;
 7: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 8: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
 9: import java.util.Date;
10: @Component
11: public class DomainEventFactory {
12:     @Autowired
13:     private SecurityUtils securityUtils;
14:     public PurchaseOrderEvent newPurchaseOrderEvent(String correlationId, String idPurchaseOrder,
15:                                                     String idProductCatalog, String idGoodsInPurchaseOrder,
16:                                                     String customerUserName, EventTypeEnum type){
17:         PurchaseOrderEvent event = new PurchaseOrderEvent();
18:         event.setId(UUIDs.timeBased());
19:         event.setCustomerUserName(customerUserName);
20:         event.setIdProductCatalog(idProductCatalog);
21:         event.setIdPurchaseOrder(idPurchaseOrder);
22:         event.setIdProductsInPurchaseOrder(idGoodsInPurchaseOrder);
23:         event.setType(type);
24:         newEventAuditData(event,correlationId);
25:         return event;
26:     }
27:     public PurchaseOrderErrorEvent newPurchaseOrderErrorEvent(String correlationId, String idPurchaseOrder,
28:                                                               String idProductCatalog, String idGoodsInPurchaseOrder,
29:                                                               String customerUserName, EventTypeEnum type,
30:                                                               String message, Class exceptionClassName){
31:         PurchaseOrderErrorEvent event = new PurchaseOrderErrorEvent();
32:         event.setId(UUIDs.timeBased());
33:         event.setCustomerUserName(customerUserName);
34:         event.setIdProductCatalog(idProductCatalog);
35:         event.setIdPurchaseOrder(idPurchaseOrder);
36:         event.setIdProductsInPurchaseOrder(idGoodsInPurchaseOrder);
37:         event.setType(type);
38:         newEventAuditData(event,correlationId);
39:         event.setExceptionClassName(exceptionClassName.getName());
40:         event.setMessage(message);
41:         return event;
42:     }
43:     private void newEventAuditData(AbstractDomainEvent eventAuditData, String correlationId) {
44:         eventAuditData.setCorrelationId(correlationId);
45:         eventAuditData.setUserName(securityUtils.getPrincipalUserName());
46:         eventAuditData.setTimeStamp(new Date());
47:     }
48: }

 1: package com.project.warehouse.event.service;
 2: import com.project.warehouse.event.factory.DomainEventFactory;
 3: import com.project.warehouse.event.model.EventTypeEnum;
 4: import com.project.warehouse.event.model.PurchaseOrderErrorEvent;
 5: import com.project.warehouse.event.model.PurchaseOrderEvent;
 6: import com.project.warehouse.event.repository.PurchaseOrderErrorEventRepository;
 7: import com.project.warehouse.event.repository.PurchaseOrderEventRepository;
 8: import lombok.Data;
 9: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
10: import org.springframework.cloud.stream.annotation.EnableBinding;
11: import org.springframework.messaging.SubscribableChannel;
12: import org.springframework.messaging.support.MessageBuilder;
13: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
14: @Data
15: @Service
16: public class EventDomainPubblishService {
17:     @Autowired
18:     private DomainEventFactory domainEventFactory;
19:     @Autowired
20:     public PurchaseOrderEventRepository purchaseOrderEventRepository;
21:     @Autowired
22:     public PurchaseOrderErrorEventRepository purchaseOrderErrorEventRepository;
23:     @Autowired
24:     private SubscribableChannel purchaseOrderEventOutboundChannel;

26:                                                         String idProductCatalogId, String idGoodsInPurchaseOrder,
27:                                                         String customerUserName, EventTypeEnum type){

29:                 idProductCatalogId, idGoodsInPurchaseOrder,
30:                 customerUserName, type);
31:         purchaseOrderEventRepository.save(event);
32:         purchaseOrderEventOutboundChannel.send(MessageBuilder.withPayload(event).build());
33:         return event;
34:     }
35:     public PurchaseOrderErrorEvent publishPurchaseOrderErrorEvent(String correlationId, String idPurchaseOrder,
36:                                                                   String idProductCatalogId, String idGoodsInPurchaseOrder,

40:                 idProductCatalogId, idGoodsInPurchaseOrder,
41:                 customerUserName, type,message, exceptionClassName);
42:         purchaseOrderErrorEventRepository.save(event);
43:         purchaseOrderEventOutboundChannel.send(MessageBuilder.withPayload(event).build());
44:         return event;
45:     }
46: }

================
File: order-service/src/main/java/com/project/warehouse/rest/rest/CustomerRestController.java
================
 1: package com.project.warehouse.rest.rest;
 2: import com.netflix.hystrix.contrib.javanica.annotation.HystrixCommand;
 3: import com.netflix.hystrix.contrib.javanica.annotation.HystrixProperty;
 4: import lombok.Data;
 5: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 6: import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
 7: import org.springframework.security.access.prepost.PreAuthorize;
 8: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;
 9: import java.security.Principal;
10: @Data
11: @RestController
12: @RequestMapping("/purchase-order")
13: public class CustomerRestFullEndPoint extends AbstractPurchaseOrderRestFullEndPoint {
14:     @Autowired
15:     private CustomerHateoasFactory customerHateoasFactory;
16:     @GetMapping("/{orderNumber}/customer")
17:     @PreAuthorize("hasRole('ROLE_USER')")
18:     @HystrixCommand(commandProperties = {@HystrixProperty(name="execution.isolation.strategy", value="SEMAPHORE")})
19:     public ResponseEntity getCustomerDataPuchaseOrder(@PathVariable String orderNumber){
20:         PurchaseOrder purchaseOrder = purchaseOrderService.findPurchaseOrder(securityUtils.getPrincipalUserName(), orderNumber);
21:         CustomerDataResponseDTO customerDataResponseDTO =
22:                 CustomerDataResponseDTO.builder()
23:                         .customer(purchaseOrder.getCustomer())
24:                         .customerContact(purchaseOrder.getCustomerContact()).build();
25:         return ResponseEntity.ok(customerHateoasFactory.toResource(orderNumber, customerDataResponseDTO));
26:     }
27:     @PatchMapping("/{orderNumber}/customer")
28:     @PreAuthorize("hasRole('ROLE_USER')")
29:     @HystrixCommand(commandProperties = {@HystrixProperty(name="execution.isolation.strategy", value="SEMAPHORE")})
30:     public ResponseEntity customerDataPuchaseOrder(@PathVariable String orderNumber, Principal principal){
31:         purchaseOrderService.withCustomerAndCustomerContact(orderNumber, principal.getName(), null, null);
32:         return ResponseEntity.noContent().build();
33:     }
34: }

================
File: product-catalog-management-website/mvnw
================
  1: #!/bin/sh
  2: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  3: # Licensed to the Apache Software Foundation (ASF) under one
  4: # or more contributor license agreements.  See the NOTICE file
  5: # distributed with this work for additional information
  6: # regarding copyright ownership.  The ASF licenses this file
  7: # to you under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the
  8: # "License"); you may not use this file except in compliance
  9: # with the License.  You may obtain a copy of the License at
 10: #
 11: #    http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
 12: #
 13: # Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing,
 14: # software distributed under the License is distributed on an
 15: # "AS IS" BASIS, WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY
 16: # KIND, either express or implied.  See the License for the
 17: # specific language governing permissions and limitations
 18: # under the License.
 19: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 20: 
 21: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 22: # Maven2 Start Up Batch script
 23: #
 24: # Required ENV vars:
 25: # ------------------
 26: #   JAVA_HOME - location of a JDK home dir
 27: #
 28: # Optional ENV vars
 29: # -----------------
 30: #   M2_HOME - location of maven2's installed home dir
 31: #   MAVEN_OPTS - parameters passed to the Java VM when running Maven
 32: #     e.g. to debug Maven itself, use
 33: #       set MAVEN_OPTS=-Xdebug -Xrunjdwp:transport=dt_socket,server=y,suspend=y,address=8000
 34: #   MAVEN_SKIP_RC - flag to disable loading of mavenrc files
 35: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 36: 
 37: if [ -z "$MAVEN_SKIP_RC" ] ; then
 38: 
 39:   if [ -f /etc/mavenrc ] ; then
 40:     . /etc/mavenrc
 41:   fi
 42: 
 43:   if [ -f "$HOME/.mavenrc" ] ; then
 44:     . "$HOME/.mavenrc"
 45:   fi
 46: 
 47: fi
 48: 
 49: # OS specific support.  $var _must_ be set to either true or false.
 50: cygwin=false;
 51: darwin=false;
 52: mingw=false
 53: case "`uname`" in
 54:   CYGWIN*) cygwin=true ;;
 55:   MINGW*) mingw=true;;
 56:   Darwin*) darwin=true
 57:     # Use /usr/libexec/java_home if available, otherwise fall back to /Library/Java/Home
 58:     # See https://developer.apple.com/library/mac/qa/qa1170/_index.html
 59:     if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ]; then
 60:       if [ -x "/usr/libexec/java_home" ]; then
 61:         export JAVA_HOME="`/usr/libexec/java_home`"
 62:       else
 63:         export JAVA_HOME="/Library/Java/Home"
 64:       fi
 65:     fi
 66:     ;;
 67: esac
 68: 
 69: if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ] ; then
 70:   if [ -r /etc/gentoo-release ] ; then
 71:     JAVA_HOME=`java-config --jre-home`
 72:   fi
 73: fi
 74: 
 75: if [ -z "$M2_HOME" ] ; then
 76:   ## resolve links - $0 may be a link to maven's home
 77:   PRG="$0"
 78: 
 79:   # need this for relative symlinks
 80:   while [ -h "$PRG" ] ; do
 81:     ls=`ls -ld "$PRG"`
 82:     link=`expr "$ls" : '.*-> \(.*\)$'`
 83:     if expr "$link" : '/.*' > /dev/null; then
 84:       PRG="$link"
 85:     else
 86:       PRG="`dirname "$PRG"`/$link"
 87:     fi
 88:   done
 89: 
 90:   saveddir=`pwd`
 91: 
 92:   M2_HOME=`dirname "$PRG"`/..
 93: 
 94:   # make it fully qualified
 95:   M2_HOME=`cd "$M2_HOME" && pwd`
 96: 
 97:   cd "$saveddir"
 98:   # echo Using m2 at $M2_HOME
 99: fi
100: 
101: # For Cygwin, ensure paths are in UNIX format before anything is touched
102: if $cygwin ; then
103:   [ -n "$M2_HOME" ] &&
104:     M2_HOME=`cygpath --unix "$M2_HOME"`
105:   [ -n "$JAVA_HOME" ] &&
106:     JAVA_HOME=`cygpath --unix "$JAVA_HOME"`
107:   [ -n "$CLASSPATH" ] &&
108:     CLASSPATH=`cygpath --path --unix "$CLASSPATH"`
109: fi
110: 
111: # For Migwn, ensure paths are in UNIX format before anything is touched
112: if $mingw ; then
113:   [ -n "$M2_HOME" ] &&
114:     M2_HOME="`(cd "$M2_HOME"; pwd)`"
115:   [ -n "$JAVA_HOME" ] &&
116:     JAVA_HOME="`(cd "$JAVA_HOME"; pwd)`"
117:   # TODO classpath?
118: fi
119: 
120: if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ]; then
121:   javaExecutable="`which javac`"
122:   if [ -n "$javaExecutable" ] && ! [ "`expr \"$javaExecutable\" : '\([^ ]*\)'`" = "no" ]; then
123:     # readlink(1) is not available as standard on Solaris 10.
124:     readLink=`which readlink`
125:     if [ ! `expr "$readLink" : '\([^ ]*\)'` = "no" ]; then
126:       if $darwin ; then
127:         javaHome="`dirname \"$javaExecutable\"`"
128:         javaExecutable="`cd \"$javaHome\" && pwd -P`/javac"
129:       else
130:         javaExecutable="`readlink -f \"$javaExecutable\"`"
131:       fi
132:       javaHome="`dirname \"$javaExecutable\"`"
133:       javaHome=`expr "$javaHome" : '\(.*\)/bin'`
134:       JAVA_HOME="$javaHome"
135:       export JAVA_HOME
136:     fi
137:   fi
138: fi
139: 
140: if [ -z "$JAVACMD" ] ; then
141:   if [ -n "$JAVA_HOME"  ] ; then
142:     if [ -x "$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java" ] ; then
143:       # IBM's JDK on AIX uses strange locations for the executables
144:       JAVACMD="$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java"
145:     else
146:       JAVACMD="$JAVA_HOME/bin/java"
147:     fi
148:   else
149:     JAVACMD="`which java`"
150:   fi
151: fi
152: 
153: if [ ! -x "$JAVACMD" ] ; then
154:   echo "Error: JAVA_HOME is not defined correctly." >&2
155:   echo "  We cannot execute $JAVACMD" >&2
156:   exit 1
157: fi
158: 
159: if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ] ; then
160:   echo "Warning: JAVA_HOME environment variable is not set."
161: fi
162: 
163: CLASSWORLDS_LAUNCHER=org.codehaus.plexus.classworlds.launcher.Launcher
164: 
165: # traverses directory structure from process work directory to filesystem root
166: # first directory with .mvn subdirectory is considered project base directory
167: find_maven_basedir() {
168: 
169:   if [ -z "$1" ]
170:   then
171:     echo "Path not specified to find_maven_basedir"
172:     return 1
173:   fi
174: 
175:   basedir="$1"
176:   wdir="$1"
177:   while [ "$wdir" != '/' ] ; do
178:     if [ -d "$wdir"/.mvn ] ; then
179:       basedir=$wdir
180:       break
181:     fi
182:     # workaround for JBEAP-8937 (on Solaris 10/Sparc)
183:     if [ -d "${wdir}" ]; then
184:       wdir=`cd "$wdir/.."; pwd`
185:     fi
186:     # end of workaround
187:   done
188:   echo "${basedir}"
189: }
190: 
191: # concatenates all lines of a file
192: concat_lines() {
193:   if [ -f "$1" ]; then
194:     echo "$(tr -s '\n' ' ' < "$1")"
195:   fi
196: }
197: 
198: BASE_DIR=`find_maven_basedir "$(pwd)"`
199: if [ -z "$BASE_DIR" ]; then
200:   exit 1;
201: fi
202: 
203: export MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=${MAVEN_BASEDIR:-"$BASE_DIR"}
204: echo $MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR
205: MAVEN_OPTS="$(concat_lines "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR/.mvn/jvm.config") $MAVEN_OPTS"
206: 
207: # For Cygwin, switch paths to Windows format before running java
208: if $cygwin; then
209:   [ -n "$M2_HOME" ] &&
210:     M2_HOME=`cygpath --path --windows "$M2_HOME"`
211:   [ -n "$JAVA_HOME" ] &&
212:     JAVA_HOME=`cygpath --path --windows "$JAVA_HOME"`
213:   [ -n "$CLASSPATH" ] &&
214:     CLASSPATH=`cygpath --path --windows "$CLASSPATH"`
215:   [ -n "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR" ] &&
216:     MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=`cygpath --path --windows "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR"`
217: fi
218: 
219: WRAPPER_LAUNCHER=org.apache.maven.wrapper.MavenWrapperMain
220: 
221: exec "$JAVACMD" \
222:   $MAVEN_OPTS \
223:   -classpath "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR/.mvn/wrapper/maven-wrapper.jar" \
224:   "-Dmaven.home=${M2_HOME}" "-Dmaven.multiModuleProjectDirectory=${MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR}" \
225:   ${WRAPPER_LAUNCHER} $MAVEN_CONFIG "$@"

File: product-catalog-management-website/mvnw.cmd
================
  1: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  2: @REM Licensed to the Apache Software Foundation (ASF) under one
  3: @REM or more contributor license agreements.  See the NOTICE file
  4: @REM distributed with this work for additional information
  5: @REM regarding copyright ownership.  The ASF licenses this file
  6: @REM to you under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the
  7: @REM "License"); you may not use this file except in compliance
  8: @REM with the License.  You may obtain a copy of the License at
  9: @REM
 10: @REM    http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
 11: @REM
 12: @REM Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing,
 13: @REM software distributed under the License is distributed on an
 14: @REM "AS IS" BASIS, WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY
 15: @REM KIND, either express or implied.  See the License for the
 16: @REM specific language governing permissions and limitations
 17: @REM under the License.
 18: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 19: 
 20: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 21: @REM Maven2 Start Up Batch script
 22: @REM
 23: @REM Required ENV vars:
 24: @REM JAVA_HOME - location of a JDK home dir
 25: @REM
 26: @REM Optional ENV vars
 27: @REM M2_HOME - location of maven2's installed home dir
 28: @REM MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO - set to 'on' to enable the echoing of the batch commands
 29: @REM MAVEN_BATCH_PAUSE - set to 'on' to wait for a key stroke before ending
 30: @REM MAVEN_OPTS - parameters passed to the Java VM when running Maven
 31: @REM     e.g. to debug Maven itself, use
 32: @REM set MAVEN_OPTS=-Xdebug -Xrunjdwp:transport=dt_socket,server=y,suspend=y,address=8000
 33: @REM MAVEN_SKIP_RC - flag to disable loading of mavenrc files
 34: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 35: 
 36: @REM Begin all REM lines with '@' in case MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO is 'on'
 37: @echo off
 38: @REM enable echoing my setting MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO to 'on'
 39: @if "%MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO%" == "on"  echo %MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO%
 40: 
 41: @REM set %HOME% to equivalent of $HOME
 42: if "%HOME%" == "" (set "HOME=%HOMEDRIVE%%HOMEPATH%")
 43: 
 44: @REM Execute a user defined script before this one
 45: if not "%MAVEN_SKIP_RC%" == "" goto skipRcPre
 46: @REM check for pre script, once with legacy .bat ending and once with .cmd ending
 47: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.bat" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.bat"
 48: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.cmd" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.cmd"
 49: :skipRcPre
 50: 
 51: @setlocal
 52: 
 53: set ERROR_CODE=0
 54: 
 55: @REM To isolate internal variables from possible post scripts, we use another setlocal
 56: @setlocal
 57: 
 58: @REM ==== START VALIDATION ====
 59: if not "%JAVA_HOME%" == "" goto OkJHome
 60: 
 61: echo.
 62: echo Error: JAVA_HOME not found in your environment. >&2
 63: echo Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the >&2
 64: echo location of your Java installation. >&2
 65: echo.
 66: goto error
 67: 
 68: :OkJHome
 69: if exist "%JAVA_HOME%\bin\java.exe" goto init
 70: 
 71: echo.
 72: echo Error: JAVA_HOME is set to an invalid directory. >&2
 73: echo JAVA_HOME = "%JAVA_HOME%" >&2
 74: echo Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the >&2
 75: echo location of your Java installation. >&2
 76: echo.
 77: goto error
 78: 
 79: @REM ==== END VALIDATION ====
 80: 
 81: :init
 82: 
 83: @REM Find the project base dir, i.e. the directory that contains the folder ".mvn".
 84: @REM Fallback to current working directory if not found.
 85: 
 86: set MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=%MAVEN_BASEDIR%
 87: IF NOT "%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%"=="" goto endDetectBaseDir
 88: 
 89: set EXEC_DIR=%CD%
 90: set WDIR=%EXEC_DIR%
 91: :findBaseDir
 92: IF EXIST "%WDIR%"\.mvn goto baseDirFound
 93: cd ..
 94: IF "%WDIR%"=="%CD%" goto baseDirNotFound
 95: set WDIR=%CD%
 96: goto findBaseDir
 97: 
 98: :baseDirFound
 99: set MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=%WDIR%
100: cd "%EXEC_DIR%"
101: goto endDetectBaseDir
102: 
103: :baseDirNotFound
104: set MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=%EXEC_DIR%
105: cd "%EXEC_DIR%"
106: 
107: :endDetectBaseDir
108: 
109: IF NOT EXIST "%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%\.mvn\jvm.config" goto endReadAdditionalConfig
110: 
111: @setlocal EnableExtensions EnableDelayedExpansion
112: for /F "usebackq delims=" %%a in ("%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%\.mvn\jvm.config") do set JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS=!JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS! %%a
113: @endlocal & set JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS=%JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS%
114: 
115: :endReadAdditionalConfig
116: 
117: SET MAVEN_JAVA_EXE="%JAVA_HOME%\bin\java.exe"
118: 
119: set WRAPPER_JAR="%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%\.mvn\wrapper\maven-wrapper.jar"
120: set WRAPPER_LAUNCHER=org.apache.maven.wrapper.MavenWrapperMain
121: 
122: %MAVEN_JAVA_EXE% %JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS% %MAVEN_OPTS% %MAVEN_DEBUG_OPTS% -classpath %WRAPPER_JAR% "-Dmaven.multiModuleProjectDirectory=%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%" %WRAPPER_LAUNCHER% %MAVEN_CONFIG% %*
123: if ERRORLEVEL 1 goto error
124: goto end
125: 
126: :error
127: set ERROR_CODE=1
128: 
129: :end
130: @endlocal & set ERROR_CODE=%ERROR_CODE%
131: 
132: if not "%MAVEN_SKIP_RC%" == "" goto skipRcPost
133: @REM check for post script, once with legacy .bat ending and once with .cmd ending
134: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.bat" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.bat"
135: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.cmd" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.cmd"
136: :skipRcPost
137: 
138: @REM pause the script if MAVEN_BATCH_PAUSE is set to 'on'
139: if "%MAVEN_BATCH_PAUSE%" == "on" pause
140: 
141: if "%MAVEN_TERMINATE_CMD%" == "on" exit %ERROR_CODE%
142: 
143: exit /B %ERROR_CODE%

 1: package com.project.warehouse.config;
 2: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 3: import org.springframework.cloud.client.loadbalancer.LoadBalanced;
 4: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
 5: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
 6: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.client.OAuth2ClientContext;
 7: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.client.OAuth2RestTemplate;
 8: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.client.resource.OAuth2ProtectedResourceDetails;
 9: @Configuration
10: public class OAuth2ClientConfig {
11:     @Autowired
12:     private OAuth2ClientContext context;
13:     @Bean
14:     @LoadBalanced
15:     public OAuth2RestTemplate oAuth2RestTemplate(OAuth2ProtectedResourceDetails authorizationCodeResourceDetails){
16:         return new OAuth2RestTemplate(authorizationCodeResourceDetails, context);
17:     }
18: }
