File: product-catalog-management-website/src/main/java/com/project/warehouse/config/OAuth2SecurityConfig.java

 1: package com.project.warehouse.config;
 2: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
 3: import org.springframework.security.config.annotation.web.builders.HttpSecurity;
 4: import org.springframework.security.config.annotation.web.configuration.WebSecurityConfigurerAdapter;
 5: import org.springframework.security.config.http.SessionCreationPolicy;
 6: @Configuration
 7: public class OAuth2SecurityConfig extends WebSecurityConfigurerAdapter {
 8:     @Override
 9:     public void configure(HttpSecurity http) throws Exception {
10:         http.csrf().disable()
11:                 .sessionManagement().sessionCreationPolicy(SessionCreationPolicy.IF_REQUIRED)
12:                 .and()
13:                 .authorizeRequests()
14:                 .antMatchers("/**").hasRole("EMPLOYEE");
15:     }
16: }

  1: #!/bin/sh
  2: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  3: # Licensed to the Apache Software Foundation (ASF) under one
  4: # or more contributor license agreements.  See the NOTICE file
  5: # distributed with this work for additional information
  6: # regarding copyright ownership.  The ASF licenses this file
  7: # to you under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the
  8: # "License"); you may not use this file except in compliance
  9: # with the License.  You may obtain a copy of the License at
 10: #
 11: #    http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
 12: #
 13: # Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing,
 14: # software distributed under the License is distributed on an
 15: # "AS IS" BASIS, WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY
 16: # KIND, either express or implied.  See the License for the
 17: # specific language governing permissions and limitations
 18: # under the License.
 19: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 20: 
 21: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 22: # Maven2 Start Up Batch script
 23: #
 24: # Required ENV vars:
 25: # ------------------
 26: #   JAVA_HOME - location of a JDK home dir
 27: #
 28: # Optional ENV vars
 29: # -----------------
 30: #   M2_HOME - location of maven2's installed home dir
 31: #   MAVEN_OPTS - parameters passed to the Java VM when running Maven
 32: #     e.g. to debug Maven itself, use
 33: #       set MAVEN_OPTS=-Xdebug -Xrunjdwp:transport=dt_socket,server=y,suspend=y,address=8000
 34: #   MAVEN_SKIP_RC - flag to disable loading of mavenrc files
 35: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 36: 
 37: if [ -z "$MAVEN_SKIP_RC" ] ; then
 38: 
 39:   if [ -f /etc/mavenrc ] ; then
 40:     . /etc/mavenrc
 41:   fi
 42: 
 43:   if [ -f "$HOME/.mavenrc" ] ; then
 44:     . "$HOME/.mavenrc"
 45:   fi
 46: 
 47: fi
 48: 
 49: # OS specific support.  $var _must_ be set to either true or false.
 50: cygwin=false;
 51: darwin=false;
 52: mingw=false
 53: case "`uname`" in
 54:   CYGWIN*) cygwin=true ;;
 55:   MINGW*) mingw=true;;
 56:   Darwin*) darwin=true
 57:     # Use /usr/libexec/java_home if available, otherwise fall back to /Library/Java/Home
 58:     # See https://developer.apple.com/library/mac/qa/qa1170/_index.html
 59:     if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ]; then
 60:       if [ -x "/usr/libexec/java_home" ]; then
 61:         export JAVA_HOME="`/usr/libexec/java_home`"
 62:       else
 63:         export JAVA_HOME="/Library/Java/Home"
 64:       fi
 65:     fi
 66:     ;;
 67: esac
 68: 
 69: if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ] ; then
 70:   if [ -r /etc/gentoo-release ] ; then
 71:     JAVA_HOME=`java-config --jre-home`
 72:   fi
 73: fi
 74: 
 75: if [ -z "$M2_HOME" ] ; then
 76:   ## resolve links - $0 may be a link to maven's home
 77:   PRG="$0"
 78: 
 79:   # need this for relative symlinks
 80:   while [ -h "$PRG" ] ; do
 81:     ls=`ls -ld "$PRG"`
 82:     link=`expr "$ls" : '.*-> \(.*\)$'`
 83:     if expr "$link" : '/.*' > /dev/null; then
 84:       PRG="$link"
 85:     else
 86:       PRG="`dirname "$PRG"`/$link"
 87:     fi
 88:   done
 89: 
 90:   saveddir=`pwd`
 91: 
 92:   M2_HOME=`dirname "$PRG"`/..
 93: 
 94:   # make it fully qualified
 95:   M2_HOME=`cd "$M2_HOME" && pwd`
 96: 
 97:   cd "$saveddir"
 98:   # echo Using m2 at $M2_HOME
 99: fi
100: 
101: # For Cygwin, ensure paths are in UNIX format before anything is touched
102: if $cygwin ; then
103:   [ -n "$M2_HOME" ] &&
104:     M2_HOME=`cygpath --unix "$M2_HOME"`
105:   [ -n "$JAVA_HOME" ] &&
106:     JAVA_HOME=`cygpath --unix "$JAVA_HOME"`
107:   [ -n "$CLASSPATH" ] &&
108:     CLASSPATH=`cygpath --path --unix "$CLASSPATH"`
109: fi
110: 
111: # For Migwn, ensure paths are in UNIX format before anything is touched
112: if $mingw ; then
113:   [ -n "$M2_HOME" ] &&
114:     M2_HOME="`(cd "$M2_HOME"; pwd)`"
115:   [ -n "$JAVA_HOME" ] &&
116:     JAVA_HOME="`(cd "$JAVA_HOME"; pwd)`"
117:   # TODO classpath?
118: fi
119: 
120: if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ]; then
121:   javaExecutable="`which javac`"
122:   if [ -n "$javaExecutable" ] && ! [ "`expr \"$javaExecutable\" : '\([^ ]*\)'`" = "no" ]; then
123:     # readlink(1) is not available as standard on Solaris 10.
124:     readLink=`which readlink`
125:     if [ ! `expr "$readLink" : '\([^ ]*\)'` = "no" ]; then
126:       if $darwin ; then
127:         javaHome="`dirname \"$javaExecutable\"`"
128:         javaExecutable="`cd \"$javaHome\" && pwd -P`/javac"
129:       else
130:         javaExecutable="`readlink -f \"$javaExecutable\"`"
131:       fi
132:       javaHome="`dirname \"$javaExecutable\"`"
133:       javaHome=`expr "$javaHome" : '\(.*\)/bin'`
134:       JAVA_HOME="$javaHome"
135:       export JAVA_HOME
136:     fi
137:   fi
138: fi
139: 
140: if [ -z "$JAVACMD" ] ; then
141:   if [ -n "$JAVA_HOME"  ] ; then
142:     if [ -x "$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java" ] ; then
143:       # IBM's JDK on AIX uses strange locations for the executables
144:       JAVACMD="$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java"
145:     else
146:       JAVACMD="$JAVA_HOME/bin/java"
147:     fi
148:   else
149:     JAVACMD="`which java`"
150:   fi
151: fi
152: 
153: if [ ! -x "$JAVACMD" ] ; then
154:   echo "Error: JAVA_HOME is not defined correctly." >&2
155:   echo "  We cannot execute $JAVACMD" >&2
156:   exit 1
157: fi
158: 
159: if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ] ; then
160:   echo "Warning: JAVA_HOME environment variable is not set."
161: fi
162: 
163: CLASSWORLDS_LAUNCHER=org.codehaus.plexus.classworlds.launcher.Launcher
164: 
165: # traverses directory structure from process work directory to filesystem root
166: # first directory with .mvn subdirectory is considered project base directory
167: find_maven_basedir() {
168: 
169:   if [ -z "$1" ]
170:   then
171:     echo "Path not specified to find_maven_basedir"
172:     return 1
173:   fi
174: 
175:   basedir="$1"
176:   wdir="$1"
177:   while [ "$wdir" != '/' ] ; do
178:     if [ -d "$wdir"/.mvn ] ; then
179:       basedir=$wdir
180:       break
181:     fi
182:     # workaround for JBEAP-8937 (on Solaris 10/Sparc)
183:     if [ -d "${wdir}" ]; then
184:       wdir=`cd "$wdir/.."; pwd`
185:     fi
186:     # end of workaround
187:   done
188:   echo "${basedir}"
189: }
190: 
191: # concatenates all lines of a file
192: concat_lines() {
193:   if [ -f "$1" ]; then
194:     echo "$(tr -s '\n' ' ' < "$1")"
195:   fi
196: }
197: 
198: BASE_DIR=`find_maven_basedir "$(pwd)"`
199: if [ -z "$BASE_DIR" ]; then
200:   exit 1;
201: fi
202: 
203: export MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=${MAVEN_BASEDIR:-"$BASE_DIR"}
204: echo $MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR
205: MAVEN_OPTS="$(concat_lines "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR/.mvn/jvm.config") $MAVEN_OPTS"
206: 
207: # For Cygwin, switch paths to Windows format before running java
208: if $cygwin; then
209:   [ -n "$M2_HOME" ] &&
210:     M2_HOME=`cygpath --path --windows "$M2_HOME"`
211:   [ -n "$JAVA_HOME" ] &&
212:     JAVA_HOME=`cygpath --path --windows "$JAVA_HOME"`
213:   [ -n "$CLASSPATH" ] &&
214:     CLASSPATH=`cygpath --path --windows "$CLASSPATH"`
215:   [ -n "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR" ] &&
216:     MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=`cygpath --path --windows "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR"`
217: fi
218: 
219: WRAPPER_LAUNCHER=org.apache.maven.wrapper.MavenWrapperMain
220: 
221: exec "$JAVACMD" \
222:   $MAVEN_OPTS \
223:   -classpath "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR/.mvn/wrapper/maven-wrapper.jar" \
224:   "-Dmaven.home=${M2_HOME}" "-Dmaven.multiModuleProjectDirectory=${MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR}" \
225:   ${WRAPPER_LAUNCHER} $MAVEN_CONFIG "$@"

================
File: product-catalog-service/mvnw.cmd
================
  1: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  2: @REM Licensed to the Apache Software Foundation (ASF) under one
  3: @REM or more contributor license agreements.  See the NOTICE file
  4: @REM distributed with this work for additional information
  5: @REM regarding copyright ownership.  The ASF licenses this file
  6: @REM to you under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the
  7: @REM "License"); you may not use this file except in compliance
  8: @REM with the License.  You may obtain a copy of the License at
  9: @REM
 10: @REM    http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
 11: @REM
 12: @REM Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing,
 13: @REM software distributed under the License is distributed on an
 14: @REM "AS IS" BASIS, WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY
 15: @REM KIND, either express or implied.  See the License for the
 16: @REM specific language governing permissions and limitations
 17: @REM under the License.
 18: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 19: 
 20: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 21: @REM Maven2 Start Up Batch script
 22: @REM
 23: @REM Required ENV vars:
 24: @REM JAVA_HOME - location of a JDK home dir
 25: @REM
 26: @REM Optional ENV vars
 27: @REM M2_HOME - location of maven2's installed home dir
 28: @REM MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO - set to 'on' to enable the echoing of the batch commands
 29: @REM MAVEN_BATCH_PAUSE - set to 'on' to wait for a key stroke before ending
 30: @REM MAVEN_OPTS - parameters passed to the Java VM when running Maven
 31: @REM     e.g. to debug Maven itself, use
 32: @REM set MAVEN_OPTS=-Xdebug -Xrunjdwp:transport=dt_socket,server=y,suspend=y,address=8000
 33: @REM MAVEN_SKIP_RC - flag to disable loading of mavenrc files
 34: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 35: 
 36: @REM Begin all REM lines with '@' in case MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO is 'on'
 37: @echo off
 38: @REM enable echoing my setting MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO to 'on'
 39: @if "%MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO%" == "on"  echo %MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO%
 40: 
 41: @REM set %HOME% to equivalent of $HOME
 42: if "%HOME%" == "" (set "HOME=%HOMEDRIVE%%HOMEPATH%")
 43: 
 44: @REM Execute a user defined script before this one
 45: if not "%MAVEN_SKIP_RC%" == "" goto skipRcPre
 46: @REM check for pre script, once with legacy .bat ending and once with .cmd ending
 47: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.bat" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.bat"
 48: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.cmd" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.cmd"
 49: :skipRcPre
 50: 
 51: @setlocal
 52: 
 53: set ERROR_CODE=0
 54: 
 55: @REM To isolate internal variables from possible post scripts, we use another setlocal
 56: @setlocal
 57: 
 58: @REM ==== START VALIDATION ====
 59: if not "%JAVA_HOME%" == "" goto OkJHome
 60: 
 61: echo.
 62: echo Error: JAVA_HOME not found in your environment. >&2
 63: echo Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the >&2
 64: echo location of your Java installation. >&2
 65: echo.
 66: goto error
 67: 
 68: :OkJHome
 69: if exist "%JAVA_HOME%\bin\java.exe" goto init
 70: 
 71: echo.
 72: echo Error: JAVA_HOME is set to an invalid directory. >&2
 73: echo JAVA_HOME = "%JAVA_HOME%" >&2
 74: echo Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the >&2
 75: echo location of your Java installation. >&2
 76: echo.
 77: goto error
 78: 
 79: @REM ==== END VALIDATION ====
 80: 
 81: :init
 82: 
 83: @REM Find the project base dir, i.e. the directory that contains the folder ".mvn".
 84: @REM Fallback to current working directory if not found.
 85: 
 86: set MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=%MAVEN_BASEDIR%
 87: IF NOT "%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%"=="" goto endDetectBaseDir
 88: 
 89: set EXEC_DIR=%CD%
 90: set WDIR=%EXEC_DIR%
 91: :findBaseDir
 92: IF EXIST "%WDIR%"\.mvn goto baseDirFound
 93: cd ..
 94: IF "%WDIR%"=="%CD%" goto baseDirNotFound
 95: set WDIR=%CD%
 96: goto findBaseDir
 97: 
 98: :baseDirFound
 99: set MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=%WDIR%
100: cd "%EXEC_DIR%"
101: goto endDetectBaseDir
102: 
103: :baseDirNotFound
104: set MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=%EXEC_DIR%
105: cd "%EXEC_DIR%"
106: 
107: :endDetectBaseDir
108: 
109: IF NOT EXIST "%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%\.mvn\jvm.config" goto endReadAdditionalConfig
110: 
111: @setlocal EnableExtensions EnableDelayedExpansion
112: for /F "usebackq delims=" %%a in ("%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%\.mvn\jvm.config") do set JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS=!JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS! %%a
113: @endlocal & set JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS=%JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS%
114: 
115: :endReadAdditionalConfig
116: 
117: SET MAVEN_JAVA_EXE="%JAVA_HOME%\bin\java.exe"
118: 
119: set WRAPPER_JAR="%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%\.mvn\wrapper\maven-wrapper.jar"
120: set WRAPPER_LAUNCHER=org.apache.maven.wrapper.MavenWrapperMain
121: 
122: %MAVEN_JAVA_EXE% %JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS% %MAVEN_OPTS% %MAVEN_DEBUG_OPTS% -classpath %WRAPPER_JAR% "-Dmaven.multiModuleProjectDirectory=%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%" %WRAPPER_LAUNCHER% %MAVEN_CONFIG% %*
123: if ERRORLEVEL 1 goto error
124: goto end
125: 
126: :error
127: set ERROR_CODE=1
128: 
129: :end
130: @endlocal & set ERROR_CODE=%ERROR_CODE%
131: 
132: if not "%MAVEN_SKIP_RC%" == "" goto skipRcPost
133: @REM check for post script, once with legacy .bat ending and once with .cmd ending
134: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.bat" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.bat"
135: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.cmd" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.cmd"
136: :skipRcPost
137: 
138: @REM pause the script if MAVEN_BATCH_PAUSE is set to 'on'
139: if "%MAVEN_BATCH_PAUSE%" == "on" pause
140: 
141: if "%MAVEN_TERMINATE_CMD%" == "on" exit %ERROR_CODE%
142: 
143: exit /B %ERROR_CODE%

================
File: product-catalog-service/src/main/java/com/project/warehouse/config/CassandraConfiguration.java
================
 1: package com.project.warehouse.config;
 2: import com.datastax.driver.core.Cluster;
 3: import org.springframework.beans.factory.BeanFactory;
 4: import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.cassandra.CassandraProperties;
 5: import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.data.cassandra.CassandraDataAutoConfiguration;
 6: import org.springframework.boot.context.properties.EnableConfigurationProperties;
 7: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
 8: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
 9: import org.springframework.core.env.Environment;
10: import org.springframework.data.cassandra.config.CassandraEntityClassScanner;
11: import org.springframework.data.cassandra.mapping.BasicCassandraMappingContext;
12: import org.springframework.data.cassandra.mapping.CassandraMappingContext;
13: import org.springframework.data.cassandra.mapping.SimpleUserTypeResolver;
14: @Configuration
15: @EnableConfigurationProperties(CassandraProperties.class)
16: public class CassandraConfiguration extends CassandraDataAutoConfiguration{
17:     public CassandraConfiguration(BeanFactory beanFactory,
18:                                   CassandraProperties properties,
19:                                   Cluster cluster, Environment environment) {
20:         super(beanFactory, properties, cluster, environment);
21:     }
22:     @Bean
23:     public CassandraMappingContext cassandraMappingContext(BeanFactory beanFactory, Cluster cluster, CassandraProperties properties) throws ClassNotFoundException {
24:         BasicCassandraMappingContext context = new BasicCassandraMappingContext();
25:         context.setInitialEntitySet(CassandraEntityClassScanner.scan("com.project.warehouse.event"));
26:         context.setUserTypeResolver(new SimpleUserTypeResolver(cluster,
27:                 properties.getKeyspaceName()));
28:         return context;
29:     }
30: }

File: product-catalog-service/src/main/java/com/project/warehouse/event/factory/DomainEventFactory.java
================
 1: package com.project.warehouse.event.factory;
 2: import com.datastax.driver.core.utils.UUIDs;
 3: import com.project.warehouse.event.model.*;
 4: import com.project.warehouse.security.SecurityUtils;
 5: import lombok.Data;
 6: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 7: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
 8: import java.util.Date;
 9: @Data
10: @Component
11: public class DomainEventFactory {
12:     @Autowired
13:     private SecurityUtils securityUtils;
14:     public ProductsEvent newGoodsEvent(String correlationId, String idGoods, String name, String barCode,
15:                                        String category, EventTypeEnum type){
16:         ProductsEvent event = new ProductsEvent();
17:         event.setId(UUIDs.timeBased());
18:         event.setBarCode(barCode);
19:         event.setName(name);
20:         event.setCategory(category);
21:         event.setIdProducts(idGoods);
22:         event.setType(type);
23:         event.setAuditData(newEventAuditData(correlationId));
24:         return event;
25:     }
26:     public ProductsErrorEvent newGoodsErrorEvent(String correlationId, String idGoods, String name, String barCode,
27:                                                  String category, EventTypeEnum type,
28:                                                  String message, Class exceptionClassName){
29:         ProductsErrorEvent event = new ProductsErrorEvent();
30:         event.setId(UUIDs.timeBased());
31:         event.setBarCode(barCode);
32:         event.setName(name);
33:         event.setCategory(category);
34:         event.setIdProducts(idGoods);
35:         event.setType(type);
36:         event.setAuditData(newEventAuditData(correlationId));
37:         event.setExceptionClassName(exceptionClassName.getName());
38:         event.setMessage(message);
39:         return event;
40:     }
41:     public PriceListEvent newPriceListEvent(String correlationId, String idPriceList,
42:                                             String name, EventTypeEnum type){
43:         PriceListEvent event = new PriceListEvent();
44:         event.setId(UUIDs.timeBased());
45:         event.setIdPriceList(idPriceList);
46:         event.setName(name);
47:         event.setType(type);
48:         event.setAuditData(newEventAuditData(correlationId));
49:         return event;
50:     }
51:     public PriceListErrorEvent newPriceListErrorEvent(String correlationId, String idPriceList,
52:                                                       String name, EventTypeEnum type,
53:                                                       String message, Class exceptionClassName){
54:         PriceListErrorEvent event = new PriceListErrorEvent();
55:         event.setId(UUIDs.timeBased());
56:         event.setIdPriceList(idPriceList);
57:         event.setName(name);
58:         event.setType(type);
59:         event.setAuditData(newEventAuditData(correlationId));
60:         event.setExceptionClassName(exceptionClassName.getName());
61:         event.setMessage(message);
62:         return event;
63:     }
64:     private EventAuditData newEventAuditData(String correlationId){
65:         EventAuditData eventAuditData = new EventAuditData();
66:         eventAuditData.setCorrelationId(correlationId);
67:         eventAuditData.setUserName(securityUtils.getPrincipalUserName());
68:         eventAuditData.setTimeStamp(new Date());
69:         return eventAuditData;
70:     }
71: }

File: product-catalog-service/src/main/java/com/project/warehouse/event/service/EventDomainPubblishService.java

 1: package com.project.warehouse.event.service;
 2: import com.project.warehouse.event.factory.DomainEventFactory;
 3: import com.project.warehouse.event.model.*;
 4: import com.project.warehouse.event.repository.PriceListErrorEventRepository;
 5: import com.project.warehouse.event.repository.PriceListEventRepository;
 6: import com.project.warehouse.event.repository.ProductsErrorEventRepository;
 7: import com.project.warehouse.event.repository.ProductsEventRepository;
 8: import lombok.Data;
 9: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
10: import org.springframework.cloud.stream.annotation.EnableBinding;
11: import org.springframework.messaging.SubscribableChannel;
12: import org.springframework.messaging.support.MessageBuilder;
13: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
14: @Data
15: @Service
16: public class EventDomainPubblishService {
17:     @Autowired
18:     private DomainEventFactory domainEventFactory;
19:     @Autowired
20:     private ProductsEventRepository goodsEventRepository;
21:     @Autowired
22:     private ProductsErrorEventRepository goodsErrorEventRepository;
23:     @Autowired
24:     private PriceListEventRepository priceListEventRepository;
25:     @Autowired
26:     private PriceListErrorEventRepository priceListErrorEventRepository;
27:     @Autowired
28:     private SubscribableChannel goodsEventOutboundChannel;
29:     @Autowired
30:     private SubscribableChannel priceListEventOutboundChannel;
31:     public ProductsEvent publishGoodsEvent(String correlationId, String idGoods, String name, String barCode,
32:                                            String category, EventTypeEnum type){
33:         ProductsEvent event = domainEventFactory.newGoodsEvent(correlationId, idGoods, name, barCode, category, type);
34:         goodsEventRepository.save(event);
35:         goodsEventOutboundChannel.send(MessageBuilder.withPayload(event).build());
36:         return event;
37:     }
38:     public ProductsErrorEvent publishGoodsErrorEvent(String correlationId, String idGoods, String name, String barCode,
39:                                                      String category, EventTypeEnum type,
40:                                                      String message, Class exceptionClassName){
41:         ProductsErrorEvent event = domainEventFactory.newGoodsErrorEvent(correlationId, idGoods, name, barCode, category, type,message,exceptionClassName);
42:         goodsErrorEventRepository.save(event);
43:         goodsEventOutboundChannel.send(MessageBuilder.withPayload(event).build());
44:         return event;
45:     }
46:     public PriceListEvent publishPriceListEvent(String correlationId, String idPriceList,
47:                                                 String name, EventTypeEnum type){
48:         PriceListEvent event = domainEventFactory.newPriceListEvent(correlationId, idPriceList, name, type);
49:         priceListEventRepository.save(event);
50:         priceListEventOutboundChannel.send(MessageBuilder.withPayload(event).build());
51:         return event;
52:     }
53:     public PriceListErrorEvent publishPriceListErrorEvent(String correlationId, String idPriceList,
54:                                                           String name, EventTypeEnum type,
55:                                                           String message, Class exceptionClassName){
56:         PriceListErrorEvent event = domainEventFactory.newPriceListErrorEvent(correlationId, idPriceList, name, type, message, exceptionClassName);
57:         priceListErrorEventRepository.save(event);
58:         priceListEventOutboundChannel.send(MessageBuilder.withPayload(event).build());
59:         return event;
60:     }
61: }

File: product-catalog-service/src/main/java/com/project/warehouse/hateoas/PriceListHateoasFactory.java
================
 1: package com.project.warehouse.hateoas;
 2: import com.project.warehouse.model.PriceList;
 3: import com.project.warehouse.rest.PriceListRestController;
 4: import org.springframework.hateoas.Link;
 5: import org.springframework.hateoas.Resource;
 6: import org.springframework.hateoas.Resources;
 7: import org.springframework.hateoas.UriTemplate;
 8: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
 9: import java.util.List;
10: import static org.springframework.hateoas.mvc.ControllerLinkBuilder.linkTo;
11: @Component
12: public class PriceListHateoasFactory {
13:     public Resource<PriceList> toResource(PriceList priceList) {
14:         Resource<PriceList> priceListResource = new Resource<>(priceList);
15:         Link selfLink = linkTo(PriceListRestController.class).slash(priceList.getId())
16:                 .withSelfRel();
17:         Link goodsInPriceListLink = linkTo(PriceListRestController.class)
18:                 .slash(priceList.getId()).slash("goods")
19:                 .withRel("goods-list-in-price-list");
20:         priceListResource.add(selfLink, goodsInPriceListLink);
21:         return priceListResource;
22:     }
23:     public Resources<PriceList> toResources(List<PriceList> priceList) {
24:         Resources<PriceList> priceListResource = new Resources<>(priceList);
25:         UriTemplate priceListLinkUriTemplate = new UriTemplate(String.format("%s/{idPriceList}",linkTo(PriceListRestController.class).toString()));
26:         Link priceListLink = new Link(priceListLinkUriTemplate, "price-list");
27:         UriTemplate goodsListInPriceListLinkUriTemplate = new UriTemplate(String.format("%s/{idPriceList}/goods",linkTo(PriceListRestController.class).toString()));
28:         Link goodsListInPriceListLink = new Link(goodsListInPriceListLinkUriTemplate, "goods-list-in-price-list");
29:         priceListResource.add(priceListLink,goodsListInPriceListLink);
30:         return priceListResource;
31:     }
32: }

 1: package com.project.warehouse.hateoas;
 2: import com.project.warehouse.model.Product;
 3: import com.project.warehouse.rest.ProductsRestController;
 4: import org.springframework.hateoas.Link;
 5: import org.springframework.hateoas.Resource;
 6: import org.springframework.hateoas.Resources;
 7: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
 8: import java.util.List;
 9: import static org.springframework.hateoas.mvc.ControllerLinkBuilder.linkTo;
10: @Component
11: public class ProductsHateoasFactory {
12:     public Resource<Product> toResource(Product goods) {
13:         Resource<Product> accountResource = new Resource<>(goods);
14:         Link selfLink = linkTo(ProductsRestController.class).slash(goods.getId())
15:                 .withSelfRel();

17:                 .withRel("category-attribute");

19:         return accountResource;
20:     }

22:         Resources<Product> accountResource = new Resources<>(goodsList);
23:         Link selfRel = linkTo(ProductsRestController.class).withSelfRel();
24:         accountResource.add(selfRel);
25:         return accountResource;
26:     }

 1: package com.project.warehouse.rest;
 2: import com.netflix.hystrix.contrib.javanica.annotation.HystrixCommand;
 3: import com.netflix.hystrix.contrib.javanica.annotation.HystrixProperty;
 4: import com.project.warehouse.hateoas.PriceListHateoasFactory;
 5: import com.project.warehouse.hateoas.ProductsInPriceListHateoasFactory;
 6: import com.project.warehouse.model.PriceList;
 7: import com.project.warehouse.service.PriceListService;
 8: import lombok.Data;
 9: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
10: import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
11: import org.springframework.security.access.prepost.PreAuthorize;
12: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;
13: import org.springframework.web.servlet.mvc.method.annotation.MvcUriComponentsBuilder;
14: import java.math.BigDecimal;
15: @Data
16: @RestController
17: @RequestMapping("/price-list")
18: public class PriceListRestController {
19:     @Autowired
20:     private PriceListService priceListService;
21:     @Autowired
22:     private PriceListHateoasFactory priceListHateoasFactory;
23:     @Autowired
24:     private ProductsInPriceListHateoasFactory goodsInPriceListHeateoasFactory;
25:     @GetMapping
26:     @HystrixCommand(commandProperties = {@HystrixProperty(name="execution.isolation.strategy", value="SEMAPHORE")})
27:     public ResponseEntity findPriceLists(@RequestParam(name = "withoutGoodsInPriceList", defaultValue = "false") boolean withoutGoodsInPriceList){
28:         return ResponseEntity.ok(priceListHateoasFactory.toResources(priceListService.findPriceLists(withoutGoodsInPriceList)));
29:     }
30:     @GetMapping("/{idPriceList}")
31:     @HystrixCommand(commandProperties = {@HystrixProperty(name="execution.isolation.strategy", value="SEMAPHORE")})
32:     public ResponseEntity findPriceList(@PathVariable String idPriceList){
33:         return ResponseEntity.ok(priceListHateoasFactory.toResource(priceListService.findPriceList(idPriceList)));
34:     }
35:     @GetMapping("/{idPriceList}/goods")
36:     @HystrixCommand(commandProperties = {@HystrixProperty(name="execution.isolation.strategy", value="SEMAPHORE")})
37:     public ResponseEntity findGoodsListInPriceList(@PathVariable String idPriceList){
38:         return ResponseEntity.ok(goodsInPriceListHeateoasFactory.toResources(idPriceList, priceListService.findGoodsListInPriceList(idPriceList)));
39:     }
40:     @GetMapping("/{idPriceList}/goods/{idGoods}")
41:     @HystrixCommand(commandProperties = {@HystrixProperty(name="execution.isolation.strategy", value="SEMAPHORE")})
42:     public ResponseEntity findGoodsInPriceList(@PathVariable String idPriceList, @PathVariable String idGoods){
43:         return ResponseEntity.ok(goodsInPriceListHeateoasFactory.toResource(idPriceList, priceListService.findGoodsInPriceList(idPriceList, idGoods)));
44:     }
45:     @PostMapping
46:     @PreAuthorize("hasRole('ROLE_EMPLOYEE')")
47:     @HystrixCommand(commandProperties = {@HystrixProperty(name="execution.isolation.strategy", value="SEMAPHORE")})
48:     public ResponseEntity createPriceList(@RequestBody PriceList priceList){
49:         PriceList priceListAux = priceListService.createPriceList(priceList);
50:         return ResponseEntity.created(MvcUriComponentsBuilder.fromMethodName(PriceListRestController.class,
51:                 "findPriceList", priceListAux.getId()).build().toUri()).build();
52:     }
53:     @PreAuthorize("hasRole('ROLE_EMPLOYEE')")
54:     @PatchMapping("/{idPriceList}/goods/{idGoods}")
55:     @HystrixCommand(commandProperties = {@HystrixProperty(name="execution.isolation.strategy", value="SEMAPHORE")})
56:     public ResponseEntity saveGoodsInPriceList(@PathVariable String idPriceList, @PathVariable  String idGoods, @RequestBody BigDecimal price){
57:         priceListService.saveGoodsInPriceList(idPriceList,idGoods,price);
58:         return ResponseEntity.noContent().build();
59:     }
60:     @PreAuthorize("hasRole('ROLE_EMPLOYEE')")
61:     @DeleteMapping("/{idPriceList}/goods/{idGoods}")
62:     @HystrixCommand(commandProperties = {@HystrixProperty(name="execution.isolation.strategy", value="SEMAPHORE")})
63:     public ResponseEntity removeGoodsInPriceList(@PathVariable String idPriceList, @PathVariable  String idGoods){
64:         priceListService.removeGoodsInPriceList(idPriceList,idGoods);
65:         return ResponseEntity.noContent().build();
66:     }
67:     @PutMapping("/{idPriceList}")
68:     @PreAuthorize("hasRole('ROLE_EMPLOYEE')")
69:     @HystrixCommand(commandProperties = {@HystrixProperty(name="execution.isolation.strategy", value="SEMAPHORE")})
70:     public ResponseEntity updatePriceList(@PathVariable String idPriceList, @RequestBody PriceList priceList){
71:         priceList.setId(idPriceList);
72:         priceListService.updatePriceList(priceList);
73:         return ResponseEntity.noContent().build();
74:     }
75:     @DeleteMapping("/{idPriceList}")
76:     @PreAuthorize("hasRole('ROLE_EMPLOYEE')")
77:     @HystrixCommand(commandProperties = {@HystrixProperty(name="execution.isolation.strategy", value="SEMAPHORE")})
78:     public ResponseEntity deletePriceList(@PathVariable String idPriceList){
79:         priceListService.deletePriceList(idPriceList);
80:         return ResponseEntity.noContent().build();
81:     }
82: }

 1: package com.project.warehouse.rest;
 2: import com.netflix.hystrix.contrib.javanica.annotation.HystrixCommand;
 3: import com.netflix.hystrix.contrib.javanica.annotation.HystrixProperty;
 4: import com.project.warehouse.hateoas.ProductsHateoasFactory;
 5: import com.project.warehouse.model.Product;
 6: import com.project.warehouse.service.ProductService;
 7: import lombok.Data;
 8: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 9: import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
10: import org.springframework.security.access.prepost.PreAuthorize;
11: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;
12: import org.springframework.web.servlet.mvc.method.annotation.MvcUriComponentsBuilder;
13: import java.net.URI;
14: import java.util.HashMap;
15: @Data
16: @RestController
17: @RequestMapping("/products")
18: public class ProductsRestController {
19:     @Autowired
20:     private ProductService goodsService;
21:     @Autowired
22:     private ProductsHateoasFactory goodsHateoasFactory;
23:     @GetMapping
24:     @HystrixCommand(commandProperties = {@HystrixProperty(name="execution.isolation.strategy", value="SEMAPHORE")})
25:     public ResponseEntity findAllGoods(){
26:         return ResponseEntity.ok(goodsHateoasFactory.toResources(goodsService.findProductsList()));
27:     }
28:     @GetMapping("/{idGoods}")
29:     @HystrixCommand(commandProperties = {@HystrixProperty(name="execution.isolation.strategy", value="SEMAPHORE")})
30:     public ResponseEntity findGoods(@PathVariable String idGoods){
31:         return ResponseEntity.ok(goodsHateoasFactory.toResource(goodsService.findProducts(idGoods)));
32:     }
33:     @PostMapping
34:     @PreAuthorize("hasRole('ROLE_EMPLOYEE')")
35:     @HystrixCommand(commandProperties = {@HystrixProperty(name="execution.isolation.strategy", value="SEMAPHORE")})
36:     public ResponseEntity createGoods(@RequestBody Product goods){
37:         Product goodsAux = goodsService.createProducts(goods);
38:         URI findGoods = MvcUriComponentsBuilder.fromMethodName(ProductsRestController.class,
39:                 "findGoods", goodsAux.getId()).build().toUri();
40:         return ResponseEntity.created(findGoods).build();
41:     }
42:     @PreAuthorize("hasRole('ROLE_EMPLOYEE')")
43:     @PatchMapping("/{idGoods}/category-attribute")
44:     @HystrixCommand(commandProperties = {@HystrixProperty(name="execution.isolation.strategy", value="SEMAPHORE")})
45:     public ResponseEntity saveGoodsAttributeValue(@PathVariable String idGoods, @RequestBody HashMap<String,String> goods){
46:         goods.entrySet().forEach(entry -> goodsService.saveProductsAttributeValue(idGoods,entry.getKey(), entry.getValue()));
47:         return ResponseEntity.noContent().build();
48:     }
49:     @PutMapping("/{idGoods}")
50:     @PreAuthorize("hasRole('ROLE_EMPLOYEE')")
51:     @HystrixCommand(commandProperties = {@HystrixProperty(name="execution.isolation.strategy", value="SEMAPHORE")})
52:     public ResponseEntity updateGoods(@PathVariable String idGoods, @RequestBody Product goods){
53:         goods.setId(idGoods);
54:         goodsService.updateProducts(goods);
55:         return ResponseEntity.noContent().build();
56:     }
57:     @DeleteMapping("/{idGoods}")
58:     @PreAuthorize("hasRole('ROLE_EMPLOYEE')")
59:     @HystrixCommand(commandProperties = {@HystrixProperty(name="execution.isolation.strategy", value="SEMAPHORE")})
60:     public ResponseEntity deleteGoods(@PathVariable String idGoods){
61:         goodsService.deleteProducts(idGoods);
62:         return ResponseEntity.noContent().build();
63:     }
64:     @PreAuthorize("hasRole('ROLE_EMPLOYEE')")
65:     @DeleteMapping("/{idGoods}/category-attribute")
66:     @HystrixCommand(commandProperties = {@HystrixProperty(name="execution.isolation.strategy", value="SEMAPHORE")})
67:     public ResponseEntity removeGoodsAttributeValue(@PathVariable String idGoods, @PathVariable String goodsAttributeKey){
68:         goodsService.removeProductsAttributeValue(idGoods, goodsAttributeKey);
69:         return ResponseEntity.noContent().build();
70:     }
71: }

File: product-catalog-service/src/main/java/com/project/warehouse/security/SecurityOAuth2ResourceServerConfig.java
================
 1: package com.project.warehouse.security;
 2: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
 3: import org.springframework.security.config.annotation.web.builders.HttpSecurity;
 4: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.config.annotation.web.configuration.EnableResourceServer;
 5: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.config.annotation.web.configuration.ResourceServerConfigurerAdapter;
 6: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.config.annotation.web.configurers.AuthorizationServerSecurityConfigurer;
 7: import org.springframework.security.oauth2.provider.error.OAuth2AccessDeniedHandler;
 8: @Configuration
 9: @EnableResourceServer
10: public class SecurityOAuth2ResourceServerConfig extends ResourceServerConfigurerAdapter {
11:     public void configure(AuthorizationServerSecurityConfigurer oauthServer) throws Exception {
12:         oauthServer.tokenKeyAccess("permitAll()")
13:                 .checkTokenAccess("isAuthenticated()")
14:                 .allowFormAuthenticationForClients();
15:     }
16:     @Override
17:     public void configure(HttpSecurity http) throws Exception {
18:         http.httpBasic().disable().authorizeRequests()
19:                 .antMatchers("/products/**","/price-list/**").permitAll()
20:                 .anyRequest().authenticated()
21:                 .and().exceptionHandling().accessDeniedHandler(new OAuth2AccessDeniedHandler());
22:     }
23: }

 1: package com.project.warehouse.security;
 2: import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
 3: import org.springframework.security.core.context.SecurityContextHolder;
 4: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
 5: @Slf4j
 6: @Component
 7: public class SecurityUtils {
 8:     public String getPrincipalUserName(){
 9:         String userName = "";
10:         try{
11:             userName = SecurityContextHolder.getContext().getAuthentication().getName();
12:         } catch (Throwable t){
13:             log.error("session without an authenticated user");
14:         }
15:         return userName;
16:     }

File: product-catalog-service/src/main/java/com/project/warehouse/service/AbstractService.java
================
 1: package com.project.warehouse.service;
 2: import com.project.warehouse.event.model.EventTypeEnum;
 3: import com.project.warehouse.event.model.PriceListErrorEvent;
 4: import com.project.warehouse.event.model.ProductsErrorEvent;
 5: import com.project.warehouse.event.service.EventDomainPubblishService;
 6: import com.project.warehouse.exception.PriceListNotFoundException;
 7: import com.project.warehouse.exception.ProductsNotFoundException;
 8: import com.project.warehouse.exception.SavePriceListException;
 9: import com.project.warehouse.exception.SaveProductsException;
10: import com.project.warehouse.model.PriceList;
11: import com.project.warehouse.model.Product;
12: import com.project.warehouse.model.ProductsInPriceList;
13: import com.project.warehouse.repository.PriceListRepository;
14: import com.project.warehouse.repository.ProductRepository;
15: import com.project.warehouse.validator.PriceListDataValidator;
16: import lombok.Data;
17: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
18: import java.util.*;
19: import java.util.function.Function;
20: import lombok.Data;
21: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
22: import java.util.*;
23: import java.util.function.Function;
24: @Data
25: public abstract class AbstractService {
26:     @Autowired
27:     protected PriceListDataValidator priceListDataValidator;
28:     @Autowired
29:     protected EventDomainPubblishService eventDomainPubblishService;
30:     @Autowired
31:     protected PriceListRepository priceListRepository;
32:     @Autowired
33:     protected ProductRepository productRepository;
34:     protected Function<Product, Map<String, String>> getSafeGoodsAttribute =
35:             (goods) -> Optional.ofNullable(goods.getProductsAttribute()).orElse(new HashMap<>());
36:     protected Function<PriceList, List<ProductsInPriceList>> getSafeGoodsInPriceList =
37:             (priceList) -> Optional.ofNullable(priceList.getProductsInPriceList()).orElse(new ArrayList<>());
38:     protected Product doSaveGoodsData(String correlationId, Product goods) {
39:         Product goodsAux;
40:         ProductsErrorEvent goodsErrorEvent;
41:         try{
42:             goodsAux = productRepository.save(goods);
43:             eventDomainPubblishService.publishGoodsEvent(correlationId,goods.getId(),
44:                     goods.getName(),goods.getBarCode(),goods.getCategory(), EventTypeEnum.SAVE);
45:         } catch (Exception e){
46:             e.printStackTrace();
47:             goodsErrorEvent = eventDomainPubblishService.publishGoodsErrorEvent(correlationId, goods.getId(), goods.getName(),goods.getBarCode(),
48:                     goods.getCategory(), EventTypeEnum.SAVE, SaveProductsException.DEFAULT_MESSAGE, SaveProductsException.class);
49:             throw  new SaveProductsException(goodsErrorEvent, SaveProductsException.DEFAULT_MESSAGE);
50:         }
51:         return goodsAux;
52:     }
53:     protected PriceList doSavePriceListData(String correlationId, PriceList priceList) {
54:         PriceList priceListAux;
55:         PriceListErrorEvent priceListErrorEvent ;
56:         try{
57:             priceListAux = priceListRepository.save(priceList);
58:             eventDomainPubblishService.publishPriceListEvent(correlationId,priceList.getId(),
59:                     priceList.getName(),EventTypeEnum.SAVE);
60:         } catch (Exception e){
61:             priceListErrorEvent = eventDomainPubblishService.publishPriceListErrorEvent(correlationId, priceList.getId(), priceList.getName(),EventTypeEnum.SAVE, SavePriceListException.DEFAULT_MESSAGE, SavePriceListException.class);
62:             throw  new SavePriceListException(priceListErrorEvent, SavePriceListException.DEFAULT_MESSAGE);
63:         }
64:         return priceListAux;
65:     }
66:     protected void doCheckGoodsExist(String correlationId, String idGoods) {
67:         Product goodsAux;
68:         Function<String, ProductsNotFoundException> f = userNameAux -> {
69:             ProductsErrorEvent goodsErrorEvent = eventDomainPubblishService.publishGoodsErrorEvent(correlationId, idGoods,
70:                     null, null, null, EventTypeEnum.READ, ProductsNotFoundException.DEFAULT_MESSAGE, ProductsNotFoundException.class);
71:             return new ProductsNotFoundException(goodsErrorEvent, ProductsNotFoundException.DEFAULT_MESSAGE);
72:         };
73:         try{
74:             goodsAux =  productRepository.findOne(idGoods);
75:             if(goodsAux== null){
76:                 throw f.apply(idGoods);
77:             }
78:         } catch (Exception e){
79:             throw f.apply(idGoods);
80:         }
81:     }
82:     protected void doCheckPriceListExist(String correlationId, String idPriceList) {
83:         PriceList priceListAux;
84:         Function<String, PriceListNotFoundException> f = userNameAux -> {
85:             PriceListErrorEvent priceListErrorEvent = eventDomainPubblishService.publishPriceListErrorEvent(correlationId, idPriceList,
86:                     null, EventTypeEnum.READ, PriceListNotFoundException.DEFAULT_MESSAGE, ProductsNotFoundException.class);
87:             return new PriceListNotFoundException(priceListErrorEvent, PriceListNotFoundException.DEFAULT_MESSAGE);
88:         };
89:         try{
90:             priceListAux =  priceListRepository.findOne(idPriceList);
91:             if(priceListAux== null){
92:                 throw f.apply(idPriceList);
93:             }
94:         } catch (Exception e){
95:             throw f.apply(idPriceList);
96:         }
97:     }
98: }

================
File: product-catalog-service/src/main/java/com/project/warehouse/service/impl/PriceListServiceImpl.java
================
  1: package com.project.warehouse.service.impl;
  2: import com.project.warehouse.event.model.EventTypeEnum;
  3: import com.project.warehouse.exception.ProductsInPriceListNotFoundException;
  4: import com.project.warehouse.model.PriceList;
  5: import com.project.warehouse.model.Product;
  6: import com.project.warehouse.model.ProductsInPriceList;
  7: import com.project.warehouse.service.AbstractService;
  8: import com.project.warehouse.service.PriceListService;
  9: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
 10: import java.math.BigDecimal;
 11: import java.util.ArrayList;
 12: import java.util.List;
 13: import java.util.Optional;
 14: import java.util.UUID;
 15: @Service
 16: public class PriceListServiceImpl extends AbstractService implements PriceListService {
 17:     @Override
 18:     public PriceList createPriceList(PriceList priceList) {
 19:         String correlationId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
 20:         priceList.setProductsInPriceList(Optional.ofNullable(priceList.getProductsInPriceList()).orElse(new ArrayList<>()));
 21:         priceListDataValidator.validate(correlationId, priceList);
 22:         PriceList save = doSavePriceListData(correlationId, priceList);
 23:         eventDomainPubblishService.publishPriceListEvent(correlationId,priceList.getId(),
 24:                 priceList.getName(), EventTypeEnum.CREATE);
 25:         return save;
 26:     }
 27:     public List<PriceList> findPriceLists(boolean withoutGoodsInPriceList) {
 28:         return withoutGoodsInPriceList ? priceListRepository.findAllWithoutProductsInPriceList() : priceListRepository.findAll();
 29:     }
 30:     @Override
 31:     public PriceList findPriceList(String idPriceList) {
 32:         doCheckPriceListExist(UUID.randomUUID().toString(), idPriceList);
 33:         return priceListRepository.findOne(idPriceList);
 34:     }
 35:     @Override
 36:     public List<ProductsInPriceList> findGoodsListInPriceList(String idPriceList) {
 37:         String correlationId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
 38:         doCheckPriceListExist(correlationId, idPriceList);
 39:         return getSafeGoodsInPriceList.apply(priceListRepository.findOne(idPriceList));
 40:     }
 41:     @Override
 42:     public ProductsInPriceList findGoodsInPriceList(String idPriceList, String idGoods) {
 43:         String correlationId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
 44:         doCheckPriceListExist(correlationId, idPriceList);
 45:         doCheckGoodsExist(correlationId, idGoods);
 46:         ProductsInPriceList goodsInPriceListAux = getSafeGoodsInPriceList.apply(priceListRepository.findOne(idPriceList)).
 47:                 stream().filter(goodsInPriceList -> goodsInPriceList.getProducts().getId().equals(idGoods))
 48:                 .findFirst().orElse(null);
 49:         if(goodsInPriceListAux == null){
 50:             throw new ProductsInPriceListNotFoundException(ProductsInPriceListNotFoundException.DEFAULT_MESSAGE);
 51:         }
 52:         return goodsInPriceListAux;
 53:     }
 54:     @Override
 55:     public PriceList saveGoodsInPriceList(String idPriceList, String idGoods, BigDecimal price) {
 56:         String correlationId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
 57:         doCheckPriceListExist(correlationId, idPriceList);
 58:         doCheckGoodsExist(correlationId, idGoods);
 59:         PriceList priceList = priceListRepository.findOne(idPriceList);
 60:         Product goods  = productRepository.findOne(idGoods);
 61:         List<ProductsInPriceList> goodsInPriceListAux = getSafeGoodsInPriceList.apply(priceList);
 62:         ProductsInPriceList goodsInPriceList = new ProductsInPriceList();
 63:         goodsInPriceList.setProducts(goods);
 64:         goodsInPriceList.setPrice(price);
 65:         int index = goodsInPriceListAux.indexOf(goodsInPriceList);
 66:         if(index == -1){
 67:             goodsInPriceListAux.add(goodsInPriceList);
 68:         } else {
 69:             goodsInPriceListAux.set(index, goodsInPriceList);
 70:         }
 71:         PriceList priceListAux = doSavePriceListData(correlationId, priceList);
 72:         eventDomainPubblishService.publishPriceListEvent(correlationId,priceList.getId(),
 73:                 priceList.getName(), EventTypeEnum.UPDATE);
 74:         return priceListAux;
 75:     }
 76:     @Override
 77:     public PriceList removeGoodsInPriceList(String idPriceList, String idGoods) {
 78:         String correlationId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
 79:         doCheckPriceListExist(correlationId, idPriceList);
 80:         doCheckGoodsExist(correlationId, idGoods);
 81:         PriceList priceList = priceListRepository.findOne(idPriceList);
 82:         Product goods  = productRepository.findOne(idGoods);
 83:         List<ProductsInPriceList> goodsInPriceListAux = getSafeGoodsInPriceList.apply(priceList);
 84:         ProductsInPriceList goodsInPriceList = new ProductsInPriceList();
 85:         goodsInPriceList.setProducts(goods);
 86:         goodsInPriceListAux.remove(goodsInPriceList);
 87:         PriceList priceListAux = doSavePriceListData(correlationId, priceList);
 88:         eventDomainPubblishService.publishPriceListEvent(correlationId,priceList.getId(),
 89:                 priceList.getName(), EventTypeEnum.UPDATE);
 90:         return priceListAux;
 91:     }
 92:     @Override
 93:     public PriceList updatePriceList(PriceList priceList) {
 94:         String correlationId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
 95:         doCheckPriceListExist(correlationId, correlationId);
 96:         return priceListRepository.save(priceList);
 97:     }
 98:     @Override
 99:     public void deletePriceList(String idPriceList) {
100:         String correlationId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
101:         doCheckPriceListExist(correlationId, idPriceList);
102:         priceListRepository.delete(idPriceList);
103:     }
104: }

================
File: product-catalog-service/src/main/java/com/project/warehouse/service/impl/ProductServiceImpl.java
================
 1: package com.project.warehouse.service.impl;
 2: import com.project.warehouse.event.model.EventTypeEnum;
 3: import com.project.warehouse.model.PriceList;
 4: import com.project.warehouse.model.Product;
 5: import com.project.warehouse.service.AbstractService;
 6: import com.project.warehouse.service.ProductService;
 7: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
 8: import java.util.List;
 9: import java.util.Map;
10: import java.util.UUID;
11: @Service
12: public class ProductServiceImpl extends AbstractService implements ProductService {
13:     @Override
14:     public Product createProducts(Product goods) {
15:         String correlationId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
16:         priceListDataValidator.validate(correlationId, goods);
17:         Product save = doSaveGoodsData(correlationId, goods);
18:         eventDomainPubblishService.publishGoodsEvent(correlationId,goods.getId(), goods.getName(),
19:                 goods.getBarCode(), goods.getCategory(), EventTypeEnum.CREATE);
20:         return save;
21:     }
22:     @Override
23:     public List<Product> findProductsList() {
24:         return productRepository.findAll();
25:     }
26:     @Override
27:     public List<Product> findProductsList(String goodsCategory) {
28:         return productRepository.findByCategory(goodsCategory);
29:     }
30:     @Override
31:     public Product findProducts(String idGoods) {
32:         doCheckGoodsExist(UUID.randomUUID().toString(), idGoods);
33:         return productRepository.findOne(idGoods);
34:     }
35:     @Override
36:     public Product saveProductsAttributeValue(String idGoods, String goodsAttributeKey, String goodsAttributeValue) {
37:         String correlationId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
38:         doCheckGoodsExist(correlationId, idGoods);
39:         Product goods = productRepository.findOne(idGoods);
40:         Map<String, String> stringStringMap = getSafeGoodsAttribute.apply(goods);
41:         stringStringMap.put(goodsAttributeKey, goodsAttributeValue);
42:         goods.setProductsAttribute(stringStringMap);
43:         return doSaveGoodsData(correlationId, goods);
44:     }
45:     @Override
46:     public Product removeProductsAttributeValue(String idGoods, String goodsAttributeKey) {
47:         String correlationId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
48:         doCheckGoodsExist(correlationId, idGoods);
49:         Product goods = productRepository.findOne(idGoods);
50:         Map<String, String> stringStringMap = getSafeGoodsAttribute.apply(goods);
51:         stringStringMap.remove(goodsAttributeKey);
52:         goods.setProductsAttribute(stringStringMap);
53:         return doSaveGoodsData(correlationId, goods);
54:     }
55:     @Override
56:     public Product updateProducts(Product goods) {
57:         String correlationId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
58:         priceListDataValidator.validate(correlationId, goods);
59:         doCheckGoodsExist(correlationId, goods.getId());
60:         Product save = doSaveGoodsData(correlationId, goods);
61:         eventDomainPubblishService.publishGoodsEvent(correlationId,goods.getId(), goods.getName(),
62:                 goods.getBarCode(), goods.getCategory(), EventTypeEnum.UPDATE);
63:         return save;
64:     }
65:     @Override
66:     public void deleteProducts(String idGoods) {
67:         String correlationId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
68:         doCheckGoodsExist(correlationId, idGoods);
69:         Product one = productRepository.findOne(idGoods);
70:         productRepository.delete(idGoods);
71:         eventDomainPubblishService.publishGoodsEvent(correlationId, idGoods, one.getName(),one.getBarCode(),
72:                 one.getCategory(),EventTypeEnum.DELETE);
73:     }
74: }

================
File: product-catalog-service/src/main/java/com/project/warehouse/validator/PriceListDataValidatorImpl.java
================
 1: package com.project.warehouse.validator;
 2: import com.project.warehouse.exception.PriceListValidationException;
 3: import com.project.warehouse.exception.ProductsValidationException;
 4: import com.project.warehouse.model.PriceList;
 5: import com.project.warehouse.model.Product;
 6: import com.project.warehouse.model.ProductsInPriceList;
 7: import lombok.Data;
 8: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 9: import org.springframework.context.MessageSource;
10: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
11: import java.util.HashMap;
12: import java.util.Locale;
13: import java.util.Map;
14: import java.util.function.Predicate;
15: @Data
16: @Component
17: public class PriceListDataValidatorImpl implements PriceListDataValidator {
18:     @Autowired
19:     protected MessageSource messageSource;
20:     @Override
21:     public void validate(String correlationId, PriceList priceList) throws PriceListValidationException {
22:         Map<String,String> errors = new HashMap<>();
23:         validateNotNullAndNotEmpty("priceListName", priceList.getName(), "PriceListDataValidator.PriceList.name", errors);
24:     }
25:     @Override
26:     public void validate(String correlationId, ProductsInPriceList goodsInPriceList) throws PriceListValidationException {
27:         goodsPriceError(goodsInPriceList);
28:     }
29:     @Override
30:     public void validate(String correlationId, Product goods) throws ProductsValidationException {
31:         Map<String,String> errors = new HashMap<>();
32:         validateNotNullAndNotEmpty("goodsName", goods.getName(), "PriceListDataValidator.Goods.name", errors);
33:         validateNotNullAndNotEmpty("goodsBarCode", goods.getBarCode(), "PriceListDataValidator.Goods.barCode", errors);
34:     }
35:     private void validateNotNullAndNotEmpty(String key,String value,String validationMessageKey, Map<String,String> errors){
36:         if (value == null || "".equals(value)){
37:             errors.put(key, messageSource.getMessage(validationMessageKey,new Object[]{}, Locale.ENGLISH));
38:         }
39:     }
40:     Predicate<ProductsInPriceList> priceIsInvalid = item -> item.getPrice()!= null || item.getPrice().doubleValue() < 0;
41:     private String goodsPriceError(ProductsInPriceList itemAux){
42:         return messageSource.getMessage("PriceListDataValidator.Goods.invalidPrice",
43:                 new Object[]{itemAux.getProducts().getName(), itemAux.getProducts().getBarCode()}, Locale.ENGLISH);
44:     }
45: }

File: spring-boot-admin/mvnw
================
  1: #!/bin/sh
  2: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  3: # Licensed to the Apache Software Foundation (ASF) under one
  4: # or more contributor license agreements.  See the NOTICE file
  5: # distributed with this work for additional information
  6: # regarding copyright ownership.  The ASF licenses this file
  7: # to you under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the
  8: # "License"); you may not use this file except in compliance
  9: # with the License.  You may obtain a copy of the License at
 10: #
 11: #    http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
 12: #
 13: # Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing,
 14: # software distributed under the License is distributed on an
 15: # "AS IS" BASIS, WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY
 16: # KIND, either express or implied.  See the License for the
 17: # specific language governing permissions and limitations
 18: # under the License.
 19: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 20: 
 21: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 22: # Maven2 Start Up Batch script
 23: #
 24: # Required ENV vars:
 25: # ------------------
 26: #   JAVA_HOME - location of a JDK home dir
 27: #
 28: # Optional ENV vars
 29: # -----------------
 30: #   M2_HOME - location of maven2's installed home dir
 31: #   MAVEN_OPTS - parameters passed to the Java VM when running Maven
 32: #     e.g. to debug Maven itself, use
 33: #       set MAVEN_OPTS=-Xdebug -Xrunjdwp:transport=dt_socket,server=y,suspend=y,address=8000
 34: #   MAVEN_SKIP_RC - flag to disable loading of mavenrc files
 35: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 36: 
 37: if [ -z "$MAVEN_SKIP_RC" ] ; then
 38: 
 39:   if [ -f /etc/mavenrc ] ; then
 40:     . /etc/mavenrc
 41:   fi
 42: 
 43:   if [ -f "$HOME/.mavenrc" ] ; then
 44:     . "$HOME/.mavenrc"
 45:   fi
 46: 
 47: fi
 48: 
 49: # OS specific support.  $var _must_ be set to either true or false.
 50: cygwin=false;
 51: darwin=false;
 52: mingw=false
 53: case "`uname`" in
 54:   CYGWIN*) cygwin=true ;;
 55:   MINGW*) mingw=true;;
 56:   Darwin*) darwin=true
 57:     # Use /usr/libexec/java_home if available, otherwise fall back to /Library/Java/Home
 58:     # See https://developer.apple.com/library/mac/qa/qa1170/_index.html
 59:     if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ]; then
 60:       if [ -x "/usr/libexec/java_home" ]; then
 61:         export JAVA_HOME="`/usr/libexec/java_home`"
 62:       else
 63:         export JAVA_HOME="/Library/Java/Home"
 64:       fi
 65:     fi
 66:     ;;
 67: esac
 68: 
 69: if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ] ; then
 70:   if [ -r /etc/gentoo-release ] ; then
 71:     JAVA_HOME=`java-config --jre-home`
 72:   fi
 73: fi
 74: 
 75: if [ -z "$M2_HOME" ] ; then
 76:   ## resolve links - $0 may be a link to maven's home
 77:   PRG="$0"
 78: 
 79:   # need this for relative symlinks
 80:   while [ -h "$PRG" ] ; do
 81:     ls=`ls -ld "$PRG"`
 82:     link=`expr "$ls" : '.*-> \(.*\)$'`
 83:     if expr "$link" : '/.*' > /dev/null; then
 84:       PRG="$link"
 85:     else
 86:       PRG="`dirname "$PRG"`/$link"
 87:     fi
 88:   done
 89: 
 90:   saveddir=`pwd`
 91: 
 92:   M2_HOME=`dirname "$PRG"`/..
 93: 
 94:   # make it fully qualified
 95:   M2_HOME=`cd "$M2_HOME" && pwd`
 96: 
 97:   cd "$saveddir"
 98:   # echo Using m2 at $M2_HOME
 99: fi
100: 
101: # For Cygwin, ensure paths are in UNIX format before anything is touched
102: if $cygwin ; then
103:   [ -n "$M2_HOME" ] &&
104:     M2_HOME=`cygpath --unix "$M2_HOME"`
105:   [ -n "$JAVA_HOME" ] &&
106:     JAVA_HOME=`cygpath --unix "$JAVA_HOME"`
107:   [ -n "$CLASSPATH" ] &&
108:     CLASSPATH=`cygpath --path --unix "$CLASSPATH"`
109: fi
110: 
111: # For Migwn, ensure paths are in UNIX format before anything is touched
112: if $mingw ; then
113:   [ -n "$M2_HOME" ] &&
114:     M2_HOME="`(cd "$M2_HOME"; pwd)`"
115:   [ -n "$JAVA_HOME" ] &&
116:     JAVA_HOME="`(cd "$JAVA_HOME"; pwd)`"
117:   # TODO classpath?
118: fi
119: 
120: if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ]; then
121:   javaExecutable="`which javac`"
122:   if [ -n "$javaExecutable" ] && ! [ "`expr \"$javaExecutable\" : '\([^ ]*\)'`" = "no" ]; then
123:     # readlink(1) is not available as standard on Solaris 10.
124:     readLink=`which readlink`
125:     if [ ! `expr "$readLink" : '\([^ ]*\)'` = "no" ]; then
126:       if $darwin ; then
127:         javaHome="`dirname \"$javaExecutable\"`"
128:         javaExecutable="`cd \"$javaHome\" && pwd -P`/javac"
129:       else
130:         javaExecutable="`readlink -f \"$javaExecutable\"`"
131:       fi
132:       javaHome="`dirname \"$javaExecutable\"`"
133:       javaHome=`expr "$javaHome" : '\(.*\)/bin'`
134:       JAVA_HOME="$javaHome"
135:       export JAVA_HOME
136:     fi
137:   fi
138: fi
139: 
140: if [ -z "$JAVACMD" ] ; then
141:   if [ -n "$JAVA_HOME"  ] ; then
142:     if [ -x "$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java" ] ; then
143:       # IBM's JDK on AIX uses strange locations for the executables
144:       JAVACMD="$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java"
145:     else
146:       JAVACMD="$JAVA_HOME/bin/java"
147:     fi
148:   else
149:     JAVACMD="`which java`"
150:   fi
151: fi
152: 
153: if [ ! -x "$JAVACMD" ] ; then
154:   echo "Error: JAVA_HOME is not defined correctly." >&2
155:   echo "  We cannot execute $JAVACMD" >&2
156:   exit 1
157: fi
158: 
159: if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ] ; then
160:   echo "Warning: JAVA_HOME environment variable is not set."
161: fi
162: 
163: CLASSWORLDS_LAUNCHER=org.codehaus.plexus.classworlds.launcher.Launcher
164: 
165: # traverses directory structure from process work directory to filesystem root
166: # first directory with .mvn subdirectory is considered project base directory
167: find_maven_basedir() {
168: 
169:   if [ -z "$1" ]
170:   then
171:     echo "Path not specified to find_maven_basedir"
172:     return 1
173:   fi
174: 
175:   basedir="$1"
176:   wdir="$1"
177:   while [ "$wdir" != '/' ] ; do
178:     if [ -d "$wdir"/.mvn ] ; then
179:       basedir=$wdir
180:       break
181:     fi
182:     # workaround for JBEAP-8937 (on Solaris 10/Sparc)
183:     if [ -d "${wdir}" ]; then
184:       wdir=`cd "$wdir/.."; pwd`
185:     fi
186:     # end of workaround
187:   done
188:   echo "${basedir}"
189: }
190: 
191: # concatenates all lines of a file
192: concat_lines() {
193:   if [ -f "$1" ]; then
194:     echo "$(tr -s '\n' ' ' < "$1")"
195:   fi
196: }
197: 
198: BASE_DIR=`find_maven_basedir "$(pwd)"`
199: if [ -z "$BASE_DIR" ]; then
200:   exit 1;
201: fi
202: 
203: export MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=${MAVEN_BASEDIR:-"$BASE_DIR"}
204: echo $MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR
205: MAVEN_OPTS="$(concat_lines "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR/.mvn/jvm.config") $MAVEN_OPTS"
206: 
207: # For Cygwin, switch paths to Windows format before running java
208: if $cygwin; then
209:   [ -n "$M2_HOME" ] &&
210:     M2_HOME=`cygpath --path --windows "$M2_HOME"`
211:   [ -n "$JAVA_HOME" ] &&
212:     JAVA_HOME=`cygpath --path --windows "$JAVA_HOME"`
213:   [ -n "$CLASSPATH" ] &&
214:     CLASSPATH=`cygpath --path --windows "$CLASSPATH"`
215:   [ -n "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR" ] &&
216:     MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=`cygpath --path --windows "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR"`
217: fi
218: 
219: WRAPPER_LAUNCHER=org.apache.maven.wrapper.MavenWrapperMain
220: 
221: exec "$JAVACMD" \
222:   $MAVEN_OPTS \
223:   -classpath "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR/.mvn/wrapper/maven-wrapper.jar" \
224:   "-Dmaven.home=${M2_HOME}" "-Dmaven.multiModuleProjectDirectory=${MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR}" \
225:   ${WRAPPER_LAUNCHER} $MAVEN_CONFIG "$@"

File: spring-boot-admin/mvnw.cmd

  1: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  2: @REM Licensed to the Apache Software Foundation (ASF) under one
  3: @REM or more contributor license agreements.  See the NOTICE file
  4: @REM distributed with this work for additional information
  5: @REM regarding copyright ownership.  The ASF licenses this file
  6: @REM to you under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the
  7: @REM "License"); you may not use this file except in compliance
  8: @REM with the License.  You may obtain a copy of the License at
  9: @REM
 10: @REM    http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
 11: @REM
 12: @REM Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing,
 13: @REM software distributed under the License is distributed on an
 14: @REM "AS IS" BASIS, WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY
 15: @REM KIND, either express or implied.  See the License for the
 16: @REM specific language governing permissions and limitations
 17: @REM under the License.
 18: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 19: 
 20: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 21: @REM Maven2 Start Up Batch script
 22: @REM
 23: @REM Required ENV vars:
 24: @REM JAVA_HOME - location of a JDK home dir
 25: @REM
 26: @REM Optional ENV vars
 27: @REM M2_HOME - location of maven2's installed home dir
 28: @REM MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO - set to 'on' to enable the echoing of the batch commands
 29: @REM MAVEN_BATCH_PAUSE - set to 'on' to wait for a key stroke before ending
 30: @REM MAVEN_OPTS - parameters passed to the Java VM when running Maven
 31: @REM     e.g. to debug Maven itself, use
 32: @REM set MAVEN_OPTS=-Xdebug -Xrunjdwp:transport=dt_socket,server=y,suspend=y,address=8000
 33: @REM MAVEN_SKIP_RC - flag to disable loading of mavenrc files
 34: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 35: 
 36: @REM Begin all REM lines with '@' in case MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO is 'on'
 37: @echo off
 38: @REM enable echoing my setting MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO to 'on'
 39: @if "%MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO%" == "on"  echo %MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO%
 40: 
 41: @REM set %HOME% to equivalent of $HOME
 42: if "%HOME%" == "" (set "HOME=%HOMEDRIVE%%HOMEPATH%")
 43: 
 44: @REM Execute a user defined script before this one
 45: if not "%MAVEN_SKIP_RC%" == "" goto skipRcPre
 46: @REM check for pre script, once with legacy .bat ending and once with .cmd ending
 47: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.bat" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.bat"
 48: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.cmd" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.cmd"
 49: :skipRcPre
 50: 
 51: @setlocal
 52: 
 53: set ERROR_CODE=0
 54: 
 55: @REM To isolate internal variables from possible post scripts, we use another setlocal
 56: @setlocal
 57: 
 58: @REM ==== START VALIDATION ====
 59: if not "%JAVA_HOME%" == "" goto OkJHome
 60: 
 61: echo.
 62: echo Error: JAVA_HOME not found in your environment. >&2
 63: echo Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the >&2
 64: echo location of your Java installation. >&2
 65: echo.
 66: goto error
 67: 
 68: :OkJHome
 69: if exist "%JAVA_HOME%\bin\java.exe" goto init
 70: 
 71: echo.
 72: echo Error: JAVA_HOME is set to an invalid directory. >&2
 73: echo JAVA_HOME = "%JAVA_HOME%" >&2
 74: echo Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the >&2
 75: echo location of your Java installation. >&2
 76: echo.
 77: goto error
 78: 
 79: @REM ==== END VALIDATION ====
 80: 
 81: :init
 82: 
 83: @REM Find the project base dir, i.e. the directory that contains the folder ".mvn".
 84: @REM Fallback to current working directory if not found.
 85: 
 86: set MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=%MAVEN_BASEDIR%
 87: IF NOT "%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%"=="" goto endDetectBaseDir
 88: 
 89: set EXEC_DIR=%CD%
 90: set WDIR=%EXEC_DIR%
 91: :findBaseDir
 92: IF EXIST "%WDIR%"\.mvn goto baseDirFound
 93: cd ..
 94: IF "%WDIR%"=="%CD%" goto baseDirNotFound
 95: set WDIR=%CD%
 96: goto findBaseDir
 97: 
 98: :baseDirFound
 99: set MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=%WDIR%
100: cd "%EXEC_DIR%"
101: goto endDetectBaseDir
102: 
103: :baseDirNotFound
104: set MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=%EXEC_DIR%
105: cd "%EXEC_DIR%"
106: 
107: :endDetectBaseDir
108: 
109: IF NOT EXIST "%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%\.mvn\jvm.config" goto endReadAdditionalConfig
110: 
111: @setlocal EnableExtensions EnableDelayedExpansion
112: for /F "usebackq delims=" %%a in ("%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%\.mvn\jvm.config") do set JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS=!JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS! %%a
113: @endlocal & set JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS=%JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS%
114: 
115: :endReadAdditionalConfig
116: 
117: SET MAVEN_JAVA_EXE="%JAVA_HOME%\bin\java.exe"
118: 
119: set WRAPPER_JAR="%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%\.mvn\wrapper\maven-wrapper.jar"
120: set WRAPPER_LAUNCHER=org.apache.maven.wrapper.MavenWrapperMain
121: 
122: %MAVEN_JAVA_EXE% %JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS% %MAVEN_OPTS% %MAVEN_DEBUG_OPTS% -classpath %WRAPPER_JAR% "-Dmaven.multiModuleProjectDirectory=%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%" %WRAPPER_LAUNCHER% %MAVEN_CONFIG% %*
123: if ERRORLEVEL 1 goto error
124: goto end
125: 
126: :error
127: set ERROR_CODE=1
128: 
129: :end
130: @endlocal & set ERROR_CODE=%ERROR_CODE%
131: 
132: if not "%MAVEN_SKIP_RC%" == "" goto skipRcPost
133: @REM check for post script, once with legacy .bat ending and once with .cmd ending
134: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.bat" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.bat"
135: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.cmd" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.cmd"
136: :skipRcPost
137: 
138: @REM pause the script if MAVEN_BATCH_PAUSE is set to 'on'
139: if "%MAVEN_BATCH_PAUSE%" == "on" pause
140: 
141: if "%MAVEN_TERMINATE_CMD%" == "on" exit %ERROR_CODE%
142: 
143: exit /B %ERROR_CODE%
